# List 21

## Unit 1

<table>
<thead><tr>
<th>单词</th>
<th>音标</th>
<th>常用</th>
<th>口语</th>
<th>词性</th>
<th>中文释义</th>
<th>常用短语</th>
</tr></thead>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>referee</b></td><td>/ˌrefəˈriː/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>名词、动词</td><td>仲裁者；裁判员</td><td>act as referee<br>担任仲裁人</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 A respected professor agreed to act as referee in the bitter dispute.  一位受人尊敬的教授同意在这场激烈争端中担任仲裁人。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 adjudicator</td><td>/əˈdʒudɪˌkeɪtər/</td><td>★</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>裁决者，评判员</td><td>an independent adjudicator<br>一位独立裁决人</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 An outside adjudicator was appointed to settle the contract dispute.  一名外部裁决人受聘解决这场合同纠纷。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 arbiter</td><td>/ˈɑːrbɪtər/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>名词</td><td>仲裁人；权威，裁定者</td><td>the final arbiter<br>最终裁定者</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The court remains the final arbiter of constitutional questions.  法院仍是宪法问题的最终裁定者。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 arbitrator</td><td>/ˈɑːrbɪˌtreɪtər/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>名词</td><td>仲裁人，公断人</td><td>appoint an arbitrator<br>指定一名仲裁人</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Both sides agreed to let an arbitrator decide the wage dispute.  双方同意由仲裁人裁决这起工资争端。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 judge</td><td>/ˈdʒʌdʒ/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>名词、动词</td><td>评判者，裁判；法官；判断</td><td>a panel of judges<br>一个评审团</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Three judges scored each performance without conferring with one another.  三名评委各自评分，互不商议。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 umpire</td><td>/ˈʌmˌpaɪər/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>名词、动词</td><td>裁判员；仲裁人</td><td>the umpire's decision<br>裁判的判决</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The umpire called the runner out despite loud protests.  尽管抗议声很大，裁判还是判跑垒员出局。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 adjudge</td><td>/əˈdʒʌdʒ/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>裁定，判决；宣判</td><td>adjudge him guilty<br>判定他有罪</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The tribunal adjudged the contract void from the start.  法庭裁定该合同自始无效。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 adjudicate</td><td>/əˈdʒuːdɪkeɪt/</td><td>★</td><td>☆</td><td>动词</td><td>裁决，判定</td><td>adjudicate a dispute<br>裁决一起纠纷</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 A retired judge was asked to adjudicate the long-running border dispute.  一位退休法官被请来裁决这场旷日持久的边界纠纷。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 arbitrate</td><td>/ˈɑːrbəˌtreɪt/</td><td>★</td><td>☆</td><td>动词</td><td>仲裁，公断</td><td>arbitrate between the parties<br>在双方之间仲裁</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 A retired judge was asked to arbitrate the family's property quarrel.  一位退休法官受邀仲裁这家人的财产纷争。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 decide</td><td>/dɪˈsaɪd/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>动词</td><td>裁决，判定；决定</td><td>decide the case<br>对案件作出裁决</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 It took the panel two days to decide who deserved the prize.  评审团花了两天才裁定谁该得奖。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 determine</td><td>/dəˈtɜːrmən/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>判定，确定；决心</td><td>determine the cause<br>判定原因</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Scientists could not determine the cause of the sudden outbreak.  科学家无法判定这次突发疫情的原因。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 settle</td><td>/ˈsetəl/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★★</td><td>动词</td><td>解决，平息；定居</td><td>settle the dispute<br>解决争端</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The two sides finally settled their dispute out of court.  双方最终在庭外解决了争端。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 consult</td><td>/kənˈsʌlt/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>动词</td><td>请教，咨询；商议</td><td>consult an expert<br>咨询一位专家</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Before signing anything you should consult a lawyer you trust.  签任何东西前你都该咨询一位信得过的律师。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>refine</b></td><td>/rɪˈfaɪn/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>提纯，精炼；改进</td><td>refine crude oil<br>精炼原油</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The plant refines crude oil into gasoline and other useful products.  这家工厂把原油提炼成汽油和其他有用的产品。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 ameliorate</td><td>/əˈmiːliəreɪt/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>改善，改进</td><td>ameliorate the situation<br>改善局面</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The new subsidy did little to ameliorate rural poverty.  新补贴对改善农村贫困几乎没有作用。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 amend</td><td>/əˈmend/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>修改，修订；改进</td><td>amend the constitution<br>修改宪法</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Parliament amended the law to close an obvious loophole.  议会修改了法律以堵住一个明显的漏洞。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 better</td><td>/ˈbetər/</td><td>★★★★★</td><td>★★★★★</td><td>动词、形容词</td><td>改善，改进；更好的</td><td>better his position<br>改善他的处境</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He took night classes to better his chances of promotion.  他上夜校以增加自己升职的机会。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 enhance</td><td>/ɪnˈhæns/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>提高，增强，提升</td><td>enhance the flavor<br>提升风味</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 A little salt greatly enhances the sweetness of the fruit.  少许盐能大大提升水果的甜味。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 enrich</td><td>/ɪnˈrɪtʃ/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>使丰富，增强</td><td>enrich the soil<br>使土壤肥沃</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Reading widely will enrich your understanding of other cultures.  广泛阅读会丰富你对其他文化的理解。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 help</td><td>/ˈhelp/</td><td>★★★★★</td><td>★★★★★</td><td>名词、动词</td><td>帮助，助益</td><td>a great help<br>很大的帮助</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Your advice was a great help during the difficult week.  在那困难的一周里你的建议帮了大忙。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳近 meliorate</del></td><td>/ˈmiliəˌreɪt/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>改善，使变好</td><td>meliorate living conditions<br>改善生活条件</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Simple drainage work meliorated conditions in the crowded district.  简单的排水工程改善了拥挤街区的状况。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 perfect</td><td>/ˈpɜːrfɪkt/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>形容词、及物动词</td><td>完美的；使完善</td><td>a perfect score<br>满分成绩</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He spent years trying to perfect his technique on the cello.  他花了数年时间钻研以完善自己的大提琴技巧。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 polish</td><td>/ˈpɑːlɪʃ/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>及物动词、名词</td><td>擦亮，磨光；润色</td><td>polish the wooden floor<br>把木地板擦亮</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She polished her essay carefully before sending it in.  她仔细润色文章后才交上去。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 upgrade</td><td>/ˌʌpˈɡreɪd/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>及物动词、名词</td><td>提升，升级</td><td>upgrade the equipment<br>升级设备</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The airline upgraded her to business class without any charge.  航空公司免费把她升级到商务舱。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 deteriorate</td><td>/dɪˈtɪriəreɪt/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>不及物动词</td><td>恶化，变坏</td><td>deteriorate rapidly<br>迅速恶化</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Her health deteriorated sharply during the cold winter.  在寒冷的冬天里她的健康急剧恶化。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 worsen</td><td>/ˈwɜːrsən/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>动词</td><td>使变坏，恶化</td><td>worsen over time<br>随时间恶化</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Her cough worsened after a week outdoors in the cold.  在寒冷的户外待了一周后，她的咳嗽加重了。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>reflect</b></td><td>/rɪˈflekt/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>及物动词、不及物动词</td><td>反映，显露；思考</td><td>reflect on the outcome<br>反思结果</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The rising sales figures reflect a real change in consumer confidence.  销售数字上升反映出消费者信心的真实变化。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 bespeak</td><td>/bɪˈspiːk/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>表明，显示；预示</td><td>bespeak great care<br>显示出极大用心</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The neat handwriting bespeaks a patience few students still possess.  整洁的字迹表明少有学生仍具备的耐心。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 betray</td><td>/bɪˈtreɪ/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>无意中显露，表明；背叛</td><td>betray his nervousness<br>显露他的紧张</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 His trembling hands betrayed the fear his calm voice concealed.  他颤抖的双手泄露了平静语调所掩藏的恐惧。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 demonstrate</td><td>/ˈdemənstreɪt/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>（通过证据）证明，表明</td><td>demonstrate the theory clearly<br>清楚地证明该理论</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The experiment demonstrated the theory clearly to every skeptical observer.  实验向每位持疑的观察者清楚证明了该理论。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 evince</td><td>/ɪˈvɪns/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>表明</td><td>evince little interest<br>表现出兴趣不大</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 His flat answers evinced very little interest in the job.  他平淡的回答表明他对这份工作兴趣不大。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 manifest</td><td>/ˈmænɪfest/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>形容词、动词</td><td>显然的，明显易懂的；显现，显露</td><td>manifest injustice<br>明显的不公</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 His discomfort at the question was manifest to everyone seated around the table.  他对这个问题的不适让围坐桌边的每个人都看得明明白白。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 reveal</td><td>/rɪˈviːl/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>揭示，透露</td><td>reveal the details<br>透露细节</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The company refused to reveal the details of the settlement.  公司拒绝透露和解的细节。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 show</td><td>/ˈʃoʊ/</td><td>★★★★★</td><td>★★★★★</td><td>及物动词、名词</td><td>显示，展现；展览</td><td>show your feelings<br>表露感情</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He rarely shows his feelings, even to close friends.  他很少表露感情，连对好友也如此。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 consider</td><td>/kənˈsɪdər/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>动词</td><td>仔细考虑，斟酌</td><td>consider the options carefully<br>仔细斟酌各种选择</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She considered the options carefully before signing the lease.  她在签租约前仔细斟酌了各种选择。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳近 cogitate</del></td><td>/ˈkɑːdʒɪˌteɪt/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>动词</td><td>认真思考，仔细考虑</td><td>cogitate on the problem<br>仔细思考这个问题</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He sat by the window and cogitated on his next move.  他坐在窗边，仔细思量下一步该怎么走。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 contemplate</td><td>/ˈkɑːntəmpleɪt/</td><td>★★</td><td>☆</td><td>动词</td><td>沉思，仔细思索</td><td>contemplate the consequences<br>仔细思索后果</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She sat by the window and contemplated the few choices left to her.  她坐在窗边，仔细思索留给自己的几个选择。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 deliberate</td><td>/dɪˈlɪbərət/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>深思熟虑的，故意的</td><td>a deliberate attempt<br>故意的尝试</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The delay was deliberate, designed to weaken our position.  这次延误是故意的，目的是削弱我们的立场。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 meditate</td><td>/ˈmedɪteɪt/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>动词</td><td>思索，沉思</td><td>meditate on the problem<br>沉思这个问题</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He sat by the window and meditated on the day's events.  他坐在窗边沉思白天发生的事。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 muse</td><td>/ˈmjuːz/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>动词、名词</td><td>沉思，冥想</td><td>muse on the past<br>追忆往事</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She sat by the fire and mused about the choices she had made.  她坐在火边，沉思自己做过的选择。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 ponder</td><td>/ˈpɑːndər/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>动词</td><td>仔细考虑，深思</td><td>ponder the question<br>细想这个问题</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He pondered the offer for a week before finally accepting it.  他把这个提议考虑了一周才最终接受。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 ruminate</td><td>/ˈruːmɪneɪt/</td><td>★</td><td>☆</td><td>动词</td><td>沉思；反复琢磨；反刍</td><td>ruminate on the past<br>反复思量往事</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He sat by the window and ruminated on the past.  他坐在窗边，反复回想往事。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 think</td><td>/ˈθɪŋk/</td><td>★★★★★</td><td>★★★★★</td><td>动词</td><td>思考，认为</td><td>think it over<br>仔细想一想</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Give me a day to think carefully about what you proposed.  给我一天时间仔细想想你的提议。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 conceal</td><td>/kənˈsiːl/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>动词</td><td>隐藏，隐瞒</td><td>conceal the truth<br>隐瞒真相</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The witness tried to conceal the truth from the police.  证人试图向警方隐瞒真相。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 cover</td><td>/ˈkʌvər/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>及物动词、名词</td><td>掩盖，遮盖；覆盖物</td><td>cover up the truth<br>掩盖真相</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 They tried to cover up the truth with a vague statement.  他们试图用含糊的声明掩盖真相。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 hide</td><td>/ˈhaɪd/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>动词、名词</td><td>隐藏，隐瞒</td><td>hide the evidence<br>藏匿证据</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She hid the letters in a drawer beneath her sweaters.  她把信藏在毛衣下面的抽屉里。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 mask</td><td>/ˈmæsk/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>及物动词、名词</td><td>掩盖，掩饰；面具</td><td>mask his anger<br>掩饰他的怒气</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Loud music masked the sound of the approaching helicopter.  响亮的音乐掩盖了直升机靠近的声音。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 obscure</td><td>/əbˈskjʊr/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>含义模糊的；平凡的，不知名的</td><td>an obscure poet<br>不知名的诗人</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He remained an obscure poet until a critic rediscovered his work decades later.  他一直是个不知名的诗人，直到几十年后一位批评家重新发现他的作品。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 occlude</td><td>/əˈklud/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>阻塞，封闭</td><td>occlude an artery<br>阻塞一条动脉</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Thick clouds occluded the sun for the entire afternoon.  厚云整个下午都遮住了太阳。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 veil</td><td>/ˈveɪl/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>及物动词、名词</td><td>遮掩，掩饰；面纱</td><td>veil his true motives<br>掩饰真实动机</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Polite words could not veil his true motives for long.  客气话没能长久掩饰他的真实动机。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>refractory</b></td><td>/rɪˈfræktəri/</td><td>★☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>倔强的，不顺从的</td><td>a refractory pupil<br>不顺从的学生</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 One refractory pupil disrupted the lesson for the entire class.  一个不顺从的学生扰乱了全班的课。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 balky</td><td>/ˈbɔːlki/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>执拗的，不肯前进的</td><td>a balky engine<br>不听使唤的引擎</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The balky engine refused to start on the coldest morning.  在最冷的那个早晨，这台不听使唤的引擎怎么也发动不起来。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳近 contumacious</del></td><td>/ˌkɑːntuˈmeɪʃəs/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>不服从的，倔强的</td><td>a contumacious witness<br>不服从的证人</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The contumacious witness was fined for defying the judge.  那名抗命的证人因违抗法官而被罚款。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 defiant</td><td>/dɪˈfaɪənt/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>公然违抗的，挑战的</td><td>a defiant attitude<br>挑战的态度</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The defiant student refused to apologize to his teacher.  这个公然抗拒的学生拒绝向老师道歉。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳近 incompliant</del></td><td>/ˌɪnkəmˈplaɪənt/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>不顺从的，不依从的</td><td>an incompliant witness<br>不肯配合的证人</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The incompliant staff refused to follow the new dress rule.  不顺从的员工拒绝遵守新的着装规定。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 insubordinate</td><td>/ɪnsəˈbɔːrdənət/</td><td>★</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>不服从的，抗命的</td><td>insubordinate behavior<br>抗命的行为</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The officer was discharged for insubordinate conduct at sea.  这名军官因在海上抗命行为被开除。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 intractable</td><td>/ˌɪnˈtræktəbəl/</td><td>★☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>难对付的，棘手的</td><td>an intractable problem<br>棘手的问题</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Poverty remains an intractable problem in many rural regions.  贫困在许多农村地区仍是棘手的问题。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 obstreperous</td><td>/əbˈstrepərəs/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>不听话的，任性的</td><td>an obstreperous child<br>不听话的孩子</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 An obstreperous child kept kicking the seat in front of him.  一个吵闹不驯的孩子一直踢着前排的座椅。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 rebellious</td><td>/rɪˈbeljəs/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>反叛的，难管束的</td><td>a rebellious teenager<br>叛逆的少年</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Her rebellious streak showed early, long before high school.  她的叛逆性格早早就显露，远在高中之前。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 unruly</td><td>/ʌnˈruːli/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>难驾驭的，不守规矩的</td><td>an unruly crowd<br>不守规矩的人群</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Two teachers struggled to calm the unruly class.  两位老师费力地安抚那个不守规矩的班级。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 amenable</td><td>/əˈmiːnəbl/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>顺从的，服从的</td><td>amenable to suggestion<br>愿意接受建议</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The new manager is amenable to any reasonable suggestion.  这位新经理对任何合理的建议都乐于接受。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 compliant</td><td>/kəmˈplaɪənt/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>顺从的</td><td>a compliant employee<br>顺从的员工</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The children were unusually compliant during the long ceremony.  在漫长的仪式上，孩子们出奇地听话。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 conformable</td><td>/kənˈfɔrməbəl/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>顺从的；一致的</td><td>conformable to the rules<br>符合规定的</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The design must be conformable to the city's new safety code.  设计必须符合城市新的安全规范。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 docile</td><td>/ˈdɑːsəl/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>温顺的，听话的</td><td>a docile animal<br>温顺的动物</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The old horse was docile enough for beginners to ride.  这匹老马很温顺，初学者也能骑。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳反 governable</del></td><td>/ˈɡʌvərnəbəl/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>可管理的，易统治的</td><td>a governable class<br>一个好管的班级</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 With patience the once wild horse became perfectly governable.  在耐心训练下，这匹曾野性难驯的马变得十分听话。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 obedient</td><td>/əˈbiːdiənt/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>服从的，顺从的</td><td>an obedient child<br>听话的孩子</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The dog sat quietly, obedient to every hand signal.  那只狗安静地坐着，服从每个手势。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳反 ruly</del></td><td>/ˈruli/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>守规矩的，服管的</td><td>a ruly classroom<br>守规矩的教室</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The new teacher was surprised to find such a ruly classroom.  新老师惊讶地发现这个教室如此守规矩。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 submissive</td><td>/səbˈmɪsɪv/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>服从的，顺从的</td><td>a submissive attitude<br>顺从的态度</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She spoke in a submissive tone that surprised her colleagues.  她以顺从的语气说话，令同事惊讶。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 tractable</td><td>/ˈtræktəbl/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>形容词</td><td>易驾驭的，温顺的</td><td>a tractable student<br>听话的学生</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The puppy proved surprisingly tractable during the first weeks of training.  这只小狗在最初几周的训练中出乎意料地听话。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del><b>refulgent</b></del></td><td>/rɪˈfʌldʒənt/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>辉煌的，灿烂的</td><td>refulgent golden domes<br>灿烂的金色圆顶</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Refulgent golden domes rose above the rooftops of the old city.  灿烂的金色圆顶耸立在老城的屋顶之上。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 beaming</td><td>/ˈbiːmɪŋ/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>光芒四射的；笑容满面的</td><td>a beaming smile<br>灿烂的笑容</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She greeted us with a beaming smile at the airport gate.  她在机场门口用灿烂的笑容迎接我们。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 brilliant</td><td>/ˈbrɪljənt/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>聪颖的；辉煌的</td><td>a brilliant student<br>才华出众的学生</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She was a brilliant mathematician long before she turned twenty.  她还没到二十岁就已是出色的数学家。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 dazzling</td><td>/ˈdæzlɪŋ/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>炫目的，耀眼的</td><td>a dazzling display<br>炫目的表演</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The dancers wore dazzling costumes that glittered under the stage lights.  舞者们穿着在舞台灯光下闪耀的炫目服装。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 incandescent</td><td>/ˌɪnkænˈdesənt/</td><td>★★</td><td>★</td><td>形容词</td><td>白热的，耀眼的</td><td>an incandescent bulb<br>白炽灯泡</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The incandescent filament burned out after only a few weeks.  白炽灯丝仅用几周就烧断了。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 lucid</td><td>/ˈluːsɪd/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>表达清晰的，易懂的</td><td>a lucid explanation<br>清晰的解释</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The professor offered a lucid summary of a difficult argument.  教授对一个艰深论证做了清晰的总结。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 luminous</td><td>/ˈluːmɪnəs/</td><td>★★</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>发光的，明亮的</td><td>a luminous dial<br>夜光表盘</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The luminous dial of his watch glowed faintly in the dark.  他手表的夜光表盘在黑暗中微微发亮。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 lustrous</td><td>/ˈlʌstrəs/</td><td>★☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>有光泽的</td><td>lustrous black hair<br>有光泽的黑发</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Her lustrous black hair shone under the bright lights of the stage.  她有光泽的黑发在明亮的舞台灯光下闪耀。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 radiant</td><td>/ˈreɪdiənt/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>光芒四射的；喜气洋洋的</td><td>a radiant smile<br>灿烂的笑容</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The bride looked radiant as she walked into the garden.  新娘走进花园时神采飞扬。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 resplendent</td><td>/rɪˈsplendənt/</td><td>★☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>华丽辉煌的</td><td>resplendent in uniform<br>身着华丽制服</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The bride appeared resplendent in a gown embroidered with hundreds of tiny pearls.  新娘身着绣满数百颗小珍珠的礼服，光彩夺目。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 splendid</td><td>/ˈsplendɪd/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>壮丽的；极好的</td><td>a splendid marble hall<br>壮丽的大理石厅堂</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 From the roof we had a splendid view of the harbor.  从屋顶望去，港口的景色极为壮丽。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 dim</td><td>/ˈdɪm/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>形容词、动词</td><td>暗淡的，模糊的</td><td>a dim light<br>昏暗的灯光</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The room was too dim for reading anything but large print.  房间太暗，只能看大字。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 dull</td><td>/dʌl/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>迟钝的；乏味的</td><td>a dull student<br>迟钝的学生</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The lecture was so dull that half the class slept.  讲座乏味得让一半学生睡着了。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 lackluster</td><td>/ˈlæklʌstər/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>黯淡无光泽的</td><td>a lackluster performance<br>平淡无光的表现</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Her lackluster hair and tired face worried everyone at home.  她黯淡的头发和疲惫的脸让家人都担心。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>refute</b></td><td>/rɪˈfjuːt/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>驳倒，反驳</td><td>refute the claim<br>驳斥这一说法</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The scientist used new data to refute the claim about rising temperatures.  这位科学家用新数据反驳了关于气温上升的说法。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 contradict</td><td>/ˌkɑːntrəˈdɪkt/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>否认，反驳；与…相矛盾</td><td>contradict the evidence<br>与证据相矛盾</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The witness's second statement flatly contradicted everything she had said earlier.  证人的第二份陈述与她此前所说完全矛盾。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳近 disaffirm</del></td><td>/ˌdɪsəˈfɜrm/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>否认，否定；废除</td><td>disaffirm the contract<br>否认合同效力</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The court may disaffirm a contract signed by a minor.  法院可以否定未成年人签署的合同。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 disavow</td><td>/ˌdɪsəˈvaʊ/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>拒绝承认，否认</td><td>disavow any knowledge<br>否认知情</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The senator moved quickly to disavow any knowledge of the secret payments.  参议员迅速表态，否认知晓那些秘密付款。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 disclaim</td><td>/dɪsˈkleɪm/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>否认，拒绝承认</td><td>disclaim responsibility<br>否认责任</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The company quickly disclaimed any link to the failed venture.  公司迅速否认与那个失败项目有任何关联。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳近 disconfirm</del></td><td>/ˌdɪskənˈfɜrm/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>证明为假，否证</td><td>disconfirm the hypothesis<br>否证该假设</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 New data disconfirmed the theory proposed a decade earlier.  新数据否证了十年前提出的理论。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 deny</td><td>/dɪˈnaɪ/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>否认；拒绝给予</td><td>flatly deny the charge<br>断然否认指控</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 I do not deny that the plan carries some very real risks.  我不否认这个计划确实带有一些实实在在的风险。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 gainsay</td><td>/ˌɡeɪnˈseɪ/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>否认</td><td>gainsay the evidence<br>否认证据</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 No one could gainsay the evidence the inspector had collected.  没有人能否认检查员收集到的证据。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 negate</td><td>/nɪˈɡeɪt/</td><td>★★</td><td>☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>否认；否定，取消</td><td>negate the effect<br>抵消效果</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 One careless error can negate weeks of careful work.  一个粗心的错误能抹掉数周的细致工作。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 negative</td><td>/ˈneɡətɪv/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>动词、形容词</td><td>否决，反驳；负面的</td><td>negative the proposal<br>否决这项提案</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The committee negatived the motion after a short debate.  委员会经短暂辩论后否决了该动议。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 reject</td><td>/rɪˈdʒekt/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>及物动词、名词</td><td>拒绝，驳回；废品</td><td>reject the proposal<br>否决提案</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The committee voted to reject the proposal without further discussion.  委员会投票否决了该提案，不再讨论。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 repudiate</td><td>/rɪˈpjuːdieɪt/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>否认，拒绝接受</td><td>repudiate a claim<br>否认一项主张</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The author repudiated the views quoted in the article.  作者否认了文章中引用的观点。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 acknowledge</td><td>/ækˈnɑːlɪdʒ/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>承认；对…表示感谢</td><td>acknowledge the mistake<br>承认错误</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The mayor finally acknowledged that the report contained serious errors.  市长最终承认那份报告有严重错误。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 admit</td><td>/ədˈmɪt/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>承认；准许进入</td><td>admit the mistake<br>承认错误</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She finally admitted that she had made a serious mistake.  她终于承认自己犯了一个严重的错误。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 avow</td><td>/əˈvaʊ/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>公开承认，坦率声明</td><td>avow one's beliefs openly<br>公开表明信念</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He avowed his support for the strike in front of reporters.  他在记者面前公开表明支持这次罢工。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 concede</td><td>/kənˈsiːd/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>承认</td><td>concede the point<br>承认这一点</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She finally conceded that her opponent had made a fair point.  她最终承认对手说得有道理。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 confirm</td><td>/kənˈfɜːrm/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>证实，确认，进一步确定</td><td>confirm the findings<br>证实这些发现</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Later tests confirmed what the first team had suspected.  后来的检测证实了第一个团队的猜测。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>regenerate</b></td><td>/rɪˈdʒenəreɪt/</td><td>★★</td><td>☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>使重获新生，使焕然一新</td><td>regenerate a neighborhood<br>使一个街区焕然一新</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The city spent millions to regenerate a neighborhood near the old port.  该市花费数百万使旧港附近的一个街区焕然一新。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 recharge</td><td>/riˈtʃɑːrdʒ/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>动词</td><td>充电；恢复精力</td><td>recharge his batteries<br>恢复精力</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 A quiet weekend in the mountains recharged him completely.  在山里过了个安静的周末，他完全恢复了精力。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 refresh</td><td>/rɪˈfreʃ/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>使恢复精力，使清爽</td><td>refresh the weary travelers<br>使疲惫的旅人恢复精神</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 A cold shower refreshed him after the long hike.  长途徒步后，一个冷水澡让他恢复了精神。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 rejuvenate</td><td>/rɪˈdʒuːvəˌneɪt/</td><td>★☆</td><td>☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>使显得年轻，使恢复活力</td><td>rejuvenate the old district<br>使老城区恢复活力</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Two weeks by the sea seemed to rejuvenate my grandmother.  在海边待了两周，我外婆好像年轻了许多。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 rekindle</td><td>/riˈkɪndəl/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>重新点燃，重新激起</td><td>rekindle interest<br>重新激起兴趣</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The documentary rekindled public interest in deep-sea exploration.  这部纪录片重新激起了公众对深海探索的兴趣。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 renew</td><td>/rɪˈnuː/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>补充更新，续期</td><td>renew the water supply<br>补充水源</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Spring rains renewed the reservoir after a long dry summer.  春雨在漫长干旱的夏季后补满了水库。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 restore</td><td>/rɪˈstɔːr/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>恢复，修复；归还</td><td>restore his health<br>恢复他的健康</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Careful craftsmen restored the painting to its original brilliance.  技艺精细的匠人把这幅画修复到原来的光彩。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 resurrect</td><td>/ˌrezəˈrekt/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>使重生，使复苏</td><td>resurrect an old idea<br>重提一个旧想法</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The new director hopes to resurrect a play that failed thirty years ago.  新导演希望让三十年前失败的一部戏重获生机。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 resuscitate</td><td>/rɪˈsʌsɪteɪt/</td><td>★☆</td><td>☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>使复活，使苏醒</td><td>resuscitate a drowning victim<br>抢救一名溺水者</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Paramedics worked for twenty minutes to resuscitate the man pulled from the lake.  急救人员花了二十分钟抢救从湖里救上来的男子。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 revitalize</td><td>/riˈvaɪtəˌlaɪz/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>使恢复生机，使复兴</td><td>revitalize the local economy<br>振兴当地经济</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The new library helped revitalize a neighborhood everyone had abandoned.  新图书馆帮助振兴了一个人人都已放弃的街区。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳近 revivify</del></td><td>/riˈvɪvɪˌfaɪ/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>使复活，使重新振作</td><td>revivify a dying tradition<br>使一项衰落的传统复兴</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Fresh funding revivified a research program left dormant for years.  新的资金使一个沉寂多年的研究项目重获生机。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 degenerate</td><td>/dɪˈdʒenəreɪt/</td><td>★★</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词、不及物动词</td><td>衰退的，堕落的；恶化</td><td>degenerate into chaos<br>恶化为混乱</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The peaceful protest began to degenerate into shouting and shoving.  和平抗议开始恶化为叫骂和推挤。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 deteriorate</td><td>/dɪˈtɪriəreɪt/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>不及物动词</td><td>恶化，变坏</td><td>deteriorate rapidly<br>迅速恶化</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Her health deteriorated sharply during the cold winter.  在寒冷的冬天里她的健康急剧恶化。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 worsen</td><td>/ˈwɜːrsən/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>动词</td><td>使变坏，恶化</td><td>worsen over time<br>随时间恶化</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Her cough worsened after a week outdoors in the cold.  在寒冷的户外待了一周后，她的咳嗽加重了。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>regimen</b></td><td>/ˈredʒɪmən/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>名词</td><td>（政治上的）统治</td><td>under a harsh regimen<br>在严酷统治之下</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The colony suffered for decades under a harsh military regimen.  这块殖民地在严酷的军事统治下受苦数十年。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 administration</td><td>/ædˌmɪnɪsˈtreɪʃən/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★☆</td><td>名词</td><td>管理，行政；政府</td><td>the current administration<br>现任政府</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The new administration promised to reform the tax system.  新政府承诺改革税制。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 authority</td><td>/əˈθɔːrəti/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★☆</td><td>名词</td><td>（某领域的）权威人士；管辖权，控制</td><td>an authority on ancient coins<br>古钱币方面的权威</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She is a recognized authority on ancient Greek coins.  她是古希腊钱币方面公认的权威人士。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 governance</td><td>/ˈɡʌvərnəns/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★☆</td><td>名词</td><td>治理，管理，统治</td><td>good corporate governance<br>良好的公司治理</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Weak governance allowed corruption to spread through the ministry.  治理薄弱使腐败在该部门蔓延。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 jurisdiction</td><td>/ˌdʒʊrəsˈdɪkʃən/</td><td>★★★</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>管辖权，管辖范围</td><td>outside our jurisdiction<br>在我们管辖范围之外</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The case falls under federal rather than state jurisdiction.  该案属于联邦而非州的管辖范围。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 regime</td><td>/rəˈʒiːm/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>名词</td><td>政权，政体；制度</td><td>a military regime<br>军事政权</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The regime collapsed within weeks of the general strike.  总罢工数周后该政权便崩溃了。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>regress</b></td><td>/rɪˈɡres/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>不及物动词</td><td>退化，恶化</td><td>regress to childish behavior<br>退回到幼稚行为</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Under stress he tends to regress to childish and sulky behavior.  在压力下他往往退化为幼稚而怨怼的行为。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 atrophy</td><td>/ˈætrəfi/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>不及物动词</td><td>萎缩，衰退</td><td>muscles atrophy quickly<br>肌肉迅速萎缩</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Muscles atrophy quickly when a patient stays in bed for weeks.  病人卧床数周后，肌肉会迅速萎缩。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 crumble</td><td>/ˈkrʌmbəl/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>动词</td><td>崩塌，瓦解；碎裂</td><td>crumble into ruin<br>崩塌成废墟</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Their alliance crumbled as soon as the money ran out.  钱一花完，他们的联盟就瓦解了。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 decay</td><td>/dɪˈkeɪ/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>不及物动词、名词</td><td>衰败，腐烂</td><td>fall into slow decay<br>逐渐衰败</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The abandoned mill has been left to decay for decades.  这座废弃磨坊已任其衰败数十年。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 decline</td><td>/dɪˈklaɪn/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>动词、名词</td><td>拒绝；下降，衰退</td><td>decline an invitation<br>谢绝邀请</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She politely declined the invitation to speak at the ceremony.  她礼貌地谢绝了在典礼上发言的邀请。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 degenerate</td><td>/dɪˈdʒenəreɪt/</td><td>★★</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词、不及物动词</td><td>衰退的，堕落的；恶化</td><td>degenerate into chaos<br>恶化为混乱</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The peaceful protest began to degenerate into shouting and shoving.  和平抗议开始恶化为叫骂和推挤。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 descend</td><td>/dɪˈsend/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>动词</td><td>下降；世代相传</td><td>descend from the summit<br>从山顶下来</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The path descends steeply toward the river through thick pine forest.  小径穿过浓密的松林，向河边陡然下降。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 deteriorate</td><td>/dɪˈtɪriəreɪt/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>不及物动词</td><td>恶化，变坏</td><td>deteriorate rapidly<br>迅速恶化</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Her health deteriorated sharply during the cold winter.  在寒冷的冬天里她的健康急剧恶化。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 ebb</td><td>/ˈeb/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>动词、名词</td><td>衰退，减弱；退潮</td><td>his strength ebbed away<br>他的体力渐渐衰退</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Public enthusiasm for the project ebbed over the winter.  公众对这个项目的热情在冬天渐渐消退。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 retrograde</td><td>/ˈretrəɡreɪd/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>倒退的</td><td>a retrograde step<br>一次倒退之举</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Banning the new textbooks feels like a retrograde step for the whole district.  禁用新教材对整个学区而言像是一次倒退。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 rot</td><td>/ˈrɑːt/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>动词、名词</td><td>腐烂，腐坏</td><td>rot in the damp<br>在潮湿中腐烂</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The wooden posts had rotted through at the base.  木桩底部已经烂穿了。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 sink</td><td>/ˈsɪŋk/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>动词、名词</td><td>下沉，下降；水槽</td><td>sink below the horizon<br>沉到地平线下</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The old boat began to sink soon after the storm.  暴风雨过后不久那条旧船开始下沉。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 worsen</td><td>/ˈwɜːrsən/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>动词</td><td>使变坏，恶化</td><td>worsen over time<br>随时间恶化</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Her cough worsened after a week outdoors in the cold.  在寒冷的户外待了一周后，她的咳嗽加重了。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 ameliorate</td><td>/əˈmiːliəreɪt/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>改善，改进</td><td>ameliorate the situation<br>改善局面</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The new subsidy did little to ameliorate rural poverty.  新补贴对改善农村贫困几乎没有作用。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 improve</td><td>/ɪmˈpruːv/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★★★★</td><td>动词</td><td>改善，提高</td><td>improve the quality of<br>提高…的质量</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Regular practice quickly improved her pronunciation and her confidence.  经常练习很快改善了她的发音和自信。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳反 meliorate</del></td><td>/ˈmiliəˌreɪt/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>改善，使变好</td><td>meliorate living conditions<br>改善生活条件</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Simple drainage work meliorated conditions in the crowded district.  简单的排水工程改善了拥挤街区的状况。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>rehabilitate</b></td><td>/ˌriːhəˈbɪlɪteɪt/</td><td>★★</td><td>☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>使复原，使康复</td><td>rehabilitate former inmates<br>帮助刑满释放者重返社会</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The program aims to rehabilitate former inmates and find them jobs.  该项目旨在帮刑满释放者重返社会并找到工作。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 cure</td><td>/ˈkjʊr/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>动词、名词</td><td>治愈，治疗；疗法</td><td>cure the infection<br>治愈感染</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Antibiotics cured the infection within a single week.  抗生素在一周内治愈了感染。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 fix</td><td>/ˈfɪks/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>及物动词、名词</td><td>修理，解决；固定</td><td>fix the leaking pipe<br>修好漏水的管子</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 A neighbor fixed the leaking pipe before the floor was ruined.  邻居在地板毁坏前修好了漏水的管子。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 mend</td><td>/ˈmend/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>动词</td><td>纠正，改正；修补</td><td>mend his ways<br>改正他的行为</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He promised to mend his ways after the second stern warning.  第二次严厉警告后他承诺改正。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 rejuvenate</td><td>/rɪˈdʒuːvəˌneɪt/</td><td>★☆</td><td>☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>使显得年轻，使恢复活力</td><td>rejuvenate the old district<br>使老城区恢复活力</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Two weeks by the sea seemed to rejuvenate my grandmother.  在海边待了两周，我外婆好像年轻了许多。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 resuscitate</td><td>/rɪˈsʌsɪteɪt/</td><td>★☆</td><td>☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>使复活，使苏醒</td><td>resuscitate a drowning victim<br>抢救一名溺水者</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Paramedics worked for twenty minutes to resuscitate the man pulled from the lake.  急救人员花了二十分钟抢救从湖里救上来的男子。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 revitalize</td><td>/riˈvaɪtəˌlaɪz/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>使恢复生机，使复兴</td><td>revitalize the local economy<br>振兴当地经济</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The new library helped revitalize a neighborhood everyone had abandoned.  新图书馆帮助振兴了一个人人都已放弃的街区。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 revive</td><td>/rɪˈvaɪv/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>不及物动词</td><td>再获新生</td><td>revive after the crisis<br>危机后重获生机</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Local trade began to revive once the new highway opened last spring.  去年春天新高速开通后，当地贸易开始重获生机。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 set up</td><td>/sɛt ˈʌp/</td><td>★★★★☆</td><td>★★★★</td><td>动词</td><td>建立，创办；安装，搭建</td><td>set up a company<br>创办一家公司</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 They set up a small company in the garage two years ago.  两年前他们在车库里创办了一家小公司。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 debilitate</td><td>/dɪˈbɪlɪteɪt/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>使衰弱</td><td>debilitate the immune system<br>使免疫系统衰弱</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The virus gradually debilitates the immune system of healthy adults.  这种病毒会逐渐削弱健康成年人的免疫系统。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳反 enervate</del></td><td>/ˈenərveɪt/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>使衰弱</td><td>enervate the whole team<br>使全队疲弱无力</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The long heat wave enervated the whole team before the final match.  漫长的热浪在决赛前使全队疲弱无力。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳反 enfeeble</del></td><td>/ɪnˈfiːbəl/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>使衰弱，使虚弱</td><td>enfeeble the patient<br>使病人虚弱</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 A long fever enfeebled him so much that walking hurt.  长期发烧使他虚弱到走路都疼。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 weaken</td><td>/ˈwiːkən/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>动词</td><td>削弱，使变弱，变衰弱</td><td>weaken the structure<br>使结构变弱</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Repeated flooding weakened the foundations of the stone bridge.  反复的洪水削弱了石桥的地基。</td></tr>
</tbody>
</table>

## Unit 2

<table>
<thead><tr>
<th>单词</th>
<th>音标</th>
<th>常用</th>
<th>口语</th>
<th>词性</th>
<th>中文释义</th>
<th>常用短语</th>
</tr></thead>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>rehearsal</b></td><td>/rɪˈhɜːrsl/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>名词</td><td>排练，彩排</td><td>a dress rehearsal<br>一次带装彩排</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The cast held a dress rehearsal the night before opening.  剧组在首演前一晚进行了带装彩排。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 practice</td><td>/ˈpræktəs/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>名词、动词</td><td>练习，排练；惯例</td><td>hours of practice<br>数小时的练习</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The choir needs one more practice before Sunday's concert.  合唱团在周日音乐会前还需要再排练一次。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 trial</td><td>/ˈtraɪəl/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>名词</td><td>试验；审判</td><td>a clinical trial<br>一项临床试验</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The drug performed well in a small clinical trial.  该药在一项小型临床试验中表现良好。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 dry run</td><td>/draɪ ˈrʌn/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★</td><td>名词</td><td>演练，试运行</td><td>do a dry run<br>做一次演练</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 We did a dry run of the presentation the night before.  我们前一晚把演示预演了一遍。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳反 extemporization</del></td><td>/ɪkˌstempərəˈzeɪʃən/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>即兴创作，即席发挥</td><td>brilliant extemporization<br>精彩的即兴发挥</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The lecture was pure extemporization, yet perfectly organized.  这场讲座纯属即兴发挥，却条理分明。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 impromptu</td><td>/ɪmˈprɑːmptuː/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>名词、形容词</td><td>即席的表演；即席的，即兴的</td><td>an impromptu performance<br>即兴表演</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The band gave an impromptu concert in the town square.  乐队在镇广场上举行了一场即兴音乐会。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 improvisation</td><td>/ˌɪmprɑvɪˈzeɪʃən/</td><td>★★</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>即兴创作，即席之作</td><td>jazz improvisation<br>爵士即兴演奏</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 His solo was a brilliant improvisation on an old melody.  他的独奏是对一段古老旋律的精彩即兴演绎。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>reign</b></td><td>/reɪn/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>名词</td><td>统治权</td><td>during her reign<br>在她统治期间</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Trade with distant ports flourished during her long and peaceful reign.  在她漫长而平静的统治期间，与远方港口的贸易十分繁荣。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 arm</td><td>/ˈɑːrm/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>名词、动词</td><td>权力，力量；武装；手臂</td><td>the arm of the law<br>法律的强制力</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 No one escapes the long arm of the tax authorities.  没人能躲过税务机关的长臂。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 authority</td><td>/əˈθɔːrəti/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★☆</td><td>名词</td><td>（某领域的）权威人士；管辖权，控制</td><td>an authority on ancient coins<br>古钱币方面的权威</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She is a recognized authority on ancient Greek coins.  她是古希腊钱币方面公认的权威人士。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 clutch</td><td>/ˈklʌtʃ/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>名词、动词</td><td>一群，一窝；抓紧</td><td>a clutch of eggs<br>一窝蛋</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 A clutch of reporters waited at the airport exit.  一群记者在机场出口等候。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 command</td><td>/kəˈmænd/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>名词、动词</td><td>指挥权，控制；命令</td><td>take command of<br>接管…的指挥权</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She took command of the regiment after the colonel fell ill.  上校病倒后，她接管了该团的指挥权。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 control</td><td>/kənˈtroʊl/</td><td>★★★★☆</td><td>★★★★☆</td><td>名词、动词</td><td>控制权，支配；控制</td><td>seize control of<br>夺取…的控制权</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Rebels seized control of the capital within a single night.  叛军一夜之间就夺取了首都的控制权。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 dominion</td><td>/dəˈmɪnjən/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>名词</td><td>统治权；领地</td><td>hold dominion over land<br>统治土地</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The king held dominion over every valley in the north.  国王统治着北方的每一个山谷。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 grip</td><td>/ˈɡrɪp/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>及物动词、名词</td><td>吸引住；紧握</td><td>grip the reader's attention<br>抓住读者注意力</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The film gripped us from the very first scene.  这部电影从第一个镜头就抓住了我们。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 hold</td><td>/ˈhoʊld/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>怀有，心存</td><td>hold a grudge<br>心怀怨恨</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He still holds a grudge against his former business partner.  他仍然对昔日的生意伙伴心怀怨恨。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 mastery</td><td>/ˈmæstəri/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>名词</td><td>技艺超群，精通</td><td>mastery of the violin<br>对小提琴的精通</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Her mastery of the violin was obvious from the very first bar she played.  她对小提琴的精通从她演奏的第一个小节就显露无疑。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 sway</td><td>/ˈsweɪ/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>名词、动词</td><td>支配，统治；摇摆</td><td>hold sway over<br>支配，统治…</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 For decades one family held sway over the entire province.  几十年间，一个家族统治着整个省份。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 dominate</td><td>/ˈdɑːməˌneɪt/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★★</td><td>动词</td><td>支配，统治；俯视</td><td>dominate the market<br>支配市场</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Two firms dominate the industry and set nearly every price.  两家公司支配着这个行业，几乎决定所有价格。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳近 domineer</del></td><td>/ˌdɑːməˈnɪr/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>不及物动词</td><td>专横跋扈，作威作福</td><td>domineer over his staff<br>专横地对待下属</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He liked to domineer over his staff instead of listening to their advice.  他喜欢专横地对待下属，而不是听取他们的建议。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 prevail</td><td>/prɪˈveɪl/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>不及物动词</td><td>盛行，占优势；获胜</td><td>prevail over rivals<br>战胜对手</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Common sense finally prevailed after hours of pointless argument.  在几小时无谓的争论之后，常识终于占了上风。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 predominate</td><td>/prɪˈdɑːməˌneɪt/</td><td>★☆</td><td>☆</td><td>不及物动词</td><td>占主导，占多数</td><td>predominate in the region<br>在该地区占主导</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Pine trees predominate on the northern slopes of the valley.  松树在山谷北坡占主导。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 rule</td><td>/ˈruːl/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>名词、动词</td><td>规则，规定</td><td>break the rules<br>违反规定</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Anyone who breaks the rules twice loses their membership.  任何人两次违反规定就会失去会员资格。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 impotence</td><td>/ˈɪmpətəns/</td><td>★☆</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>无力，无能</td><td>a sense of impotence<br>无能为力之感</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Voters expressed impotence in the face of rising prices.  面对物价上涨，选民表达了无能为力之感。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 impotency</td><td>/ˈɪmpətənsi/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>无力，无能</td><td>political impotency<br>政治上的无能</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The crisis exposed the impotency of the old committee.  这场危机暴露了老委员会的无能。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 powerlessness</td><td>/ˈpaʊərləsnəs/</td><td>★☆</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>无力，无权</td><td>a sense of powerlessness<br>无力感</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Long unemployment bred a deep feeling of powerlessness.  长期失业催生了深深的无力感。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>rein</b></td><td>/reɪn/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>名词</td><td>抑制，限制</td><td>keep a tight rein<br>严加约束</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The new director kept a tight rein on every department's spending.  新任主管对各部门的开支严加约束。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 circumscription</td><td>/ˌsɜrkəmˈskrɪpʃən/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>限制，界限</td><td>the circumscription of power<br>对权力的限制</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Strict circumscription of their duties frustrated the young officers.  对职责的严格限制让这些年轻军官感到沮丧。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 confinement</td><td>/kənˈfaɪnmənt/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>名词</td><td>限制，禁闭；监禁</td><td>solitary confinement<br>单独禁闭</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Months of confinement indoors had left the children pale.  数月的室内禁闭让孩子们脸色发白。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 limitation</td><td>/ˌlɪmɪˈteɪʃən/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★</td><td>名词</td><td>限制，局限</td><td>impose limitations on<br>对…施加限制</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Budget limitations forced the crew to shoot indoors.  预算限制迫使剧组在室内拍摄。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 stint</td><td>/stɪnt/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>不及物动词</td><td>吝惜，节省</td><td>stint on materials<br>在材料上省钱</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The builder refused to stint on materials even when costs rose.  即便成本上升，这位建筑商也不肯在材料上省钱。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 bridle</td><td>/ˈbraɪdl/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>动词</td><td>限制</td><td>bridle his anger<br>克制他的怒气</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She bridled her temper and answered the rude question politely.  她克制住脾气，礼貌地回答了那个无礼的问题。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 check</td><td>/tʃek/</td><td>★★★★☆</td><td>★★★★☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>使突然停止，阻止；检查，核对</td><td>check the spread of disease<br>阻止疾病蔓延</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Strict quarantine measures checked the spread of the disease.  严格的隔离措施阻止了疾病的蔓延。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 constrain</td><td>/kənˈstreɪn/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>限制</td><td>constrain his freedom<br>限制他的自由</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Strict rules constrain his freedom to travel outside the country.  严格的规定限制了他出国旅行的自由。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 contain</td><td>/kənˈteɪn/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>控制，抑制；包含</td><td>contain the outbreak<br>控制疫情爆发</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Doctors worked around the clock to contain the outbreak.  医生们昼夜不停地努力控制疫情。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 curb</td><td>/ˈkɜːrb/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>及物动词、名词</td><td>控制，约束</td><td>curb government spending<br>控制政府开支</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The city introduced new rules to curb traffic downtown.  市政府出台新规以限制市区交通。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 govern</td><td>/ˈɡʌvərn/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>动词</td><td>控制，支配；统治</td><td>govern his temper<br>控制他的脾气</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Strict rules govern how the samples must be stored.  严格的规定约束着样本必须如何保存。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 hold</td><td>/ˈhoʊld/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>怀有，心存</td><td>hold a grudge<br>心怀怨恨</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He still holds a grudge against his former business partner.  他仍然对昔日的生意伙伴心怀怨恨。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 inhibit</td><td>/ɪnˈhɪbɪt/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>抑制，阻碍</td><td>inhibit the growth of bacteria<br>抑制细菌生长</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Cold weather inhibits the growth of these tropical plants.  寒冷天气抑制了这些热带植物的生长。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 keep</td><td>/ˈkiːp/</td><td>★★★★☆</td><td>★★★★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>遵守，坚持；保留</td><td>keep a promise<br>守约，履行承诺</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He always keeps his promises, however inconvenient they are.  无论多不方便，他总是守约。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 measure</td><td>/ˈmeʒər/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>名词、及物动词</td><td>衡量标准；测量；措施</td><td>a measure of success<br>衡量成功的尺度</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Test scores are a poor measure of a child's real ability.  考试分数不是衡量孩子真实能力的好尺度。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳近 manacle</del></td><td>/ˈmænəkl/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>给…戴上手铐；束缚，限制</td><td>manacle the prisoner<br>给囚犯戴上手铐</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Heavy debts manacle the young couple, leaving them no freedom to change careers.  沉重的债务束缚了这对年轻夫妇，让他们没有转行的自由。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 regulate</td><td>/ˈreɡjəˌleɪt/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>管理，调控，规范</td><td>regulate the industry<br>对该行业进行监管</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 New laws regulate how companies collect personal data.  新法规范了公司收集个人数据的方式。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 restrain</td><td>/rɪˈstreɪn/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>抑制，约束，制止</td><td>restrain his temper<br>克制他的脾气</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She could hardly restrain her laughter during the speech.  演讲时她几乎忍不住笑。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 rule</td><td>/ˈruːl/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>名词、动词</td><td>规则，规定</td><td>break the rules<br>违反规定</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Anyone who breaks the rules twice loses their membership.  任何人两次违反规定就会失去会员资格。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 tame</td><td>/teɪm/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>形容词、及物动词</td><td>被驯化的；控制，抑制</td><td>tame the wild horse<br>驯服野马</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 It took the trainer months to tame the wild horse completely.  训马师花了几个月才彻底驯服那匹野马。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 liberate</td><td>/ˈlɪbəreɪt/</td><td>★★</td><td>☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>解放，释放</td><td>liberate the city<br>解放这座城市</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Allied troops liberated the city in the spring of that year.  盟军在那年春天解放了这座城市。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 loose</td><td>/ˈluːs/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>及物动词、形容词</td><td>释放，放开；松的</td><td>loose the dogs<br>放开猎狗</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The keeper loosed the hawk and watched it climb into the sky.  饲养员放开那只鹰，看它飞向天空。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 loosen</td><td>/ˈluːsən/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>动词</td><td>放松，松开；解除限制</td><td>loosen the restrictions<br>放宽限制</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The government loosened controls on foreign investment last year.  政府去年放宽了对外国投资的管制。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 unleash</td><td>/ənˈliːʃ/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>释放，发动</td><td>unleash a storm of protest<br>引发一场抗议风暴</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The decision unleashed criticism from every corner of the press.  这一决定引发了媒体各方的批评。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>reiterate</b></td><td>/riˈɪtəreɪt/</td><td>★★</td><td>☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>重申</td><td>reiterate his warning<br>重申他的警告</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The minister reiterated his warning about the danger of further delay.  部长重申了他关于再延误将有危险的警告。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 chime</td><td>/ˈtʃaɪm/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>动词、名词</td><td>反复说；鸣响，报时</td><td>chime the same phrase<br>反复念同一句话</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The candidate chimed the same three slogans at every campaign stop.  这位候选人在每个竞选点都反复念着同样的三句口号。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 din</td><td>/ˈdɪn/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>名词</td><td>嘈杂喧闹的声音</td><td>the din of traffic<br>交通的喧闹声</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 We could barely talk above the din of the factory.  在工厂的喧闹声中我们几乎无法交谈。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 iterate</td><td>/ˈɪtəˌreɪt/</td><td>★</td><td>☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>重复，反复说</td><td>iterate the main point<br>重复要点</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The teacher iterated the instructions until everyone understood them.  老师反复讲解要求，直到所有人都听懂。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 recapitulate</td><td>/ˌriːkəˈpɪtʃuleɪt/</td><td>☆</td><td>★</td><td>动词</td><td>概括，摘要</td><td>recapitulate the main points<br>概括要点</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Let me recapitulate the main points before we vote.  投票前让我概括一下要点。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 rehearse</td><td>/riˈhɜːrs/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>动词</td><td>重述，反复讲；排练</td><td>rehearse his grievances<br>反复诉说他的不满</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He rehearsed the same complaints at every family dinner.  他在每次家庭晚餐上都重复同样的抱怨。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 repeat</td><td>/rɪˈpiːt/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>动词、名词</td><td>重复，重述</td><td>repeat the question<br>重复这个问题</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Could you repeat the address more slowly this time?  你能把地址再慢一点重复一遍吗？</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>rejoice</b></td><td>/rɪˈdʒɔɪs/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>不及物动词</td><td>欣喜，喜悦</td><td>rejoice at the news<br>为消息而欣喜</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The whole village rejoiced at the news of the safe return.  听到平安归来的消息，全村都欣喜不已。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 crow</td><td>/kroʊ/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>不及物动词</td><td>得意地夸耀，自鸣得意</td><td>crow about the win<br>为获胜而得意</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He crowed about his victory until even his closest friends grew tired.  他为胜利得意个不停，连最亲近的朋友都听厌了。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 delight</td><td>/dɪˈlaɪt/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>及物动词、名词</td><td>使高兴，使愉快；愉悦</td><td>delight the audience<br>使观众高兴</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The young pianist delighted the audience with a lively encore.  这位年轻钢琴家用一首活泼的加演曲取悦了观众。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳近 exuberate</del></td><td>/ɪɡˈzubəˌreɪt/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>不及物动词</td><td>兴高采烈，极其欢欣</td><td>exuberate over the news<br>为这消息欢欣不已</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The whole village exuberated when the harvest proved unusually rich.  收成出奇地好，全村人欢天喜地。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 glory</td><td>/ˈɡlɔːri/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★</td><td>动词、名词</td><td>感到自豪，得意；荣耀</td><td>glory in her success<br>为她的成功而得意</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He gloried in the praise his research finally received.  他为研究终于获得的赞誉而洋洋自得。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 jubilate</td><td>/ˈdʒubəleɪt/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>不及物动词</td><td>欢欣，喜悦</td><td>jubilate over the news<br>为这消息而欢欣</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The whole town began to jubilate when the rescue team returned safely.  救援队平安归来时，全城开始欢庆。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 joy</td><td>/ˈdʒɔɪ/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>名词</td><td>欢乐，喜悦</td><td>jump for joy<br>高兴得跳起来</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The news of his recovery filled the whole family with joy.  他康复的消息让全家人充满喜悦。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳近 kvell</del></td><td>/kvɛl/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>不及物动词</td><td>（因骄傲而）满心欢喜</td><td>kvell over her success<br>为她的成功由衷高兴</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 His parents kvelled as he walked across the graduation stage.  他走过毕业典礼的舞台时，父母满心欢喜。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 triumph</td><td>/ˈtraɪəmf/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>名词、不及物动词</td><td>巨大成功，胜利</td><td>a stunning military triumph<br>惊人的军事胜利</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The new bridge is a triumph of modern engineering and careful planning.  这座新桥是现代工程与周密规划的巨大成功。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 bemoan</td><td>/bɪˈmoʊn/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>哀叹，抱怨</td><td>bemoan his bad luck<br>哀叹自己运气不好</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Farmers bemoaned the loss of their crops after the flood.  洪水过后农民哀叹作物尽毁。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳反 bewail</del></td><td>/bɪˈweɪl/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>痛惜，哀叹</td><td>bewail her sad fate<br>哀叹她的悲惨命运</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The villagers gathered to bewail the loss of their homes.  村民聚在一起痛惜失去家园。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 deplore</td><td>/dɪˈplɔːr/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>谴责，强烈反对</td><td>deplore the violence<br>谴责暴力行为</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Church leaders deplored the violence and called for immediate restraint.  教会领袖谴责暴力行为，呼吁立即克制。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 grieve</td><td>/ɡriːv/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>不及物动词、及物动词</td><td>（使）感到悲伤</td><td>grieve for a lost friend<br>为逝去的朋友悲伤</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The whole village grieved for the fishermen lost in the autumn storm.  全村人都为在秋季风暴中失踪的渔民而悲伤。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 lament</td><td>/ləˈment/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>动词</td><td>为…哀悼，表达痛苦或遗憾</td><td>lament the loss<br>哀叹损失</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Older residents still lament the loss of the orchards that once surrounded town.  年长的居民仍在哀叹曾环绕小镇的果园的消失。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 moan</td><td>/ˈmoʊn/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>动词、名词</td><td>哀叹，抱怨</td><td>moan about the weather<br>抱怨天气</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 They moaned about the heat for the entire drive.  整段车程他们都在抱怨炎热。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 mourn</td><td>/ˈmɔːrn/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>动词</td><td>哀悼，悲伤</td><td>mourn the dead<br>哀悼死者</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The whole village mourned the fishermen lost at sea.  全村哀悼在海上失踪的渔民。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 regret</td><td>/rəˈɡret/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>动词、名词</td><td>后悔，遗憾；懊悔</td><td>regret his decision<br>后悔他的决定</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She regretted not saying goodbye before he left the country.  她后悔在他出国前没有道别。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 weep</td><td>/ˈwiːp/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>动词</td><td>哭泣，流泪</td><td>weep bitterly<br>痛哭</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Some viewers still weep at the closing scene of that old film.  有些观众看到那部老电影的结尾时依然会哭。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>relapse</b></td><td>/rɪˈlæps/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>不及物动词、名词</td><td>重蹈覆辙，再犯</td><td>relapse into old habits<br>重犯旧习</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Without support many patients relapse into old habits within months.  缺乏支持的话，许多病人几个月内就重蹈旧习。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳近 recidivate</del></td><td>/rɪˈsɪdəveɪt/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>不及物动词</td><td>回到原先的习惯，尤指重新犯罪</td><td>recidivate after release<br>获释后重新犯罪</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Many offenders recidivate within a year of release without proper support.  许多罪犯在缺乏适当支持时会在获释一年内重新犯罪。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 regress</td><td>/rɪˈɡres/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>不及物动词</td><td>退化，恶化</td><td>regress to childish behavior<br>退回到幼稚行为</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Under stress he tends to regress to childish and sulky behavior.  在压力下他往往退化为幼稚而怨怼的行为。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳近 retrogress</del></td><td>/ˌrɛtrəˈɡrɛs/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>不及物动词</td><td>退步，倒退</td><td>retrogress to barbarism<br>倒退回野蛮状态</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Without steady funding the program began to retrogress noticeably.  缺乏稳定资金，这个项目开始明显倒退。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 revert</td><td>/rɪˈvɜːrt/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>不及物动词</td><td>恢复原状，回复</td><td>revert to type<br>回到本性</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 After a week of dieting he reverted to eating late-night snacks.  节食一周后，他又回到深夜吃零食的老样子。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳反 habilitate</del></td><td>/həˈbɪlɪˌteɪt/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>使具备能力，使胜任</td><td>habilitate disabled workers<br>使残障工人具备工作能力</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The center exists to habilitate young adults with learning difficulties.  该中心旨在使有学习障碍的青年具备生活能力。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 reclaim</td><td>/riˈkleɪm/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>感化，使改造；开垦，回收</td><td>reclaim young offenders<br>感化少年犯</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The charity tries to reclaim addicts through work and counseling.  该慈善机构试图通过工作和咨询感化成瘾者。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 redeem</td><td>/rɪˈdiːm/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>改过自新</td><td>redeem oneself through service<br>以服务改过自新</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He hoped to redeem himself through years of quiet public service.  他希望通过多年默默的公共服务来改过自新。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 regenerate</td><td>/rɪˈdʒenəreɪt/</td><td>★★</td><td>☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>使重获新生，使焕然一新</td><td>regenerate a neighborhood<br>使一个街区焕然一新</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The city spent millions to regenerate a neighborhood near the old port.  该市花费数百万使旧港附近的一个街区焕然一新。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>rehash</b></td><td>/riːˈhæʃ/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>动词</td><td>重复，老调重弹</td><td>rehash old arguments<br>重弹旧论调</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The candidates simply rehashed old arguments instead of offering new ideas.  候选人只是老调重弹，没有提出新的想法。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>relentless</b></td><td>/rɪˈlentləs/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>残酷的，无情的；固执的，不肯妥协的</td><td>relentless pressure<br>无休止的压力</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Relentless pressure from creditors finally forced the small firm to close.  债权人无情的压力最终迫使这家小公司关门。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 grim</td><td>/ˈɡrɪm/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>冷酷的，不肯让步的；严峻的</td><td>grim determination<br>冷酷的决心</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He fought on with grim persistence until the very end.  他以冷酷的坚韧一直战斗到最后。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 inexorable</td><td>/ɪnˈeksərəbl/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>无法劝阻的，不为所动的</td><td>the inexorable march of time<br>时间无情的推进</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The inexorable rise of the tide slowly swallowed the sandcastle we built.  潮水无可阻挡地上涨，慢慢吞没了我们堆的沙堡。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳近 ironfisted</del></td><td>/ˈaɪərnˌfɪstɪd/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>铁腕的，严厉无情的</td><td>ironfisted rule<br>铁腕统治</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The colony was run by an ironfisted governor for thirty years.  这块殖民地被一位铁腕总督统治了三十年。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 merciless</td><td>/ˈmɜːrsələs/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>毫无仁慈的，无情的</td><td>a merciless critic<br>毫不留情的批评者</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The merciless summer sun beat down on the empty streets.  无情的夏日阳光炙烤着空荡的街道。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 mortal</td><td>/ˈmɔːrtəl/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>形容词、名词</td><td>终将死亡的，凡人的；致命的</td><td>mortal beings<br>凡人</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The play reminds us that even great kings are mortal men.  这部剧提醒我们连伟大的国王也是凡人。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 ruthless</td><td>/ˈruːθləs/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>没有怜悯心的，残忍的</td><td>a ruthless dictator<br>残忍无情的独裁者</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The general was ruthless with anyone who questioned his orders.  将军对任何质疑其命令的人都残忍无情。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 unrelenting</td><td>/ˌʌnrɪˈlentɪŋ/</td><td>★☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>不松懈的，不屈不挠的</td><td>unrelenting pressure<br>持续不断的压力</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Her unrelenting effort finally forced the company to apologize.  她不懈的努力最终迫使公司道歉。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 adamant</td><td>/ˈædəmənt/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>固执的，不可动摇的</td><td>adamant about the rules<br>对规则毫不通融</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The coach was adamant that no player would skip practice.  教练坚决要求没有球员可以缺席训练。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 dogged</td><td>/ˈdɔːɡɪd/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>顽强的；固执的</td><td>dogged determination<br>顽强的决心</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Her dogged determination carried the project through two years of setbacks.  她顽强的决心让这个项目度过了两年的挫折。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 headstrong</td><td>/ˈhedˌstrɔːŋ/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>任性的，固执的</td><td>a headstrong child<br>任性的孩子</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The headstrong young man ignored every warning from his parents.  这个任性的年轻人对父母的每句劝告都置之不理。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳近 mulish</del></td><td>/ˈmjuːlɪʃ/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>固执的</td><td>mulish refusal to listen<br>固执地不肯听</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 His mulish refusal to listen frustrated everyone on the committee.  他固执地拒绝倾听，让委员会所有人都很沮丧。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 obdurate</td><td>/ˈɑːbdərət/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>固执的；冷酷无情的</td><td>remain obdurate<br>仍然固执不改</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The landlord was obdurate and refused to lower the rent.  房东顽固不化，拒绝降低房租。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳近 pertinacious</del></td><td>/ˌpɜːrtnˈeɪʃəs/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>顽固的，固执地坚持的</td><td>pertinacious in her demands<br>固执地坚持要求</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The pertinacious reporter kept calling until she got an answer.  这位执着的记者不停打电话，直到得到答复。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 rigid</td><td>/ˈrɪdʒəd/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>严格的，僵硬的</td><td>rigid discipline<br>严格的纪律</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The school enforces rigid rules about mobile phones.  学校对手机执行严格的规定。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 uncompromising</td><td>/ʌnˈkɑːmprəmaɪzɪŋ/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>不妥协的，坚定的</td><td>an uncompromising stance<br>毫不妥协的立场</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She took an uncompromising stand against corruption in the department.  她对部门内的腐败采取了毫不妥协的立场。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 unyielding</td><td>/ʌnˈjiːldɪŋ/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>不屈的，不让步的</td><td>unyielding opposition<br>毫不让步的反对</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The union remained unyielding on the question of wages.  工会在工资问题上依然毫不让步。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 charitable</td><td>/ˈtʃærətəbəl/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>宽容的，仁慈的</td><td>a charitable view<br>宽容的看法</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He took a charitable view of his student's careless mistake.  他对学生的粗心错误持宽容态度。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 clement</td><td>/ˈklemənt/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★</td><td>形容词</td><td>宽容的，善良的；气候温和的</td><td>a clement judge<br>宽容的法官</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 A clement judge gave the young offender a second chance.  一位宽容的法官给了这名年轻罪犯第二次机会。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 merciful</td><td>/ˈmɜːrsɪfəl/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>仁慈的，宽厚的</td><td>a merciful judge<br>宽仁的法官</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The court was merciful and gave him only a small fine.  法庭很宽仁，只罚了他少许款项。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 lenient</td><td>/ˈliːniənt/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>宽大仁慈的</td><td>a lenient sentence<br>从轻的判决</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The judge handed down a lenient sentence because of the boy's age.  由于这个男孩年纪小，法官作出了从轻的判决。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 acquiescent</td><td>/ˌækwiˈesnt/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>顺从的，默许的</td><td>an acquiescent audience<br>顺从的听众</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Too acquiescent to protest, the staff accepted the new rules.  员工太顺从，没有抗议就接受了新规。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 amenable</td><td>/əˈmiːnəbl/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>顺从的，服从的</td><td>amenable to suggestion<br>愿意接受建议</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The new manager is amenable to any reasonable suggestion.  这位新经理对任何合理的建议都乐于接受。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 compliant</td><td>/kəmˈplaɪənt/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>顺从的</td><td>a compliant employee<br>顺从的员工</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The children were unusually compliant during the long ceremony.  在漫长的仪式上，孩子们出奇地听话。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 complying</td><td>/kəmˈplaɪɪŋ/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>顺从的，遵从的</td><td>complying with the rules<br>遵从规定</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Firms complying with the standard receive a tax rebate.  遵从该标准的企业可获得退税。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 flexible</td><td>/ˈfleksəbl/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>灵活的，可变的；易受影响的</td><td>a flexible schedule<br>灵活的日程</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The company offers a flexible schedule to parents of young children.  公司为有幼儿的员工提供灵活的日程。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 pliable</td><td>/ˈplaɪəbl/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>易弯曲的，柔软的；易受影响的，温顺的</td><td>pliable young minds<br>易受影响的年轻心智</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Young minds are pliable and easily shaped by the adults around them.  年轻的心智易受影响，很容易被身边的成年人塑造。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 pliant</td><td>/ˈplaɪənt/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>形容词</td><td>易弯曲的；顺从的</td><td>a pliant assistant<br>顺从的助手</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He wanted a pliant assistant who would never question his decisions.  他想要一个顺从的助手，永远不会质疑他的决定。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 relenting</td><td>/rɪˈlentɪŋ/</td><td>☆</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>态度软化的，让步的</td><td>show a relenting attitude<br>表现出软化的态度</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 After hours of pleading, his relenting expression gave us hope.  在几小时的哀求之后，他态度软化的表情给了我们希望。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 yielding</td><td>/ˈjiːldɪŋ/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>顺从的，易屈服的</td><td>a yielding personality<br>顺从的性格</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Her yielding nature made her an easy target for pushy salesmen.  她顺从的性格使她很容易被强势的推销员盯上。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>relevant</b></td><td>/ˈreləvənt/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>有关系的，重要的</td><td>relevant to the case<br>与本案有关</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The judge ruled that the old letters were relevant to the case.  法官裁定那些旧信件与本案有关。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 applicable</td><td>/ˈæplɪkəbl/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>可用的，可行的；相关的，有关的</td><td>applicable to all students<br>适用于所有学生</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 This rule is applicable to all students regardless of their year.  这条规定适用于所有学生，不论他们是哪个年级。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 apposite</td><td>/ˈæpəzɪt/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>相关的，合适的</td><td>an apposite comment<br>恰当的评论</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She made one apposite comment that settled the whole argument.  她一句恰当的评论就平息了整场争论。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 apropos</td><td>/ˌæprəˈpoʊ/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>形容词、介词</td><td>相关的；关于，有关</td><td>apropos of nothing<br>毫无来由地</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Apropos of nothing, he asked whether I had ever been to Rome.  他毫无来由地问我是否去过罗马。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 germane</td><td>/dʒɜːrˈmeɪn/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>形容词</td><td>有关的，适当的</td><td>germane to the discussion<br>与讨论相关</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Her question was germane to the discussion and changed our plan.  她的问题与讨论密切相关，改变了我们的计划。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 material</td><td>/məˈtɪriəl/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>形容词、名词</td><td>物质的；重要的；材料</td><td>material comforts<br>物质上的享受</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He cared little for material wealth and lived very simply.  他不太在意物质财富，生活极为简朴。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 pertinent</td><td>/ˈpɜːrtnənt/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>相关的，恰当的</td><td>pertinent to the discussion<br>与讨论相关的</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Only evidence pertinent to the discussion should be presented today.  今天只应提出与讨论相关的证据。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 pointed</td><td>/ˈpɔɪntɪd/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>尖锐的，一针见血的</td><td>a pointed question<br>尖锐的问题</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She asked a pointed question that the minister could not answer.  她提出了一个部长无法回答的尖锐问题。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 relative</td><td>/ˈrelətɪv/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>形容词、名词</td><td>相关的；相对的；亲属</td><td>relative to the topic<br>与该话题相关</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Comfort is relative and depends largely on what you expect.  舒适是相对的，很大程度上取决于你的期望。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 extraneous</td><td>/ɪkˈstreɪniəs/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★</td><td>形容词</td><td>无关的，不重要的</td><td>extraneous information<br>无关的信息</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Please delete all extraneous details from the summary.  请把摘要中所有无关的细节删掉。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 immaterial</td><td>/ˌɪməˈtɪriəl/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>无关的，不重要的</td><td>immaterial to the case<br>与本案无关</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Whether he liked the plan is immaterial to the legal question here.  他是否喜欢这个计划与这里的法律问题无关。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 impertinent</td><td>/ɪmˈpɜːrtnənt/</td><td>★☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>不相关的；无礼的，鲁莽的</td><td>an impertinent remark<br>无礼的言论</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The lawyer objected that the question was impertinent to the charges.  律师反对说，这个问题与指控无关。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 inapplicable</td><td>/ˌɪnəˈplɪkəbəl/</td><td>★</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>不适用的，不相关的</td><td>inapplicable to this case<br>不适用于本案</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The old rule is inapplicable to digital contracts.  旧规则不适用于电子合同。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳反 inapposite</del></td><td>/ɪnˈæpəzɪt/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>不恰当的，不相关的</td><td>an inapposite comparison<br>不恰当的比较</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The comparison to wartime rationing seems inapposite today.  把它比作战时配给在今天显得不恰当。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 irrelevant</td><td>/ˌɪˈreləvənt/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>无关的，不相干的</td><td>an irrelevant detail<br>无关的细节</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The lawyer objected because the question was completely irrelevant here.  律师提出反对，因为这个问题在此完全无关。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 pointless</td><td>/ˈpɔɪntləs/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>无意义的，徒劳的</td><td>a pointless argument<br>毫无意义的争论</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Arguing with him about politics is completely pointless and exhausting.  和他争论政治完全没有意义，而且很累。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>reminiscent</b></td><td>/ˌremɪˈnɪsnt/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>怀旧的，引发回忆的</td><td>reminiscent of childhood<br>让人想起童年</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The smell of wet grass was strongly reminiscent of her childhood summers.  湿草的气味强烈地让她想起童年的夏天。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 evocative</td><td>/ɪˈvɑːkətɪv/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>引起回忆的，唤起感情的</td><td>evocative of the countryside<br>让人想起乡间</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The smell of woodsmoke is evocative of winters at my grandparents' house.  柴火烟味让我想起在祖父母家过的冬天。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 suggestive</td><td>/səˈdʒestɪv/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>引人联想的，暗示的</td><td>a suggestive silence<br>意味深长的沉默</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 His suggestive silence told the reporters more than words.  他意味深长的沉默比言语告诉记者的更多。</td></tr>
</tbody>
</table>

## Unit 3

<table>
<thead><tr>
<th>单词</th>
<th>音标</th>
<th>常用</th>
<th>口语</th>
<th>词性</th>
<th>中文释义</th>
<th>常用短语</th>
</tr></thead>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>relinquish</b></td><td>/rɪˈlɪŋkwɪʃ/</td><td>★★</td><td>★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>放弃（权力、职位）</td><td>relinquish control<br>放弃控制权</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She refused to relinquish control of the family business.  她拒绝放弃对家族企业的控制权。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳近 abnegate</del></td><td>/ˈæbnɪɡeɪt/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>动词</td><td>放弃，否认</td><td>abnegate one's rights<br>放弃自己的权利</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The monk chose to abnegate all personal comforts.  这位僧人选择放弃一切个人享受。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 cede</td><td>/siːd/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>放弃，割让</td><td>cede territory<br>割让领土</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The defeated nation was forced to cede two provinces.  战败国被迫割让两个省。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 renounce</td><td>/rɪˈnaʊns/</td><td>★★</td><td>★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>正式放弃，声明弃绝</td><td>renounce one's claim<br>声明放弃权利主张</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He renounced his claim to the inheritance in writing.  他以书面形式放弃了继承权。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 resign</td><td>/rɪˈzaɪn/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>动词</td><td>辞职，放弃（职位）</td><td>resign from office<br>辞去公职</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The minister resigned from office after the scandal broke.  丑闻爆发后，那位部长辞职了。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 surrender</td><td>/səˈrendər/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>动词</td><td>交出，放弃，投降</td><td>surrender to the police<br>向警方投降</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The thief finally surrendered to the police after a long chase.  经过长时间追捕，小偷终于向警方投降。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 inaugurate</td><td>/ɪˈnɔːɡjəreɪt/</td><td>★☆</td><td>☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>开始</td><td>inaugurate a new era<br>开创一个新时代</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The treaty inaugurated a long period of peace between the two nations.  这项条约开启了两国之间长期的和平。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>relish</b></td><td>/ˈrelɪʃ/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>名词、及物动词</td><td>享受，喜爱；乐趣，兴味</td><td>with obvious relish<br>带着明显的享受</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He described the meal with obvious relish.  他津津有味地描述那顿饭。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 appetite</td><td>/ˈæpəˌtaɪt/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>名词</td><td>欲望，爱好；食欲</td><td>an appetite for risk<br>对风险的偏好</td></tr>
