<tr><td colspan="7">例 The public has a huge appetite for stories about lost cities.  公众对失落之城的故事有极大兴趣。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 fancy</td><td>/ˈfænsi/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★★★</td><td>及物动词、名词</td><td>喜爱，想要</td><td>fancy a cup of tea<br>想喝杯茶</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Do you fancy a walk along the river this evening?  今晚想不想去河边散散步？</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 favor</td><td>/ˈfeɪvər/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>名词、及物动词</td><td>赞同，好评；恩惠</td><td>win public favor<br>赢得公众好评</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The proposal quickly won favor among the younger members.  这项提议很快赢得年轻成员的支持。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 fondness</td><td>/ˈfɑːndnəs/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>名词</td><td>喜爱，偏爱</td><td>a fondness for old films<br>对老电影的喜爱</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He inherited his mother's fondness for long country walks.  他继承了母亲对乡间长途散步的喜爱。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 like</td><td>/ˈlaɪk/</td><td>★★★★★</td><td>★★★★★</td><td>形容词、及物动词</td><td>相似的，像；喜欢</td><td>two of like mind<br>两个想法相似的人</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 They were of like mind on almost every important question.  他们在几乎所有重要问题上想法相似。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 love</td><td>/ˈlʌv/</td><td>★★★★★</td><td>★★★★★</td><td>动词、名词</td><td>爱，热爱；喜爱</td><td>fall in love with<br>爱上，与…相爱</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 I love walking along the river in the early morning.  我喜欢清晨沿着河边散步。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 partiality</td><td>/ˌpɑːrʃiˈæləti/</td><td>★</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>偏爱；偏袒</td><td>a partiality for sweets<br>对甜食的偏爱</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The coach was accused of partiality toward his own students.  教练被指责偏袒自己的学生。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 preference</td><td>/ˈprefərəns/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★☆</td><td>名词</td><td>偏好，偏爱</td><td>a preference for quiet<br>偏好安静</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Most readers expressed a preference for the printed edition.  多数读者表示更偏好印刷版。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 taste</td><td>/ˈteɪst/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>名词、动词</td><td>爱好，兴味；品尝；味道</td><td>a taste for adventure<br>对冒险的爱好</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He developed a taste for opera surprisingly late in life.  他在相当晚的年纪才培养出对歌剧的爱好。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 adore</td><td>/əˈdɔːr/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>热爱，极喜爱；崇拜</td><td>absolutely adore someone<br>非常喜爱某人</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The children adore their new puppy.  孩子们非常喜爱他们的新小狗。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 enjoy</td><td>/ɪnˈdʒɔɪ/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>享受，喜爱</td><td>enjoy every minute<br>享受每一分钟</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She enjoys long walks along the river after dinner.  她喜欢晚饭后沿河长距离散步。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 rejoice</td><td>/rɪˈdʒɔɪs/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>不及物动词</td><td>欣喜，喜悦</td><td>rejoice at the news<br>为消息而欣喜</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The whole village rejoiced at the news of the safe return.  听到平安归来的消息，全村都欣喜不已。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 savor</td><td>/ˈseɪvər/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>品味，享受</td><td>savor every moment<br>品味每一刻</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She paused to savor the smell of fresh bread.  她停下来细细品味新鲜面包的香味。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 aversion</td><td>/əˈvɜːrʒən/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>名词</td><td>厌恶，反感</td><td>a strong aversion to<br>对…强烈厌恶</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He has a lifelong aversion to crowded parties.  他一生都厌恶拥挤的聚会。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 disfavor</td><td>/dɪsˈfeɪvər/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>不赞成；失宠</td><td>fall into disfavor<br>失宠，不受待见</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The old method gradually fell into disfavor among researchers.  这种旧方法逐渐不受研究者青睐。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 disgust</td><td>/dɪsˈɡʌst/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>及物动词、名词</td><td>使厌恶；厌恶</td><td>disgust even his allies<br>连他的盟友也感到厌恶</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The state of the kitchen disgusted the health inspector.  厨房的状况让卫生检查员十分厌恶。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 dislike</td><td>/dɪsˈlaɪk/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>动词、名词</td><td>不喜欢，厌恶</td><td>dislike crowded places<br>讨厌拥挤的地方</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He dislikes crowded places and avoids the subway during rush hour.  他讨厌拥挤的地方，高峰时段避开地铁。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 distaste</td><td>/dɪsˈteɪst/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>名词</td><td>厌恶，反感</td><td>a distaste for gossip<br>对闲话的厌恶</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Her distaste for gossip kept her out of office politics.  她厌恶闲话，因此远离办公室政治。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 hatred</td><td>/ˈheɪtrəd/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>名词</td><td>憎恨，仇恨</td><td>deep-seated hatred<br>根深蒂固的仇恨</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Years of war left a hatred that no treaty could erase.  多年战争留下的仇恨，任何条约都无法消除。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 loathing</td><td>/ˈloʊθɪŋ/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>名词</td><td>厌恶，憎恶</td><td>a deep loathing of cruelty<br>对残忍的深切憎恶</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He wrote with obvious loathing about the old regime.  他带着明显的憎恶书写旧政权。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 hate</td><td>/ˈheɪt/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>动词、名词</td><td>憎恨，厌恶</td><td>hate being lied to<br>讨厌被人骗</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She hates being lied to, even about very small things.  她讨厌被骗，哪怕是很小的事。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 loathe</td><td>/loʊð/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>厌恶，讨厌</td><td>loathe the sight of<br>一见就厌恶</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He loathes the smell of boiled cabbage.  他讨厌煮卷心菜的味道。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>reluctant</b></td><td>/rɪˈlʌktənt/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>不情愿的，勉强的</td><td>reluctant to answer<br>不愿回答</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The witness seemed reluctant to answer any question about that evening.  证人似乎不愿回答关于那天晚上的任何问题。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 averse</td><td>/əˈvɜːrs/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>厌恶的，反对的</td><td>averse to risk<br>厌恶风险的</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Older investors are usually averse to risk of any kind.  年长的投资者通常厌恶任何形式的风险。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 disinclined</td><td>/ˌdɪsɪnˈklaɪnd/</td><td>★</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>不愿的，不情愿的</td><td>disinclined to help<br>不愿帮忙</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 After the argument she was disinclined to help him again.  争吵之后她不愿再帮他。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 indisposed</td><td>/ˌɪndɪˈspoʊzd/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>形容词</td><td>不愿的；身体不适的</td><td>indisposed to talk<br>不愿交谈</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The witness seemed indisposed to talk about that evening.  证人似乎不愿谈那晚的事。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 loath</td><td>/loʊθ/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>不情愿的，勉强的</td><td>loath to admit it<br>不愿承认这一点</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He was loath to admit that his own carelessness caused the accident.  他不愿承认是自己的粗心造成了这场事故。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 unwilling</td><td>/ənˈwɪlɪŋ/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>不愿意的，勉强的</td><td>unwilling to compromise<br>不愿妥协</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He was clearly unwilling to discuss what happened that night.  他显然不愿谈那晚发生的事。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 willing</td><td>/ˈwɪlɪŋ/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>愿意的，乐意的</td><td>willing to help<br>愿意帮忙</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Several neighbors were willing to look after the children.  几位邻居都愿意照看孩子。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>remiss</b></td><td>/rɪˈmɪs/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★</td><td>形容词</td><td>疏忽的，不留心的</td><td>be remiss in one's duties<br>疏于职守</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 I would be remiss if I did not thank the volunteers.  如果我不感谢志愿者，那就是我的疏忽。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 careless</td><td>/ˈkerləs/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>粗心的，疏忽的</td><td>a careless mistake<br>粗心的错误</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 One careless remark ruined months of patient negotiation.  一句不慎的话毁了数月的耐心谈判。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 derelict</td><td>/ˈderəlɪkt/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>玩忽职守的，不认真的</td><td>derelict in his duty<br>玩忽职守</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The guard was found derelict in his duty on the night of the theft.  那名警卫被认定在失窃当夜玩忽职守。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳近 disregardful</del></td><td>/ˌdɪsrɪˈɡɑrdfəl/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>不顾的，漠视的</td><td>disregardful of safety<br>漠视安全</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The driver was disregardful of every posted speed limit.  这名司机漠视每一处限速标志。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 heedless</td><td>/ˈhiːdləs/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>形容词</td><td>不注意的，不顾的</td><td>heedless of danger<br>不顾危险</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He ran into the street heedless of the traffic.  他不顾车流冲进了街道。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 lax</td><td>/læks/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>松懈的，不严格的；漫不经心的</td><td>lax security standards<br>松懈的安保标准</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Lax security standards at the warehouse allowed the theft to go unnoticed.  仓库松懈的安保标准使这起盗窃未被发觉。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 lazy</td><td>/ˈleɪzi/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>懒惰的</td><td>too lazy to cook<br>懒得做饭</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He was too lazy to walk the dog on cold mornings.  寒冷的早晨他懒得去遛狗。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 neglectful</td><td>/nɪˈɡlektfəl/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>形容词</td><td>疏忽的，不留心的</td><td>neglectful of his duties<br>疏忽职责</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The report found the agency neglectful of basic safety inspections.  报告认定该机构疏忽了基本安全检查。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 neglecting</td><td>/nɪˈɡlektɪŋ/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词、动词</td><td>忽视的，怠慢的</td><td>neglecting his studies<br>忽视学业</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He spent the term gaming, neglecting every assignment he received.  他整学期打游戏，忽视了收到的每项作业。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 slack</td><td>/slæk/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>松弛的；疏忽的，大意的</td><td>slack about deadlines<br>对截止日期松懈</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The new manager was slack about deadlines and lost several clients.  新经理对截止日期很松懈，因此丢了好几个客户。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 attentive</td><td>/əˈtentɪv/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>留心的，专注的；周到的</td><td>an attentive audience<br>专注的听众</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The nurse stayed attentive to every change in his breathing.  护士留心他呼吸的每一点变化。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 careful</td><td>/ˈkerfəl/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>谨慎的，仔细的</td><td>careful consideration<br>慎重考虑</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Be careful when you cross this busy street at night.  夜里穿过这条繁忙的街道时要小心。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 conscientious</td><td>/ˌkɑːnʃiˈenʃəs/</td><td>★★</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>仔细的，一丝不苟的；有良心的，正直的</td><td>a conscientious worker<br>一位认真的员工</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 A conscientious editor checks every fact before the article goes to print.  一丝不苟的编辑会在文章付印前核实每一个事实。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 meticulous</td><td>/məˈtɪkjələs/</td><td>★★</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>一丝不苟的，极为细致的</td><td>meticulous attention to detail<br>对细节极为在意</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The restorer's meticulous attention to detail saved the fragile manuscript from further damage.  修复师一丝不苟地关注细节，使这份脆弱的手稿免于进一步损坏。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 painstaking</td><td>/ˈpeɪnzteɪkɪŋ/</td><td>★☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>煞费苦心的，一丝不苟的</td><td>painstaking research<br>煞费苦心的研究</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Years of painstaking research went into this single short article.  这篇短文背后是多年一丝不苟的研究。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 scrupulous</td><td>/ˈskruːpjələs/</td><td>★☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>正直的；一丝不苟的</td><td>scrupulous honesty<br>一丝不苟的诚实</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Her scrupulous honesty made her the obvious choice to handle the accounts.  她一丝不苟的诚实使她成为管账的当然人选。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>resent</b></td><td>/rɪˈzent/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>动词</td><td>怨恨，憎恨；抗议，抱怨</td><td>resent the interference<br>对干涉感到愤恨</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Older staff members resent being asked to train their own replacements.  资深员工对被要求培训自己的替代者感到愤恨。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 begrudge</td><td>/bɪˈɡrʌdʒ/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>嫉妒，不愿给予</td><td>begrudge her success<br>嫉妒她的成功</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Nobody begrudges him the rest he has clearly earned.  没人不满他享受这份显然应得的休息。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 envy</td><td>/ˈenvi/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>动词、名词</td><td>妒忌，羡慕</td><td>envy his talent<br>羡慕他的才华</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 I envy the ease with which she speaks three languages.  我羡慕她能轻松讲三种语言。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>remonstrance</b></td><td>/rɪˈmɑːnstrəns/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>抗议，规劝</td><td>a formal written remonstrance<br>正式的书面抗议</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The villagers sent a written remonstrance to the district governor.  村民向区长递交了一份书面抗议。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 challenge</td><td>/ˈtʃæləndʒ/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>及物动词、名词</td><td>质疑，对…提出挑战</td><td>challenge the theory<br>质疑该理论</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Several scientists challenged the conclusions of the new study.  几位科学家质疑这项新研究的结论。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 complaint</td><td>/kəmˈpleɪnt/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>名词</td><td>抱怨，投诉</td><td>file a complaint<br>提出投诉</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Several residents filed a complaint about the noise from the site.  几位居民就工地噪音提出了投诉。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 demur</td><td>/dɪˈmɜːr/</td><td>☆</td><td>★</td><td>名词、不及物动词</td><td>表示异议，反对</td><td>demur at the suggestion<br>对建议表示异议</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She demurred at the suggestion that she should resign.  对于让她辞职的建议，她表示了异议。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 difficulty</td><td>/ˈdɪfəkəlti/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>名词</td><td>异议，反对；困难</td><td>make no difficulty about<br>对…不加异议</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The landlord made no difficulty about renewing our lease.  房东对续租没提任何异议。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 fuss</td><td>/fʌs/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>名词、不及物动词</td><td>小题大做，抱怨；忙乱</td><td>make a fuss about<br>为…大惊小怪</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Don't make a fuss about a tiny stain on the tablecloth.  别为桌布上一小块污渍小题大做。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 objection</td><td>/əbˈdʒekʃən/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★★</td><td>名词</td><td>反对，异议</td><td>raise an objection<br>提出异议</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The lawyer raised an objection before the witness could answer.  证人还没来得及回答，律师就提出了异议。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 protest</td><td>/ˈproʊˌtest/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>名词、动词</td><td>抗议，反对</td><td>register a formal protest<br>提出正式抗议</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The ambassador registered a formal protest with the foreign ministry.  大使向外交部提出正式抗议。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 question</td><td>/ˈkwestʃən/</td><td>★★★★☆</td><td>★★★</td><td>及物动词、名词</td><td>询问，质疑；问题</td><td>question a witness<br>询问证人</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Police questioned three men about the late-night break-in.  警方就深夜入室盗窃询问了三名男子。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳近 remonstration</del></td><td>/ˌrimɑnˈstreɪʃən/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>抗议，规劝</td><td>despite his remonstration<br>尽管他提出抗议</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Their remonstration against the new tax filled three long letters.  他们反对新税的抗议写满了三封长信。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 stink</td><td>/ˈstɪŋk/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>名词、动词</td><td>大声抗议，抱怨；臭味</td><td>raise a stink about it<br>为此大吵大闹抗议</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Parents raised a stink about the sudden tuition increase.  家长们为学费突然上涨大闹抗议。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 acceptance</td><td>/ækˈseptəns/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★☆</td><td>名词</td><td>接受，认可</td><td>quiet acceptance of fate<br>对命运的平静接受</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 His calm acceptance of the verdict surprised everyone in court.  他平静地接受判决，令法庭上所有人惊讶。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 acquiescence</td><td>/ˌækwiˈesəns/</td><td>★</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>默许，顺从</td><td>silent acquiescence to authority<br>对权威的默默顺从</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Her silent acquiescence surprised colleagues who expected a fierce argument.  她默默顺从让预料会激烈争论的同事很惊讶。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 agreement</td><td>/əˈɡriːmənt/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>名词</td><td>一致，同意；协议</td><td>come to an agreement<br>达成协议</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The two firms signed an agreement to share research costs.  两家公司签署协议分担研究费用。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 approval</td><td>/əˈpruːvəl/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>名词</td><td>批准，认可；赞成</td><td>seek her approval<br>征求她的同意</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The plan needs approval from the board before work begins.  该计划开工前需要董事会批准。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 assent</td><td>/əˈsent/</td><td>★★</td><td>★</td><td>不及物动词、名词</td><td>同意，赞同</td><td>assent to the proposal<br>赞同该提议</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The committee assented to the proposal without further debate.  委员会未经进一步辩论就同意了该提案。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 sanction</td><td>/ˈsæŋkʃn/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>批准，同意，认可</td><td>sanction the new policy<br>批准这项新政策</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The board refused to sanction the plan without a detailed budget.  董事会拒绝在没有详细预算的情况下批准该计划。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>remorse</b></td><td>/rɪˈmɔːrs/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>名词</td><td>懊悔，悔恨</td><td>show no remorse<br>毫无悔意</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The defendant showed no remorse for the harm he had caused.  被告对自己造成的伤害毫无悔意。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳近 contriteness</del></td><td>/kənˈtraɪtnəs/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>悔悟，痛悔</td><td>genuine contriteness<br>真诚的悔悟</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The letter's contriteness persuaded the judge to reduce his sentence.  信中的悔意说服法官减轻了他的刑罚。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 contrition</td><td>/kənˈtrɪʃən/</td><td>★☆</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>痛悔，悔罪</td><td>an act of contrition<br>一次悔罪之举</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He spoke with such contrition that his friends forgave him.  他说话时那样痛悔，朋友们都原谅了他。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 penitence</td><td>/ˈpɛnɪtəns/</td><td>★</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>悔悟，忏悔</td><td>a sign of penitence<br>悔悟的表现</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Years of quiet penitence followed that one reckless night.  那一个鲁莽的夜晚之后是多年的默默忏悔。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 regret</td><td>/rəˈɡret/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>动词、名词</td><td>后悔，遗憾；懊悔</td><td>regret his decision<br>后悔他的决定</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She regretted not saying goodbye before he left the country.  她后悔在他出国前没有道别。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳近 remorsefulness</del></td><td>/rɪˈmɔrsfəlnəs/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>悔恨，懊悔</td><td>deep remorsefulness<br>深深的悔恨</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Her remorsefulness was obvious in every line of the apology.  她的悔恨在道歉信的每一行里都显而易见。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 repentance</td><td>/rɪˈpentəns/</td><td>★★</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>悔悟，忏悔</td><td>sincere repentance<br>真诚的悔悟</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The parole board looked for evidence of genuine repentance.  假释委员会在寻找真正悔悟的证据。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 rue</td><td>/ruː/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>名词</td><td>后悔，遗憾</td><td>to his rue<br>令他后悔的是</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 To his lasting rue, he sold the painting for almost nothing.  令他长久后悔的是，他几乎白送掉了那幅画。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 self-reproach</td><td>/ˌsɛlf rɪˈproʊtʃ/</td><td>★☆</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>自责，内疚</td><td>a pang of self-reproach<br>一阵自责</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Years of self-reproach followed the accident he could not prevent.  那场他无法阻止的事故之后是多年的自责。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 shame</td><td>/ˈʃeɪm/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>及物动词、名词</td><td>使羞愧，羞辱；羞耻</td><td>shame him into silence<br>让他羞愧得沉默</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The teacher never tried to shame students in front of the class.  这位老师从不在全班面前羞辱学生。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳反 impenitence</del></td><td>/ɪmˈpɛnɪtəns/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>不悔悟，顽固不改</td><td>stubborn impenitence<br>顽固的不悔悟</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The court noted his impenitence when deciding the sentence.  法庭在量刑时注意到他毫无悔意。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳反 remorselessness</del></td><td>/rɪˈmɔrsləsnəs/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>无悔意，冷酷无情</td><td>chilling remorselessness<br>令人心寒的冷酷无情</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Witnesses were shaken by the remorselessness in his voice.  证人们被他语气中的冷酷无情惊住了。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>remunerate</b></td><td>/rɪˈmjuːnəreɪt/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>支付报酬，补偿</td><td>remunerate him generously<br>给他优厚的报酬</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The company promised to remunerate him generously for the extra work.  公司承诺为这些额外工作给他丰厚的报酬。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 compensate</td><td>/ˈkɑːmpənˌseɪt/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★</td><td>动词</td><td>补偿，弥补；赔偿</td><td>compensate for the loss<br>弥补损失</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Extra practice can compensate for a lack of natural talent.  额外的练习能弥补天赋的不足。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 indemnify</td><td>/ˌɪnˈdemnəfaɪ/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>赔偿，补偿；保障</td><td>indemnify the owner<br>赔偿所有人</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The contract requires the builder to indemnify us for any damage.  合同要求建筑方赔偿我们的任何损失。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 recompense</td><td>/ˈrekəmˌpens/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>名词、及物动词</td><td>补偿，报酬</td><td>recompense for the loss<br>对损失的补偿</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She was given a small sum as recompense for her trouble.  她得到一小笔钱作为麻烦的补偿。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 recoup</td><td>/rɪˈkuːp/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>动词</td><td>恢复，复原；补偿</td><td>recoup his strength<br>恢复体力</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 After the long march the soldiers rested to recoup their strength.  长途行军后，士兵们休息以恢复体力。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 redress</td><td>/rɪˈdres/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>名词、及物动词</td><td>赔偿，纠正</td><td>seek legal redress<br>寻求法律赔偿</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Victims went to court to seek redress for their losses.  受害者诉诸法庭为损失寻求赔偿。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 remedy</td><td>/ˈremədi/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>动词、名词</td><td>补救，纠正；补救办法</td><td>remedy the defect<br>纠正这个缺陷</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Nothing can remedy the harm done by that careless remark.  那句不慎的话造成的伤害无可弥补。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 repay</td><td>/riˈpeɪ/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>偿还；报答</td><td>repay a favor<br>报答一份恩情</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 I can never repay the patience my mentor showed me.  导师给我的耐心，我永远无法回报。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 requite</td><td>/rɪˈkwaɪt/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>酬谢，报答；报仇</td><td>requite his loyalty<br>报答他的忠诚</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He wanted to requite her kindness with a gift she would truly treasure.  他想用一件她会真正珍视的礼物报答她的善意。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>rend</b></td><td>/rend/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>撕裂，猛拉</td><td>rend the cloth in two<br>把布撕成两半</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The angry protester tried to rend the banner into two ragged halves.  那名愤怒的抗议者试图把横幅撕成破烂的两半。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 cleave</td><td>/kliːv/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>劈开，割裂；紧贴，坚持</td><td>cleave the log in two<br>把木头劈成两半</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 One blow of the axe cleaved the log in two.  斧头一击就把木头劈成了两半。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 lacerate</td><td>/ˈlæsəreɪt/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>使非常痛苦</td><td>lacerate someone's feelings<br>深深伤害某人感情</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 His careless remarks lacerated her feelings more deeply than he realized.  他随口的话比他意识到的更深地伤害了她的感情。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 ribbon</td><td>/ˈrɪbən/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>名词、动词</td><td>撕成条；带子，丝带</td><td>torn to ribbons<br>被撕成碎条</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The flag hung in ribbons after weeks of storms.  经过数周风暴，旗子已被撕成条条挂着。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 rip</td><td>/ˈrɪp/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>动词、名词</td><td>撕开，猛地扯开；裂口</td><td>rip open the envelope<br>撕开信封</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He ripped the letter open before he even sat down.  他还没坐下就把信撕开了。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 rive</td><td>/raɪv/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>动词</td><td>撕开</td><td>rive the log apart<br>把木头劈开</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Lightning can rive a tall oak straight down the middle of its trunk.  闪电能把高大的橡树从树干正中劈开。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 rupture</td><td>/ˈrʌptʃər/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>动词</td><td>打破；打碎，破裂</td><td>rupture a blood vessel<br>使血管破裂</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The old pipe could rupture at any moment under such high pressure.  这根旧管道随时可能在如此高的压力下破裂。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 shred</td><td>/ʃred/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>名词</td><td>碎片，细条；少量</td><td>not a shred of evidence<br>没有一丝证据</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 There is not a shred of evidence linking him to the theft.  没有一丝证据能把他和这起盗窃联系起来。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 split</td><td>/ˈsplɪt/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>动词、名词</td><td>分裂，分开</td><td>split into groups<br>分成小组</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The committee split into two camps over the proposal.  委员会因该提案分裂成两派。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳近 tatter</del></td><td>/ˈtætər/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>名词、动词</td><td>破布条，碎片；撕碎</td><td>in tatters<br>破烂不堪；彻底破灭</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 His reputation lay in tatters after the investigation.  调查之后他的名誉已经一败涂地。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 tear</td><td>/ˈter/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>动词、名词</td><td>撕开，扯破；眼泪</td><td>tear the paper in half<br>把纸撕成两半</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She tore the photograph in half and threw it away.  她把照片撕成两半扔掉了。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 associate</td><td>/əˈsoʊʃieɪt/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★</td><td>动词</td><td>联合，结交；联想</td><td>associate with the group<br>与该团体结交</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He refused to associate himself with such a risky plan.  他拒绝与这样冒险的计划有牵连。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 coalesce</td><td>/ˌkoʊəˈles/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★</td><td>动词</td><td>合并，融合</td><td>coalesce into a group<br>合并成一个团体</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The scattered protests coalesced into a national movement.  分散的抗议汇合成一场全国性运动。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 combine</td><td>/kəmˈbaɪn/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>动词</td><td>结合，联合</td><td>combine two elements<br>结合两种元素</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The recipe combines sweet and sour flavors very successfully.  这道菜谱把甜味和酸味结合得很成功。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 conjoin</td><td>/kənˈdʒɔɪn/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>动词</td><td>结合，连接</td><td>conjoin the two parts<br>把两部分接合</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The bridge conjoins the island and the mainland neatly.  这座桥把小岛与大陆巧妙相连。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 conjugate</td><td>/ˈkɑːndʒəɡeɪt/</td><td>★☆</td><td>☆</td><td>动词、形容词</td><td>结合，配对；成对的</td><td>conjugate two molecules<br>使两个分子结合</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Researchers conjugate the drug to an antibody for precise delivery.  研究者把药物与抗体结合以实现精准投递。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 connect</td><td>/kəˈnekt/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>动词</td><td>连接，联结</td><td>connect the two wires<br>连接两根导线</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 A narrow tunnel connects the two halves of the building.  一条窄隧道把大楼的两半连起来。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 fuse</td><td>/ˈfjuːz/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★★</td><td>动词</td><td>融合，熔合</td><td>fuse two traditions<br>融合两种传统</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Her music fuses jazz rhythms with traditional folk melodies.  她的音乐把爵士节奏与传统民谣旋律融为一体。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳反 interfuse</del></td><td>/ˌɪntərˈfjuz/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>动词</td><td>使混合，交融</td><td>interfuse the two styles<br>交融两种风格</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The composer interfuses folk melodies with modern harmony.  作曲家把民间旋律与现代和声交融在一起。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 join</td><td>/ˈdʒɔɪn/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>动词</td><td>连接，接合；加入</td><td>join the two pieces<br>把两块接起来</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Join the ends of the rope with a firm knot.  用牢固的结把绳子两端接起来。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 link</td><td>/ˈlɪŋk/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>名词、动词</td><td>联系，纽带；连接</td><td>a close link<br>紧密的联系</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Researchers found a close link between sleep and memory.  研究者发现睡眠与记忆之间存在紧密联系。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 unify</td><td>/ˈjuːnəˌfaɪ/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>统一，使成一体</td><td>unify the country<br>统一国家</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 A common language helped unify the scattered tribes into one nation.  共同的语言帮助把分散的部落统一为一个民族。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>renegade</b></td><td>/ˈrenɪɡeɪd/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>名词</td><td>背教者，叛徒</td><td>a renegade from the church<br>背教出走者</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Once a devoted monk, he became a renegade and denounced the church openly.  他曾是虔诚的修士，后来成了背教者并公开谴责教会。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 apostate</td><td>/əˈpɑːsteɪt/</td><td>★☆</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>叛教者，背弃信念者</td><td>denounced as an apostate<br>被斥为叛教者</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Former comrades denounced him as an apostate and a liar.  昔日同志斥他为叛徒和骗子。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 betrayer</td><td>/bɪˈtreɪər/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>名词</td><td>背叛者，叛徒</td><td>a betrayer of trust<br>背弃信任的人</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 History remembers him mainly as the betrayer of his own city.  历史主要记住他是自己城市的背叛者。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 defector</td><td>/dɪˈfektər/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>名词</td><td>背叛者，投敌者</td><td>a high-ranking defector<br>一名高级投诚者</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The defector brought documents that embarrassed his former government.  这名投诚者带来了让其前政府难堪的文件。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 deserter</td><td>/dɪˈzɜːrtər/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>名词</td><td>逃兵，背弃者</td><td>shoot the deserters<br>枪决逃兵</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Deserters were hunted down and tried before military courts.  逃兵被追捕并交军事法庭审判。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳近 recreant</del></td><td>/ˈrɛkriənt/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>名词、形容词</td><td>背叛者，怯懦的叛徒</td><td>branded a recreant<br>被打上叛徒烙印</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The ballad calls him a recreant who fled the battle.  这首民谣称他是逃离战场的怯懦叛徒。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 traitor</td><td>/ˈtreɪtər/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>名词</td><td>叛徒，卖国者</td><td>hanged as a traitor<br>作为叛徒被处死</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 History records him as a traitor to his own city.  历史把他记为背叛自己城市的叛徒。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 turncoat</td><td>/ˈtɜːrnkoʊt/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>名词</td><td>叛徒</td><td>a political turncoat<br>政治上的变节者</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Old comrades denounced him as a turncoat after he joined the rival party.  他加入敌对政党后，老战友们谴责他是叛徒。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 adherent</td><td>/ədˈhɪrənt/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>名词、形容词</td><td>追随者，拥护者</td><td>a devoted adherent<br>忠实的拥护者</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The movement attracted thousands of adherents in a single year.  这场运动一年内就吸引了数千名拥护者。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 loyalist</td><td>/ˈlɔɪəlɪst/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>名词</td><td>忠诚者，效忠者</td><td>a party loyalist<br>党内忠诚分子</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Only a few loyalists stayed with the leader after the split.  分裂后只有少数忠诚者仍跟随领袖。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 partisan</td><td>/ˈpɑːrtəzn/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>名词、形容词</td><td>党派偏见的；盲目拥护者</td><td>partisan politics<br>党派偏见的政治</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The debate quickly sank into partisan shouting about very old grievances.  辩论很快沦为带党派偏见的叫嚷，纠缠陈年旧怨。</td></tr>
</tbody>
</table>

## Unit 4

<table>
<thead><tr>
<th>单词</th>
<th>音标</th>
<th>常用</th>
<th>口语</th>
<th>词性</th>
<th>中文释义</th>
<th>常用短语</th>
</tr></thead>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>renounce</b></td><td>/rɪˈnaʊns/</td><td>★★</td><td>★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>正式放弃，声明弃绝</td><td>renounce one's claim<br>声明放弃权利主张</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He renounced his claim to the inheritance in writing.  他以书面形式放弃了继承权。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳近 abnegate</del></td><td>/ˈæbnɪɡeɪt/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>动词</td><td>放弃，否认</td><td>abnegate one's rights<br>放弃自己的权利</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The monk chose to abnegate all personal comforts.  这位僧人选择放弃一切个人享受。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 cede</td><td>/siːd/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>放弃，割让</td><td>cede territory<br>割让领土</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The defeated nation was forced to cede two provinces.  战败国被迫割让两个省。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 relinquish</td><td>/rɪˈlɪŋkwɪʃ/</td><td>★★</td><td>★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>放弃（权力、职位）</td><td>relinquish control<br>放弃控制权</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She refused to relinquish control of the family business.  她拒绝放弃对家族企业的控制权。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 resign</td><td>/rɪˈzaɪn/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>动词</td><td>辞职，放弃（职位）</td><td>resign from office<br>辞去公职</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The minister resigned from office after the scandal broke.  丑闻爆发后，那位部长辞职了。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 surrender</td><td>/səˈrendər/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>动词</td><td>交出，放弃，投降</td><td>surrender to the police<br>向警方投降</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The thief finally surrendered to the police after a long chase.  经过长时间追捕，小偷终于向警方投降。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 inaugurate</td><td>/ɪˈnɔːɡjəreɪt/</td><td>★☆</td><td>☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>开始</td><td>inaugurate a new era<br>开创一个新时代</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The treaty inaugurated a long period of peace between the two nations.  这项条约开启了两国之间长期的和平。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>renovate</b></td><td>/ˈrenəveɪt/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>修复，维修</td><td>renovate the old house<br>翻修老房子</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 We must renovate the old house completely before moving in.  搬进去之前我们必须彻底翻修这栋老房子。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 doctor</td><td>/ˈdɑːktər/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★★★★</td><td>及物动词、名词</td><td>篡改，窜改；医生</td><td>doctor the accounts<br>篡改账目</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He was accused of doctoring the financial records last year.  他被指控篡改了去年的财务记录。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 fix</td><td>/ˈfɪks/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>及物动词、名词</td><td>修理，解决；固定</td><td>fix the leaking pipe<br>修好漏水的管子</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 A neighbor fixed the leaking pipe before the floor was ruined.  邻居在地板毁坏前修好了漏水的管子。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 patch</td><td>/ˈpætʃ/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>名词</td><td>斑块，小片；补丁</td><td>a damp patch<br>一块潮湿的斑迹</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 There was a damp patch on the ceiling above the door.  门上方的天花板上有一块潮湿的斑迹。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 recondition</td><td>/ˌriːkənˈdɪʃən/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>修复，翻新</td><td>recondition the engine<br>翻修发动机</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 They recondition old bicycles and sell them at low prices.  他们翻新旧自行车并低价出售。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 repair</td><td>/rɪˈper/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>及物动词、名词</td><td>修理，修补</td><td>repair the roof<br>修补屋顶</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Workers repaired the roof just before the rainy season began.  工人们在雨季开始前修好了屋顶。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 revamp</td><td>/riˈvæmp/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>及物动词、名词</td><td>改进，翻新，改造</td><td>revamp the website<br>改版网站</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The company revamped its website to attract younger overseas customers.  公司改版网站以吸引更年轻的海外顾客。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 blemish</td><td>/ˈblemɪʃ/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>名词、及物动词</td><td>缺点，污点；损害，玷污</td><td>a blemish on his record<br>他履历上的污点</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 One late payment left a small blemish on his credit record.  一次逾期付款在他的信用记录上留下了小小的污点。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 break</td><td>/ˈbreɪk/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>动词、名词</td><td>损坏，打破；间歇</td><td>break the vase<br>打破花瓶</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The cat broke the vase while chasing a moth indoors.  猫在屋里追蛾子时打破了花瓶。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 damage</td><td>/ˈdæmədʒ/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>及物动词、名词</td><td>损害，破坏；损失</td><td>damage the crops<br>损害庄稼</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Late frost damaged the crops across the entire river valley.  晚霜损害了整个河谷的庄稼。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 deface</td><td>/dɪˈfeɪs/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>损毁外观，涂污</td><td>deface a monument<br>损毁纪念碑</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Someone defaced the statue with spray paint during the night.  有人夜里用喷漆涂污了那座雕像。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 disfigure</td><td>/dɪsˈfɪɡjər/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>损毁外形，使难看</td><td>disfigure the facade<br>损毁建筑正面</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Cheap repairs disfigured the elegant facade of the old theater.  廉价的修补损毁了老剧院优雅的正面。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 harm</td><td>/ˈhɑːrm/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>及物动词、名词</td><td>伤害，损害</td><td>harm the environment<br>损害环境</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Pesticide runoff harms the fish living in the nearby stream.  农药径流危害了附近溪流中的鱼。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 hurt</td><td>/ˈhɜːrt/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>动词、名词</td><td>伤害，使疼痛</td><td>hurt his feelings<br>伤害他的感情</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Your careless comment clearly hurt her feelings at the dinner.  你随口的评论显然在晚餐上伤了她的感情。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 impair</td><td>/ɪmˈper/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>损害，削弱</td><td>impair his judgment<br>削弱他的判断力</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Lack of sleep seriously impairs judgment and slows your reactions.  睡眠不足严重损害判断力并减慢反应。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 injure</td><td>/ˈɪndʒər/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>使受伤，伤害</td><td>injure his knee<br>伤到他的膝盖</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He injured his knee while running downhill on wet stones.  他在湿滑石头上跑下坡时伤了膝盖。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 mar</td><td>/mɑːr/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>破坏，削弱</td><td>mar the celebration<br>破坏庆典气氛</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 A careless remark at dinner managed to mar an otherwise perfect evening.  餐桌上一句轻率的话毁掉了本该完美的一晚。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 ruin</td><td>/ˈruːɪn/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>及物动词、名词</td><td>毁坏，毁灭；废墟</td><td>ruin the harvest<br>毁掉收成</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Weeks of drought ruined the harvest and emptied the village granary.  数周干旱毁掉收成，村里粮仓也空了。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 vandalize</td><td>/ˈvændəˌlaɪz/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>破坏，损毁（公物）</td><td>vandalize the monument<br>破坏这座纪念碑</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Someone vandalized the bus shelter again over the weekend.  周末又有人破坏了公交候车亭。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 wreck</td><td>/ˈrek/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>名词、及物动词</td><td>残骸；失事；破坏</td><td>the wreck of a ship<br>一艘船的残骸</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Divers photographed the wreck lying deep on the sandy bottom.  潜水员拍摄了深卧沙底的残骸。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>repartee</b></td><td>/ˌrepɑːrˈtiː/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>名词</td><td>机智的应答，妙语</td><td>quick repartee<br>机敏的应答</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Their dinner conversation sparkled with witty repartee that kept every guest laughing.  他们的餐桌交谈妙语连珠，逗得每位客人都在笑。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳近 backchat</del></td><td>/ˈbæktʃæt/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>顶嘴；打趣的对答</td><td>cheeky backchat<br>无礼的顶嘴</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The teacher tolerated no backchat from students during her lessons.  这位老师上课时不容学生顶嘴。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 badinage</td><td>/ˌbædənˈɑːʒ/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>打趣，善意的玩笑</td><td>light badinage<br>轻松的打趣</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Their light badinage over dinner kept the whole table laughing.  他们晚餐时轻松的打趣让整桌人笑声不断。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 chaff</td><td>/tʃæf/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>动词、名词</td><td>取笑，善意地嘲弄</td><td>chaff a friend<br>取笑朋友</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 His teammates chaffed him about his terrible singing voice.  队友们拿他糟糕的歌喉取笑他。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 jesting</td><td>/ˈdʒɛstɪŋ/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>名词、形容词</td><td>说笑，打趣</td><td>good-humored jesting<br>善意的玩笑</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Their good-humored jesting made the long trip pass quickly.  他们善意的说笑让漫长的旅程过得很快。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 Joshing</td><td>/ˈdʒɑʃɪŋ/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>名词、动词</td><td>打趣，开玩笑</td><td>friendly joshing<br>友好的打趣</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The brothers kept up a friendly joshing all through dinner.  兄弟俩整顿晚餐都在友好地互相打趣。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳近 persiflage</del></td><td>/ˈpɜːrsɪflɑːʒ/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>开玩笑</td><td>idle persiflage<br>无聊的玩笑</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The dinner party was full of idle persiflage and very little substance.  晚宴上尽是无聊的玩笑，几乎没有实质内容。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳近 raillery</del></td><td>/ˈreɪləri/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>善意的调侃，玩笑</td><td>gentle raillery<br>温和的调侃</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He endured her gentle raillery with a patient smile.  他带着耐心的微笑忍受她温和的调侃。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>repatriate</b></td><td>/ˌriːˈpeɪtrieɪt/</td><td>★</td><td>☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>遣返</td><td>repatriate the refugees<br>遣返难民</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The government agreed to repatriate the stranded workers before the holiday season.  政府同意在假期前将滞留的工人遣返回国。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 banish</td><td>/ˈbænɪʃ/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>驱逐出境</td><td>banish him from court<br>将他逐出宫廷</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The king decided to banish the poet from the court forever.  国王决定把这位诗人永远逐出宫廷。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 deport</td><td>/dɪˈpɔːrt/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>驱逐出境，遣送</td><td>deport illegal immigrants<br>驱逐非法移民</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The government threatened to deport anyone without valid papers.  政府威胁要驱逐任何没有有效证件的人。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 expatriate</td><td>/ekˈspeɪtriət/</td><td>★☆</td><td>☆</td><td>名词、及物动词</td><td>移居国外者；驱逐出国</td><td>an American expatriate<br>一位美国侨民</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Many expatriates gather at that café to speak their own language.  许多侨民聚在那家咖啡馆说母语。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>repeal</b></td><td>/rɪˈpiːl/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>撤销，废除（法律等）</td><td>repeal the old law<br>废除旧法律</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Lawmakers voted to repeal the outdated statute that had confused courts for decades.  议员们投票废除了这部困扰法院数十年的过时法规。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 abort</td><td>/əˈbɔːrt/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>动词</td><td>中止，取消；流产</td><td>abort the mission<br>中止任务</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Bad weather forced the crew to abort the launch.  恶劣天气迫使工作人员中止发射。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 abrogate</td><td>/ˈæbrəɡeɪt/</td><td>★</td><td>☆</td><td>动词</td><td>正式废除（法律、条约）</td><td>abrogate a treaty<br>废除条约</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Parliament voted to abrogate the outdated law.  议会投票废除这项过时的法律。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 annul</td><td>/əˈnʌl/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>宣告无效，取消</td><td>annul the marriage<br>宣告婚姻无效</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The court agreed to annul the marriage after only three weeks.  法院同意在仅仅三周之后宣告这段婚姻无效。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 cancel</td><td>/ˈkænsəl/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>取消，撤销；抵消</td><td>cancel the meeting<br>取消会议</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 They canceled the outdoor concert because of the heavy rain.  因为大雨，他们取消了露天音乐会。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳近 disannul</del></td><td>/ˌdɪsəˈnʌl/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>废除，取消</td><td>disannul a contract<br>废除合同</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The court moved to disannul the fraudulent agreement.  法院着手废除这份欺诈协议。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 invalidate</td><td>/ɪnˈvælɪdeɪt/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>使无效，使作废</td><td>invalidate a contract<br>使合同无效</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 A missing signature will invalidate the whole form.  缺少签名会使整张表格失效。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 negate</td><td>/nɪˈɡeɪt/</td><td>★★</td><td>☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>否认；否定，取消</td><td>negate the effect<br>抵消效果</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 One careless error can negate weeks of careful work.  一个粗心的错误能抹掉数周的细致工作。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 null</td><td>/ˈnʌl/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>无效的；等于零的</td><td>null and void<br>无效的，作废的</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The court declared the agreement null and void.  法院宣布该协议无效。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 nullify</td><td>/ˈnʌləfaɪ/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>使无效，废除</td><td>nullify an agreement<br>使协议失效</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The judge nullified the election result.  法官宣布选举结果无效。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 rescind</td><td>/rɪˈsɪnd/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>废除，取消</td><td>rescind an offer<br>撤回报价</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The company rescinded the job offer without explanation.  公司未加解释便撤回了聘用通知。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 revoke</td><td>/rɪˈvoʊk/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>撤销，废除；吊销</td><td>revoke a license<br>吊销执照</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The court revoked his driving license after the second offense.  法院在他第二次违法后吊销了他的驾照。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 vacate</td><td>/ˈveɪkeɪt/</td><td>★★</td><td>☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>腾空，搬出；辞去</td><td>vacate the premises<br>腾出房屋</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Tenants must vacate the apartment before noon on the last day.  租户必须在最后一天中午前搬出公寓。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 void</td><td>/ˈvɔɪd/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>及物动词、形容词、名词</td><td>使无效；排空；空虚</td><td>void the contract<br>使合同无效</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Missing a single payment can void the entire warranty agreement.  漏付一期就可能使整个保修协议失效。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 continue</td><td>/kənˈtɪnjuː/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>动词</td><td>继续，持续</td><td>continue without pause<br>不停地持续</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The negotiations continued for eleven hours without a proper break.  谈判持续了十一小时没有正式休息。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 keep</td><td>/ˈkiːp/</td><td>★★★★☆</td><td>★★★★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>遵守，坚持；保留</td><td>keep a promise<br>守约，履行承诺</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He always keeps his promises, however inconvenient they are.  无论多不方便，他总是守约。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>repel</b></td><td>/rɪˈpel/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>击退，驱退；使厌恶</td><td>repel the attackers<br>击退进攻者</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The greasy smell of the kitchen seemed to repel every customer who entered.  厨房油腻的气味似乎让每个进来的顾客都感到厌恶。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 disgust</td><td>/dɪsˈɡʌst/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>及物动词、名词</td><td>使厌恶；厌恶</td><td>disgust even his allies<br>连他的盟友也感到厌恶</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The state of the kitchen disgusted the health inspector.  厨房的状况让卫生检查员十分厌恶。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳近 nauseate</del></td><td>/ˈnɔːzieɪt/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>动词</td><td>使恶心；使厌恶</td><td>nauseate the audience<br>使观众反感</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The graphic descriptions in the film nauseated much of the audience.  影片中露骨的描写让大部分观众感到恶心。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 repulse</td><td>/rɪˈpʌls/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>使厌恶，排斥</td><td>repulsed by the smell<br>被那气味恶心到</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The sight of the rotting fruit repulsed everyone standing near the crate.  腐烂水果的样子让站在箱子旁的每个人都感到恶心。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 revolt</td><td>/rɪˈvoʊlt/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>使厌恶，使反感</td><td>revolted by the smell<br>被气味恶心到</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The smell of the spoiled milk revolted everyone in the small kitchen.  变质牛奶的气味让狭小厨房里的每个人都反胃。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 sicken</td><td>/ˈsɪkən/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>动词</td><td>生病，患病；使厌恶</td><td>sicken and die<br>患病死去</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Many of the sheep sickened after drinking from the polluted stream.  许多羊喝了污染的溪水后患病。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 allure</td><td>/əˈlʊr/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>及物动词、名词</td><td>吸引，诱惑</td><td>the allure of fame<br>名声的诱惑</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Cheap rents allure young artists to this run-down district.  低廉的租金吸引年轻艺术家来到这个破旧街区。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 attract</td><td>/əˈtrækt/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>吸引，引起</td><td>attract attention<br>吸引注意</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The exhibition attracted thousands of visitors in its first week.  展览首周吸引了数千名参观者。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 bewitch</td><td>/bɪˈwɪtʃ/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>使着迷；用魔法迷住</td><td>bewitch the audience<br>让观众为之着迷</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Her singing bewitched everyone in the crowded little hall.  她的歌声让拥挤小厅里的每个人都着了迷。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 captivate</td><td>/ˈkæptɪveɪt/</td><td>★</td><td>★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>使着迷，深深吸引</td><td>captivate the audience<br>深深吸引观众</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Her first novel captivated readers around the world.  她的第一部小说令世界各地的读者着迷。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 charm</td><td>/ˈtʃɑːrm/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>及物动词、名词</td><td>迷住，吸引；魅力</td><td>charm the audience<br>迷住观众</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The young pianist charmed the audience with her first piece.  这位年轻钢琴家用第一首曲子迷住了观众。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 enchant</td><td>/ɪnˈtʃænt/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>使入迷，使陶醉</td><td>be enchanted by the scenery<br>被风景迷住</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The old fishing village enchanted every visitor who stayed there.  那个古老渔村令每位住过的游客陶醉。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 entice</td><td>/ɪnˈtaɪs/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>诱使，引诱</td><td>entice customers with discounts<br>用折扣吸引顾客</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Free samples were used to entice shoppers into the new store.  免费样品被用来引诱购物者进入新店。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 fascinate</td><td>/ˈfæsɪneɪt/</td><td>★</td><td>★★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>强烈吸引，使入迷</td><td>fascinate young readers<br>使小读者着迷</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Stories about strange deep-sea creatures fascinate young readers all over the world.  关于奇异深海生物的故事让全世界的小读者着迷。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 lure</td><td>/ˈlʊr/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>动词、名词</td><td>吸引，诱惑；诱饵</td><td>lure customers in<br>把顾客吸引进来</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Low prices lured shoppers away from the older stores.  低价把购物者从老店吸引走了。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 seduce</td><td>/sɪˈduːs/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>诱使（背离正道），引诱</td><td>seduce someone into crime<br>引诱某人犯罪</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 High salaries seduced many teachers away from the classroom.  高薪诱使许多教师离开了课堂。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 tempt</td><td>/ˈtempt/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>诱惑，引诱</td><td>tempt fate<br>冒险行事，玩火</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The smell of fresh bread tempted us into the little bakery.  新鲜面包的香味把我们诱进了那家小面包店。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>repertoire</b></td><td>/ˈrepərtwɑːr/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>名词</td><td>全部剧目；全部技能</td><td>a wide repertoire<br>丰富的曲目或本领</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The young pianist has a wide repertoire of romantic pieces.  这位年轻钢琴家有大量的浪漫派曲目。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 budget</td><td>/ˈbʌdʒɪt/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>动词、名词</td><td>编制预算，谋划；预算</td><td>budget for the trip<br>为旅行做预算</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 We budgeted carefully for the trip and still ran short.  我们为旅行仔细做了预算，钱还是不够。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 fund</td><td>/ˈfʌnd/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>名词、动词</td><td>储备，积累；基金</td><td>a fund of anecdotes<br>一肚子的轶事</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The old sailor had an endless fund of sea stories.  这位老水手有说不完的海上故事。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 inventory</td><td>/ˈɪnvəntɔːri/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★☆</td><td>名词</td><td>详细清单，清点</td><td>take inventory of skills<br>盘点自身技能</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The report offers a valuable inventory of everything known about this virus.  这份报告对目前关于这种病毒的全部认识做了有价值的清点。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 pool</td><td>/ˈpuːl/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>名词、动词</td><td>储备，共用资源；水池</td><td>a pool of talent<br>一批人才储备</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The city draws on a large pool of volunteer translators.  这座城市拥有大批志愿译员可供调用。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 reservoir</td><td>/ˈrezərvwɑːr/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>名词</td><td>储备，蕴藏；水库</td><td>a reservoir of goodwill<br>一份善意的储备</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She drew on a deep reservoir of patience that afternoon.  那个下午她动用了自己深厚的耐心储备。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 stock</td><td>/stɑːk/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>普通的，常备的</td><td>a stock phrase<br>一句套话</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The politician answered every question with the same stock phrase.  这位政客用同一句套话回答每个问题。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del><b>repine</b></del></td><td>/rɪˈpaɪn/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>不及物动词</td><td>抱怨，表达不满；渴望</td><td>repine at one's fate<br>抱怨自己的命运</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Rather than repine at his losses, he quietly started the business again.  他没有抱怨自己的损失，而是默默重新开始了生意。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 carp</td><td>/kɑːrp/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>不及物动词</td><td>对小事吹毛求疵，挑剔，表现不满</td><td>carp at every detail<br>对每个细节都挑剔</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She carped at every detail of the seating arrangement.  她对座位安排的每个细节都挑剔。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 complain</td><td>/kəmˈpleɪn/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>动词</td><td>抱怨，投诉</td><td>complain about the noise<br>抱怨噪音</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Guests complained about the cold water all through the week.  整周里客人都在抱怨水太冷。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 fuss</td><td>/fʌs/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>名词、不及物动词</td><td>小题大做，抱怨；忙乱</td><td>make a fuss about<br>为…大惊小怪</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Don't make a fuss about a tiny stain on the tablecloth.  别为桌布上一小块污渍小题大做。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 gripe</td><td>/ɡraɪp/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>不及物动词</td><td>抱怨</td><td>gripe about the food<br>抱怨饭菜</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The passengers griped loudly about the delay for nearly a whole hour.  乘客们大声抱怨延误，抱怨了将近一个小时。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 grouch</td><td>/ˈɡraʊtʃ/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>不及物动词、名词</td><td>抱怨；牢骚满腹的人</td><td>grouch about the rules<br>抱怨规定</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He grouched about the new rules but followed them anyway.  他抱怨新规定，但还是照做了。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 grouse</td><td>/ˈɡraʊs/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>不及物动词、名词</td><td>抱怨，发牢骚</td><td>grouse about long hours<br>抱怨工时太长</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The crew groused about long hours during the harvest season.  收割季里工人们抱怨工时太长。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 growl</td><td>/ˈɡraʊl/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>动词、名词</td><td>抱怨着说；咆哮</td><td>growl a complaint<br>低声咕哝着抱怨</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He growled something about the traffic and slammed the door.  他咕哝了几句路上堵车，然后重重关上门。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 grumble</td><td>/ˈɡrʌmbl/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>不及物动词</td><td>（尤指低声地）抱怨，埋怨</td><td>grumble under one's breath<br>低声抱怨</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The workers grumbled about the new rules but followed them anyway.  工人们低声抱怨新规定，但还是照办了。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳近 inveigh</del></td><td>/ɪnˈveɪ/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>不及物动词</td><td>猛烈抨击，痛斥</td><td>inveigh against the new tax<br>激烈抗议新税</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Farmers gathered downtown to inveigh against the new fuel tax.  农民们聚集在市中心痛斥新的燃油税。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 moan</td><td>/ˈmoʊn/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>动词、名词</td><td>哀叹，抱怨</td><td>moan about the weather<br>抱怨天气</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 They moaned about the heat for the entire drive.  整段车程他们都在抱怨炎热。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 murmur</td><td>/ˈmɜːrmər/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>不及物动词</td><td>低声抱怨，发牢骚</td><td>murmur a complaint<br>低声抱怨一句</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The workers murmured about the new rules but nobody complained openly.  工人们对新规定低声抱怨，却没人公开抗议。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 mutter</td><td>/ˈmʌtər/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>不及物动词</td><td>低声抱怨</td><td>mutter under one's breath<br>低声嘟囔</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He muttered under his breath as he carried the heavy boxes upstairs.  他把沉重的箱子搬上楼时低声嘟囔着。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 wail</td><td>/ˈweɪl/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>动词、名词</td><td>痛哭，哀号；抱怨</td><td>wail in grief<br>悲痛地哀号</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The mourners wailed as the coffin was lowered into the grave.  棺木下葬时，吊唁者们放声哀号。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 whine</td><td>/ˈwaɪn/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>动词、名词</td><td>哀鸣，抱怨</td><td>whine about the weather<br>抱怨天气</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The children whined about the long walk to the station.  孩子们抱怨去车站要走那么远。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 ache</td><td>/ˈeɪk/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>动词、名词</td><td>渴望；疼痛</td><td>ache for home<br>渴望回家</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 After three years abroad she ached for her hometown.  在国外三年后，她十分渴望回到故乡。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 covet</td><td>/ˈkʌvət/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>贪求，渴望得到</td><td>covet his neighbor's land<br>贪图邻居的土地</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Every young pianist in the country covets that scholarship.  国内每个年轻钢琴家都渴望得到那份奖学金。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 crave</td><td>/kreɪv/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>热望</td><td>crave attention<br>渴望关注</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 After weeks in the desert, they craved nothing but cold clean water.  在沙漠里待了几周后，他们只渴望干净的凉水。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳近 desiderate</del></td><td>/dɪˈsɪdəˌreɪt/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>渴望，觉得需要</td><td>desiderate a fuller account<br>希望得到更完整的说明</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Scholars desiderate a complete edition of the poet's letters.  学者们渴望有一部这位诗人书信的全集。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 hunger</td><td>/ˈhʌŋɡər/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>名词、动词</td><td>渴望；饥饿</td><td>a hunger for knowledge<br>对知识的渴望</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 His hunger for knowledge kept him reading until dawn.  对知识的渴望让他读书到黎明。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 itch</td><td>/ˈɪtʃ/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>不及物动词、名词</td><td>渴望；发痒</td><td>itch to travel<br>渴望去旅行</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The children were itching to open their presents early.  孩子们急着想早点拆礼物。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 long</td><td>/ˈlɔːŋ/</td><td>★★★★★</td><td>★★★★★</td><td>不及物动词、形容词</td><td>渴望；长的</td><td>long for company<br>渴望有人陪伴</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 After months at sea he longed for his family's cooking.  在海上数月后，他渴望家人做的饭菜。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 lust</td><td>/ˈlʌst/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>名词、不及物动词</td><td>强烈欲望，渴望</td><td>a lust for power<br>对权力的强烈欲望</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 His lust for power destroyed several old and valuable friendships.  他对权力的强烈欲望毁掉了几段珍贵的旧友情。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 pine</td><td>/paɪn/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>不及物动词</td><td>渴望，思念</td><td>pine for home<br>思念家乡</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 After a year abroad he began to pine for the food of home.  在国外一年后，他开始渴望家乡的食物。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 salivate</td><td>/ˈsæləˌveɪt/</td><td>★</td><td>☆</td><td>不及物动词</td><td>垂涎，眼馋；流口水</td><td>salivate over the profits<br>对利润垂涎不已</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Investors salivated at the thought of such easy returns.  想到如此轻易的回报，投资者们垂涎三尺。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 thirst</td><td>/ˈθɜːrst/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>名词、不及物动词</td><td>渴望；口渴</td><td>a thirst for knowledge<br>求知欲</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He thirsted for recognition far more than for money.  他对认可的渴望远超对金钱的渴望。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 want</td><td>/ˈwɑːnt/</td><td>★★★★★</td><td>★★★★★</td><td>名词、动词</td><td>缺乏，短缺</td><td>for want of evidence<br>因缺乏证据</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The project collapsed for want of steady funding and clear leadership.  项目因缺乏稳定资金和明确领导而失败。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 wish</td><td>/ˈwɪʃ/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>动词、名词</td><td>希望，想要；愿望</td><td>wish for a change<br>盼望有所改变</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She wished for nothing more than a quiet evening at home.  她只盼望在家过一个安静的晚上。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 yearn</td><td>/ˈjɜːrn/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>不及物动词</td><td>渴望，向往</td><td>yearn for freedom<br>渴望自由</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 After years abroad he yearned for the food of his childhood.  在国外多年后，他渴望儿时的食物。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 crow</td><td>/kroʊ/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>不及物动词</td><td>得意地夸耀，自鸣得意</td><td>crow about the win<br>为获胜而得意</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He crowed about his victory until even his closest friends grew tired.  他为胜利得意个不停，连最亲近的朋友都听厌了。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 delight</td><td>/dɪˈlaɪt/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>及物动词、名词</td><td>使高兴，使愉快；愉悦</td><td>delight the audience<br>使观众高兴</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The young pianist delighted the audience with a lively encore.  这位年轻钢琴家用一首活泼的加演曲取悦了观众。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 rejoice</td><td>/rɪˈdʒɔɪs/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>不及物动词</td><td>欣喜，喜悦</td><td>rejoice at the news<br>为消息而欣喜</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The whole village rejoiced at the news of the safe return.  听到平安归来的消息，全村都欣喜不已。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 abhor</td><td>/əbˈhɔːr/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>极度厌恶，深恶痛绝</td><td>abhor violence<br>痛恨暴力</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She abhors any form of cruelty to animals.  她极度厌恶任何形式的虐待动物行为。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳反 abominate</del></td><td>/əˈbɑːmɪneɪt/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>动词</td><td>痛恨，厌恶</td><td>abominate cruelty<br>憎恶残忍</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He abominated the hypocrisy he saw all around him.  他痛恨周围随处可见的虚伪。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 detest</td><td>/dɪˈtest/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★★★</td><td>动词</td><td>厌恶，憎恨</td><td>detest hypocrisy<br>厌恶虚伪</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 I detest waiting in long lines on hot days.  我讨厌在炎热的日子里排长队。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 dislike</td><td>/dɪsˈlaɪk/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>动词、名词</td><td>不喜欢，厌恶</td><td>dislike crowded places<br>讨厌拥挤的地方</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He dislikes crowded places and avoids the subway during rush hour.  他讨厌拥挤的地方，高峰时段避开地铁。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 hate</td><td>/ˈheɪt/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>动词、名词</td><td>憎恨，厌恶</td><td>hate being lied to<br>讨厌被人骗</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She hates being lied to, even about very small things.  她讨厌被骗，哪怕是很小的事。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 loathe</td><td>/loʊð/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>厌恶，讨厌</td><td>loathe the sight of<br>一见就厌恶</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He loathes the smell of boiled cabbage.  他讨厌煮卷心菜的味道。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>replete</b></td><td>/rɪˈpliːt/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★</td><td>形容词</td><td>充满…的，富于…的</td><td>replete with errors<br>错误连篇</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 His essay was replete with spelling errors.  他的文章满是拼写错误。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 abounding</td><td>/əˈbaʊndɪŋ/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>形容词</td><td>充满的，大量的</td><td>abounding in fish<br>盛产鱼类</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 They found a river abounding in trout.  他们发现一条鳟鱼很多的河。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 abundant</td><td>/əˈbʌndənt/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>大量的，丰富的</td><td>abundant evidence<br>大量的证据</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Water was abundant after the spring rains.  春雨过后水源充足。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 awash</td><td>/əˈwɔːʃ/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>（如洪水般）泛滥的</td><td>awash with cash<br>现金泛滥</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The small town was awash with cash during the oil boom.  石油繁荣时期，这个小镇资金充裕。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 flush</td><td>/flʌʃ/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>形容词、不及物动词</td><td>充裕的，有钱的；冲洗</td><td>flush with money<br>钱很充裕</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 After the bonus he was flush with money for months.  拿到奖金后他有好几个月钱都很充裕。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 fraught</td><td>/frɔːt/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>充满（危险）的；忧虑的</td><td>fraught with danger<br>充满危险</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The mountain route is fraught with danger in early spring.  早春时节这条山路危机四伏。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 lousy</td><td>/ˈlaʊzi/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>多得很的，充满的；糟糕的</td><td>lousy with tourists<br>游客多得很</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 In August the town is lousy with tourists from every corner of Europe.  八月这座小镇挤满了来自欧洲各地的游客。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 swarming</td><td>/ˈswɔːrmɪŋ/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>拥挤的，密集涌动的</td><td>swarming with tourists<br>挤满游客</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The market was swarming with shoppers on Saturday morning.  周六早上市场里挤满了购物者。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 teeming</td><td>/ˈtiːmɪŋ/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>大量的，充满的</td><td>teeming with life<br>充满生机</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The pond was teeming with tadpoles in May.  五月池塘里满是蝌蚪。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 thick</td><td>/ˈθɪk/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>充满的，密布的；厚的</td><td>thick with smoke<br>烟雾弥漫</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The air was thick with dust from the passing trucks.  空气里满是过路卡车扬起的尘土。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 thronging</td><td>/ˈθrɑːŋɪŋ/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>蜂拥而至的，拥挤的</td><td>thronging crowds<br>蜂拥的人群</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Thronging fans filled the stadium hours before the concert began.  蜂拥的粉丝在演唱会开始前几小时就挤满了体育场。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 depleted</td><td>/dɪˈpliːtɪd/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>耗尽的，枯竭的</td><td>severely depleted fish stocks<br>严重枯竭的鱼类资源</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Years of overfarming left the soil badly depleted of nutrients.  多年过度耕作使土壤养分严重枯竭。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 drained</td><td>/ˈdreɪnd/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>精疲力竭的；被排空的</td><td>feel completely drained<br>感到精疲力竭</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 After the twelve-hour shift she felt utterly drained.  上了十二小时的班，她感到彻底精疲力竭。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 exhausted</td><td>/ɪɡˈzɔːstɪd/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>疲惫不堪的；耗尽的</td><td>exhausted after the climb<br>登山后疲惫不堪</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The hikers arrived exhausted but happy at the mountain hut.  徒步者们疲惫却愉快地到达了山中小屋。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>repose</b></td><td>/rɪˈpoʊz/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>名词</td><td>（劳作后的）休息；平静，宁静</td><td>seek repose<br>寻求休息</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 After the long harvest, the villagers enjoyed a few days of repose.  漫长的收割之后，村民们享受了几天的休息。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 ease</td><td>/ˈiːz/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★★</td><td>动词、名词</td><td>减轻，缓和；容易</td><td>ease the pain<br>减轻疼痛</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The medicine eased her headache within minutes of the first dose.  这药在服下第一剂几分钟内就缓解了她的头痛。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 leisure</td><td>/ˈliːʒər/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>名词</td><td>闲暇，休闲</td><td>at your leisure<br>在你方便的时候</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Read the report at your leisure and tell me later.  你有空时看看报告，之后告诉我。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 relaxation</td><td>/ˌriːlækˈseɪʃən/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★</td><td>名词</td><td>放松，休息；缓和</td><td>rest and relaxation<br>休息与放松</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Gardening is her chief form of relaxation after work.  园艺是她下班后主要的放松方式。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 idle</td><td>/ˈaɪdl/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>闲置的，未使用的；懒散的</td><td>idle machines<br>闲置的机器</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Half the factory's machines stood idle for most of last year.  这家工厂一半的机器去年大部分时间都闲置着。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 loll</td><td>/lɑːl/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>不及物动词</td><td>懒躺，闲混度日</td><td>loll on the sofa<br>懒躺在沙发上</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The children lolled on the sofa instead of finishing their homework.  孩子们懒躺在沙发上，而不是把作业做完。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 lounge</td><td>/ˈlaʊndʒ/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>不及物动词、名词</td><td>懒散地待着，闲荡</td><td>lounge by the pool<br>在泳池边闲躺</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 They lounged in the shade all afternoon without doing anything.  他们整个下午在阴凉处闲躺，什么也没做。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 relax</td><td>/rɪˈlæks/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>动词</td><td>放松，休息；缓和</td><td>relax for a while<br>放松一会儿</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Sit down and relax while I finish making the tea.  你坐下休息一会儿，我把茶泡好。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 rest</td><td>/ˈrest/</td><td>★★★★☆</td><td>★★★★☆</td><td>名词、动词</td><td>休息，静止；剩余部分</td><td>take a short rest<br>稍作休息</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The doctor ordered him to rest for at least a week.  医生要他至少休息一周。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 calmness</td><td>/ˈkɑːmnəs/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>名词</td><td>平静，镇定</td><td>with surprising calmness<br>以惊人的镇定</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Her calmness during the fire kept the whole class safe.  火灾中她的镇定使全班安然无恙。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 hush</td><td>/ˈhʌʃ/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>动词</td><td>使安静，平息</td><td>hush the crying baby<br>哄哭闹的婴儿安静</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She hushed the restless children with one long story.  她用一个长故事把躁动的孩子们哄安静了。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 peace</td><td>/ˈpiːs/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>名词</td><td>和平，和睦；平静</td><td>live in peace<br>和睦相处</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The two villages have lived in peace for over a century.  这两个村庄和睦相处已逾一个世纪。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳近 placidity</del></td><td>/pləˈsɪdɪti/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>平静，安宁</td><td>the placidity of the lake<br>湖面的平静</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Nothing disturbed the placidity of that summer afternoon.  没有什么打扰那个夏日午后的宁静。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 quietness</td><td>/ˈkwaɪətnəs/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>名词</td><td>安静，宁静</td><td>the quietness of dawn<br>黎明的宁静</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 I love the quietness of the house before anyone wakes.  我喜欢家人醒来之前屋子里的宁静。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳近 restfulness</del></td><td>/ˈrɛstfəlnəs/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>安宁，令人放松</td><td>the restfulness of the garden<br>花园的宁静宜人</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Visitors often remark on the restfulness of these old courtyards.  来客常常提到这些老庭院令人放松的宁静。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 serenity</td><td>/səˈrenəti/</td><td>★★</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>宁静，安详</td><td>a sense of serenity<br>一种宁静感</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The garden offers a rare serenity in the middle of the city.  这座花园在城市中心提供了难得的宁静。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 still</td><td>/ˈstɪl/</td><td>★★★★★</td><td>★★★★★</td><td>形容词、名词</td><td>寂静无声的；寂静</td><td>the still of night<br>夜晚的寂静</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The air was still and the lake looked like glass.  空气静止无风，湖面像玻璃一样。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 tranquility</td><td>/træŋˈkwɪləti/</td><td>★★</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>宁静，淡定</td><td>an air of tranquility<br>宁静的气息</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He enjoyed the tranquility of the snowy fields at dusk.  他享受黄昏时白雪覆盖的田野的静谧。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 exertion</td><td>/ɪɡˈzɜːrʃən/</td><td>★☆</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>费力，努力；运用</td><td>physical exertion<br>体力劳累</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He was breathing hard from the exertion of the climb.  攀爬的辛劳让他呼吸沉重。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 labor</td><td>/ˈleɪbər/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>名词、动词</td><td>劳动，苦工；劳工</td><td>manual labor<br>体力劳动</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Years of hard labor had bent his back.  多年的苦力劳动使他驼了背。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 toil</td><td>/ˈtɔɪl/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>名词、不及物动词</td><td>辛苦劳作，苦干</td><td>toil in the fields<br>在田里劳作</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Farmers toiled from sunrise until dark during harvest.  收获时农民从日出劳作到天黑。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 work</td><td>/ˈwɜːrk/</td><td>★★★★★</td><td>★★★★★</td><td>名词、动词</td><td>劳作，工作；作品</td><td>hard physical work<br>繁重的体力劳动</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 A day of heavy work in the fields left them exhausted.  在田里干了一天重活，他们累得不行。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 commotion</td><td>/kəˈmoʊʃn/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>名词</td><td>骚乱</td><td>cause a commotion<br>引起一阵骚动</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The escaped goat caused quite a commotion in the market.  逃跑的山羊在市场上引起一阵不小的骚动。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 pandemonium</td><td>/ˌpændɪˈmoʊniəm/</td><td>★☆</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>喧嚣，一片混乱</td><td>pandemonium broke out<br>顿时一片混乱</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Pandemonium broke out when the final goal was scored.  最后一个进球时全场一片沸腾混乱。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 tumult</td><td>/ˈtuːmʌlt/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>名词</td><td>骚动，暴动</td><td>the tumult of the crowd<br>人群的骚动</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The tumult in the square grew louder as more protesters arrived.  随着更多抗议者到来，广场上的骚动越来越大。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 turmoil</td><td>/ˈtɜːrmɔɪl/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★★</td><td>名词</td><td>混乱，动荡，骚动</td><td>in political turmoil<br>处于政治动荡中</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The country remained in political turmoil for nearly a decade.  这个国家在近十年里一直处于政治动荡之中。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 unrest</td><td>/ʌnˈrest/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>名词</td><td>不安，动荡，骚乱</td><td>social unrest<br>社会动荡</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Rising food prices led to months of social unrest.  食品价格上涨导致了数月的社会动荡。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 uproar</td><td>/ˈʌˌprɔːr/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>名词</td><td>喧嚣，骚动</td><td>cause an uproar<br>引起轩然大波</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The new tax plan caused an uproar among small business owners.  新税收方案在小企业主中引起轩然大波。</td></tr>
</tbody>
</table>

## Unit 5

<table>
<thead><tr>
<th>单词</th>
<th>音标</th>
<th>常用</th>
<th>口语</th>
<th>词性</th>
<th>中文释义</th>
<th>常用短语</th>
</tr></thead>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del><b>reprehend</b></del></td><td>/reprɪˈhend/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>谴责，指责</td><td>reprehend such conduct<br>谴责这种行为</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The committee did not hesitate to reprehend the officer for his careless report.  委员会毫不犹豫地谴责了那名官员草率的报告。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 berate</td><td>/bɪˈreɪt/</td><td>★</td><td>☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>严厉指责，斥责</td><td>berate him publicly<br>公开斥责他</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The coach began to berate his players in front of reporters.  教练开始在记者面前严厉斥责他的球员。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 blame</td><td>/ˈbleɪm/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★★★</td><td>及物动词、名词</td><td>责备，归咎于</td><td>blame someone for something<br>因某事责怪某人</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Don't blame me for the broken window.  别把窗户破了怪在我头上。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 castigate</td><td>/ˈkæstɪɡeɪt/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>强烈（公开）指责</td><td>castigate for negligence<br>因疏忽而受斥责</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The judge castigated the company for its negligence.  法官严厉指责该公司的疏忽。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 censure</td><td>/ˈsenʃər/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>动词、名词</td><td>公开表示反对，谴责</td><td>a vote of censure<br>谴责性投票</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The council voted to censure the mayor for his conduct.  议会投票谴责市长的行为。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 condemn</td><td>/kənˈdem/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>谴责（…为不道德的、邪恶的）</td><td>condemn the attack<br>谴责这次袭击</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 World leaders quickly condemned the attack on the hospital.  各国领导人迅速谴责了对医院的袭击。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 denounce</td><td>/dɪˈnaʊns/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>公开指责</td><td>denounce corruption<br>公开指责腐败</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Local leaders publicly denounced the corruption exposed by the newspaper.  当地领导人公开谴责报纸曝光的腐败行为。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳近 dispraise</del></td><td>/dɪˈspreɪz/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>及物动词、名词</td><td>贬低，责难</td><td>dispraise a rival's work<br>贬低对手的作品</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Reviewers dispraised the novel for its clumsy final chapter.  评论者因结尾笨拙而贬低这部小说。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 fault</td><td>/ˈfɔːlt/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>名词</td><td>缺点，过错；断层</td><td>find fault with<br>挑剔，找毛病</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 His only fault is that he never admits being wrong.  他唯一的缺点是从不承认自己错了。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 knock</td><td>/ˈnɑːk/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>动词、名词</td><td>批评，指责；敲击</td><td>knock his performance<br>批评他的表现</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Critics knocked the film for its careless historical detail.  评论者批评这部电影历史细节草率。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 lambaste</td><td>/læmˈbeɪst/</td><td>☆</td><td>★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>严厉斥责，痛骂</td><td>lambaste the government<br>痛斥政府</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Critics lambasted the film for its shallow, sentimental ending.  批评家严厉斥责这部电影结局浅薄而煽情。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 pan</td><td>/pæn/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>及物动词、名词</td><td>严厉批评</td><td>pan a new film<br>严厉批评一部新片</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Critics panned the film for its clumsy dialogue and predictable ending.  影评人严厉批评这部电影对话笨拙、结局可预料。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳近 upbraid</del></td><td>/ʌpˈbreɪd/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>（严厉地）谴责，责骂</td><td>upbraid him for lying<br>因撒谎而责骂他</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The manager upbraided him for missing three deadlines in one month.  经理因他一个月内三次误期而严厉责骂他。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 extol</td><td>/ɪkˈstoʊl/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>赞美；吹捧</td><td>extol his virtues<br>赞美他的品德</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Neighbors still extol his kindness during the long winter flood.  邻居们至今仍赞美他在漫长冬季洪水期间的善举。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 laud</td><td>/ˈlɔːd/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>赞美，称赞</td><td>laud his courage<br>赞扬他的勇气</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Critics lauded the young pianist for her fearless interpretation.  评论家赞扬这位年轻钢琴家大胆的演绎。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 praise</td><td>/ˈpreɪz/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>名词、动词</td><td>赞扬，称赞</td><td>win high praise<br>获得高度赞扬</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Her first novel won high praise from serious critics.  她的第一部小说赢得了严肃评论家的高度赞扬。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>repress</b></td><td>/rɪˈpres/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>镇压；阻止（正常的表达、活动或发展等）</td><td>repress a smile<br>忍住笑</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She struggled to repress a smile during the solemn ceremony.  在庄严的仪式上她努力忍住笑意。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 crush</td><td>/krʌʃ/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>压碎，碾碎</td><td>crush garlic with a knife<br>用刀把蒜压碎</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He crushed the dry leaves into powder between his palms.  他用手掌把干叶子搓成了粉末。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 quash</td><td>/kwɔːʃ/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>撤销，废除；镇压，阻止</td><td>quash a conviction<br>撤销有罪判决</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The appeal court quashed his conviction last week.  上诉法院上周撤销了对他的定罪。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 quell</td><td>/kwel/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>压制；使平静，使安静</td><td>quell the unrest<br>平息动乱</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Troops were sent in to quell the growing unrest.  部队被派去平息日益扩大的动乱。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 silence</td><td>/ˈsaɪləns/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★</td><td>名词、及物动词</td><td>寂静，沉默；使沉默</td><td>break the silence<br>打破沉默</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 A long silence followed his surprising announcement to the class.  他向全班宣布这惊人消息后是长久的沉默。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 squash</td><td>/ˈskwɑːʃ/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>镇压，压制；压扁</td><td>squash the rumor<br>压下谣言</td></tr>
