<tr><td colspan="7">例 She watched the serene surface of the lake at dawn without speaking.  她默默看着黎明时宁静的湖面。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 tranquil</td><td>/ˈtræŋkwəl/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>平静的，安宁的</td><td>a tranquil lake<br>平静的湖面</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 We spent a tranquil afternoon reading beside the quiet river.  我们在静静的河边度过了一个安宁的下午。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>revelation</b></td><td>/ˌrevəˈleɪʃn/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★☆</td><td>名词</td><td>爆料，揭示</td><td>a startling revelation<br>一个惊人的爆料</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The diary contained a revelation that changed how historians view the war.  那本日记中的一处爆料改变了历史学家对这场战争的看法。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 disclosure</td><td>/dɪsˈkloʊʒər/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>名词</td><td>披露，透露；被披露的事</td><td>full disclosure<br>完全披露</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The disclosure of the emails ended his political career.  这些邮件的披露结束了他的政治生涯。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳近 divulgence</del></td><td>/dɪˈvʌldʒəns/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>泄露，透露</td><td>the divulgence of secrets<br>秘密的泄露</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Any divulgence of client data breaks the contract.  任何泄露客户数据的行为都违反合同。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 exposure</td><td>/ɪkˈspoʊʒər/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>名词</td><td>揭露，曝光；暴露</td><td>the exposure of fraud<br>对欺诈行为的揭露</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Public exposure of the scandal forced two directors to resign.  丑闻的公开曝光迫使两名董事辞职。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>resurgence</b></td><td>/rɪˈsɜːrdʒəns/</td><td>★★</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>复兴</td><td>a resurgence of interest<br>兴趣的再度高涨</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Recent films have caused a surprising resurgence of interest in classical music.  近期的电影令人们对古典音乐的兴趣意外复兴。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 reanimation</td><td>/riˌænɪˈmeɪʃən/</td><td>★</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>复苏，重新振作</td><td>the reanimation of the movement<br>这场运动的复苏</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The festival brought a reanimation of local craft traditions.  这个节庆使当地手工艺传统重新活跃起来。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 rebirth</td><td>/riˈbɜːrθ/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>名词</td><td>重生，复兴</td><td>a cultural rebirth<br>一次文化复兴</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The new theater marked the rebirth of the whole district.  新剧院标志着整个区域的重生。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 regeneration</td><td>/rɪˌdʒenəˈreɪʃən/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>重生，复兴；再生</td><td>urban regeneration<br>城市复兴</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Urban regeneration brought shops back to the empty waterfront.  城市复兴让空荡的滨水区重新有了店铺。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 rejuvenation</td><td>/rɪˌdʒuːvəˈneɪʃən/</td><td>★☆</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>恢复活力，返老还童</td><td>urban rejuvenation<br>城市复兴</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The holiday brought a complete rejuvenation of his spirits.  假期让他的精神完全恢复了活力。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 renewal</td><td>/rɪˈnuːəl/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>名词</td><td>复兴，更新；续期</td><td>spiritual renewal<br>精神上的更新</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Spring always brings a feeling of renewal to the village.  春天总给这个村子带来一种更新的感觉。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 resurrection</td><td>/ˌrezəˈrekʃən/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>复活，复兴</td><td>the resurrection of hope<br>希望的重生</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Fans witnessed the resurrection of a club everyone had written off.  球迷们见证了一家人人都不看好的俱乐部的复兴。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 resuscitation</td><td>/rɪˌsʌsɪˈteɪʃən/</td><td>★☆</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>复苏，苏醒；急救</td><td>attempt resuscitation<br>尝试急救复苏</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Paramedics performed resuscitation on the scene for ten minutes.  急救人员在现场进行了十分钟的复苏抢救。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 revitalization</td><td>/ˌriːvaɪtələˈzeɪʃən/</td><td>★☆</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>复兴，重新振兴</td><td>urban revitalization<br>城市复兴</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The festival began the revitalization of the sleepy river town.  这个节庆开启了沉寂河镇的复兴。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 decay</td><td>/dɪˈkeɪ/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>不及物动词、名词</td><td>衰败，腐烂</td><td>fall into slow decay<br>逐渐衰败</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The abandoned mill has been left to decay for decades.  这座废弃磨坊已任其衰败数十年。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 degradation</td><td>/ˌdeɡrəˈdeɪʃən/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>退化，恶化；堕落</td><td>environmental degradation<br>环境退化</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Rapid environmental degradation has already destroyed much of the wetland.  快速的环境退化已破坏了大部分湿地。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 deterioration</td><td>/dɪˌtɪriəˈreɪʃn/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>恶化；堕落</td><td>a sharp deterioration in health<br>健康急剧恶化</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Doctors noted a sharp deterioration in the old man's health.  医生注意到这位老人的健康急剧恶化。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 downfall</td><td>/ˈdaʊnˌfɔːl/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>名词</td><td>垮台，衰落</td><td>lead to his downfall<br>导致他垮台</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Greed eventually led to the powerful banker's sudden downfall.  贪婪最终导致这位有权势的银行家突然垮台。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 ebb</td><td>/ˈeb/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>动词、名词</td><td>衰退，减弱；退潮</td><td>his strength ebbed away<br>他的体力渐渐衰退</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Public enthusiasm for the project ebbed over the winter.  公众对这个项目的热情在冬天渐渐消退。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 fall</td><td>/ˈfɔːl/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>动词、名词</td><td>下降，落下；秋天</td><td>a sharp fall in prices<br>价格急剧下跌</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Share prices fell again after the disappointing earnings report.  令人失望的财报公布后股价再次下跌。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>resuscitate</b></td><td>/rɪˈsʌsɪteɪt/</td><td>★☆</td><td>☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>使复活，使苏醒</td><td>resuscitate a drowning victim<br>抢救一名溺水者</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Paramedics worked for twenty minutes to resuscitate the man pulled from the lake.  急救人员花了二十分钟抢救从湖里救上来的男子。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 reanimate</td><td>/riˈænɪˌmeɪt/</td><td>★</td><td>☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>使复活，使重新振作</td><td>reanimate the debate<br>使争论重新活跃</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 A single letter reanimated a friendship dormant for years.  一封信让沉寂多年的友谊重新活了过来。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 recharge</td><td>/riˈtʃɑːrdʒ/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>动词</td><td>充电；恢复精力</td><td>recharge his batteries<br>恢复精力</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 A quiet weekend in the mountains recharged him completely.  在山里过了个安静的周末，他完全恢复了精力。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 refresh</td><td>/rɪˈfreʃ/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>使恢复精力，使清爽</td><td>refresh the weary travelers<br>使疲惫的旅人恢复精神</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 A cold shower refreshed him after the long hike.  长途徒步后，一个冷水澡让他恢复了精神。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 rejuvenate</td><td>/rɪˈdʒuːvəˌneɪt/</td><td>★☆</td><td>☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>使显得年轻，使恢复活力</td><td>rejuvenate the old district<br>使老城区恢复活力</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Two weeks by the sea seemed to rejuvenate my grandmother.  在海边待了两周，我外婆好像年轻了许多。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 rekindle</td><td>/riˈkɪndəl/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>重新点燃，重新激起</td><td>rekindle interest<br>重新激起兴趣</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The documentary rekindled public interest in deep-sea exploration.  这部纪录片重新激起了公众对深海探索的兴趣。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 renew</td><td>/rɪˈnuː/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>补充更新，续期</td><td>renew the water supply<br>补充水源</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Spring rains renewed the reservoir after a long dry summer.  春雨在漫长干旱的夏季后补满了水库。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 resurrect</td><td>/ˌrezəˈrekt/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>使重生，使复苏</td><td>resurrect an old idea<br>重提一个旧想法</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The new director hopes to resurrect a play that failed thirty years ago.  新导演希望让三十年前失败的一部戏重获生机。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 revitalize</td><td>/riˈvaɪtəˌlaɪz/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>使恢复生机，使复兴</td><td>revitalize the local economy<br>振兴当地经济</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The new library helped revitalize a neighborhood everyone had abandoned.  新图书馆帮助振兴了一个人人都已放弃的街区。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 revive</td><td>/rɪˈvaɪv/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>不及物动词</td><td>再获新生</td><td>revive after the crisis<br>危机后重获生机</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Local trade began to revive once the new highway opened last spring.  去年春天新高速开通后，当地贸易开始重获生机。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳近 revivify</del></td><td>/riˈvɪvɪˌfaɪ/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>使复活，使重新振作</td><td>revivify a dying tradition<br>使一项衰落的传统复兴</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Fresh funding revivified a research program left dormant for years.  新的资金使一个沉寂多年的研究项目重获生机。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 faint</td><td>/ˈfeɪnt/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>虚弱的；微弱的</td><td>a faint voice<br>微弱的声音</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She felt faint after standing in the sun too long.  在太阳下站太久后她感到虚弱。</td></tr>
</tbody>
</table>

## Unit 8

<table>
<thead><tr>
<th>单词</th>
<th>音标</th>
<th>常用</th>
<th>口语</th>
<th>词性</th>
<th>中文释义</th>
<th>常用短语</th>
</tr></thead>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>retainer</b></td><td>/rɪˈteɪnər/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>名词</td><td>家仆</td><td>a faithful old retainer<br>一位忠心的老家仆</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 An elderly retainer opened the heavy door and led us into the hall.  一位年老的家仆打开厚重的门，带我们进入大厅。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 menial</td><td>/ˈmiːniəl/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>卑贱的，低下的</td><td>menial tasks<br>低贱的杂活</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She took menial jobs to pay for night school.  她做低微的工作来支付夜校学费。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 servant</td><td>/ˈsɜːrvənt/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>名词</td><td>仆人，佣人</td><td>a household servant<br>一名家仆</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 A servant carried the trunks up the narrow back stairs.  一名仆人把箱子搬上狭窄的后楼梯。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 lord</td><td>/ˈlɔːrd/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>名词</td><td>主人，领主；贵族</td><td>lord of the manor<br>庄园领主</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Tenants paid rent to the lord of the manor each autumn.  佃户每年秋天向庄园领主交租。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 master</td><td>/ˈmæstər/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>名词、动词</td><td>大师，名家；主人</td><td>a master of disguise<br>伪装大师</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Only a master of the craft could carve such fine details.  只有行家高手才能雕出这般精细的细节。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>retaliate</b></td><td>/rɪˈtælieɪt/</td><td>★★</td><td>☆</td><td>动词</td><td>报复，反击</td><td>retaliate against critics<br>报复批评者</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 If we raise tariffs, our trading partners will almost certainly retaliate.  如果我们提高关税，贸易伙伴几乎肯定会报复。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 avenge</td><td>/əˈvendʒ/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>为…报仇，报复</td><td>avenge his father's death<br>为父之死报仇</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He swore to avenge the insult done to his family.  他发誓要为家族所受的侮辱报仇。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 redress</td><td>/rɪˈdres/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>名词、及物动词</td><td>赔偿，纠正</td><td>seek legal redress<br>寻求法律赔偿</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Victims went to court to seek redress for their losses.  受害者诉诸法庭为损失寻求赔偿。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 requite</td><td>/rɪˈkwaɪt/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>酬谢，报答；报仇</td><td>requite his loyalty<br>报答他的忠诚</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He wanted to requite her kindness with a gift she would truly treasure.  他想用一件她会真正珍视的礼物报答她的善意。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 revenge</td><td>/rɪˈvendʒ/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>名词</td><td>报复，复仇</td><td>take revenge on<br>向…报复</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He waited years to take revenge on the men who ruined his family.  他等了多年才向毁掉他家庭的那些人报复。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 absolve</td><td>/əbˈzɑːlv/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★</td><td>动词</td><td>使无罪，解除责任</td><td>absolve someone of blame<br>免除某人的责任</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The report absolved the pilot of all blame.  报告免除了飞行员的全部责任。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 condone</td><td>/kənˈdoʊn/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>宽恕；忽视</td><td>condone the violence<br>宽恕这种暴力</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The school will never condone violence between its students.  学校绝不会宽恕学生之间的暴力行为。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 forgive</td><td>/fərˈɡɪv/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>宽容，原谅</td><td>forgive a small mistake<br>原谅小错</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She forgave him quickly, though the insult had stung for days.  她很快原谅了他，尽管那侮辱刺痛了她几天。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 pardon</td><td>/ˈpɑːrdən/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>名词、及物动词</td><td>赦免，宽恕；原谅</td><td>grant him a pardon<br>给予他赦免</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The president pardoned two men convicted decades earlier.  总统赦免了两名几十年前被定罪的男子。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>retard</b></td><td>/rɪˈtɑːrd/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>减速，延迟</td><td>retard the growth<br>延缓生长</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Language barriers retarded the progress of the peace negotiations.  语言障碍阻碍了和平谈判的进展。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 bog</td><td>/ˈbɑːɡ/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>名词</td><td>泥沼；困境</td><td>a bog of regulations<br>法规的泥沼</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The plan got stuck in a bog of official rules.  这个计划陷在官方规定的泥沼中。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 brake</td><td>/breɪk/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>动词</td><td>刹车</td><td>brake sharply<br>急刹车</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The driver braked hard when a deer crossed the road.  司机看到一只鹿穿过马路，猛地刹了车。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 decelerate</td><td>/dɪˈseləreɪt/</td><td>★</td><td>☆</td><td>动词</td><td>减速，放慢</td><td>decelerate before the curve<br>转弯前减速</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The train decelerated smoothly as it entered the station.  列车进站时平稳地减了速。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 delay</td><td>/dɪˈleɪ/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>动词、名词</td><td>延迟，推迟</td><td>delay the departure<br>推迟出发</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Heavy fog delayed the flight by more than four hours.  大雾使航班延误了四个多小时。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 detain</td><td>/dɪˈteɪn/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>拘留，扣押；留住</td><td>detain a suspect<br>拘留嫌疑人</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Police detained the two men for questioning about the missing car.  警方拘留了这两名男子，就失踪汽车一事问话。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 hamper</td><td>/ˈhæmpər/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>妨碍；阻碍；束缚</td><td>hamper the rescue effort<br>妨碍救援工作</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Heavy snow hampered the rescue teams trying to reach the stranded climbers.  大雪妨碍了试图接近被困登山者的救援队。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 impede</td><td>/ɪmˈpiːd/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>妨碍，阻碍</td><td>impede progress<br>阻碍进展</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Thick fog impeded the rescue team's progress.  浓雾阻碍了救援队的前进。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 mire</td><td>/ˈmaɪər/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>名词</td><td>泥沼；泥潭；困境</td><td>a mire of debt<br>债务的泥潭</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The company sank into a mire of debt from which it never escaped.  公司陷入债务的泥潭，再也没能脱身。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 slacken</td><td>/ˈslækən/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>动词</td><td>放慢，减弱；放松</td><td>slacken the pace<br>放慢速度</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The runners slackened their pace on the final hill.  跑者们在最后一个坡上放慢了速度。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 accelerate</td><td>/əkˈseləreɪt/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★</td><td>动词</td><td>加速，加快</td><td>accelerate the process<br>加快进程</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 New funding accelerated the development of the vaccine.  新的资金加快了疫苗的研发。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 catalyze</td><td>/ˈkætəlaɪz/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>成为…的导火索，导致</td><td>catalyze reform<br>推动改革</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The lawsuit catalyzed a wave of reform across the industry.  这场诉讼引发了整个行业的改革浪潮。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 expedite</td><td>/ˈekspədaɪt/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>加快进程</td><td>expedite the process<br>加快流程</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Hiring two more clerks expedited the review of every application.  又雇了两名职员加快了每份申请的审核。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 hasten</td><td>/ˈheɪsn/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>加速，促进；催促</td><td>hasten the decision<br>促使尽快决定</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Warm weather hastened the melting of snow on the high mountain passes.  暖和的天气促进了高山山口积雪的融化。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 precipitate</td><td>/prɪˈsɪpɪteɪt/</td><td>★☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词、及物动词</td><td>匆忙的；促使，导致</td><td>a precipitate departure<br>匆忙的离去</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 His precipitate resignation surprised colleagues who expected a much longer transition.  他匆忙的辞职让原以为过渡期会长得多的同事大吃一惊。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>repetitious</b></td><td>/ˌrepəˈtɪʃəs/</td><td>★</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>重复的，反复的</td><td>repetitious paperwork<br>重复繁琐的文书工作</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 His lectures grew repetitious, covering the same three examples week after week.  他的讲座变得重复，一周又一周讲同样那三个例子。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 duplicative</td><td>/ˈduːplɪkətɪv/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>重复的，多余的</td><td>duplicative effort<br>重复的工作</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Two teams doing duplicative research wasted half the budget.  两个团队做重复研究浪费了一半预算。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳近 reiterative</del></td><td>/riˈɪtərətɪv/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>重复的，反复的</td><td>a reiterative pattern<br>一种反复出现的样式</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The song's reiterative chorus stays in your head for days.  这首歌反复的副歌能在你脑中盘旋好几天。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 repetitive</td><td>/rɪˈpetɪtɪv/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>重复的，单调的</td><td>repetitive factory work<br>单调重复的工厂劳动</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The job is well paid but numbingly repetitive.  这份工作薪水不错，但重复得令人麻木。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>reticent</b></td><td>/ˈretɪsnt/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>沉默不语的</td><td>reticent about his past<br>对往事讳莫如深</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He was oddly reticent about the years he spent working abroad.  他对自己在国外工作的那些年出奇地沉默不语。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳近 closemouthed</del></td><td>/ˈkloʊsˈmaʊðd/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>沉默寡言的，不透露的</td><td>closemouthed about details<br>对细节守口如瓶</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Officials stayed closemouthed about the ongoing negotiations.  官员们对正在进行的谈判守口如瓶。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 constrained</td><td>/kənˈstreɪnd/</td><td>★★</td><td>★</td><td>形容词</td><td>拘谨的，不自然的；受限的</td><td>a constrained smile<br>一个勉强的微笑</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Their conversation was polite but oddly constrained.  他们的谈话礼貌，却出奇地拘谨。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 laconic</td><td>/ləˈkɑːnɪk/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>形容词</td><td>简洁（以至于显得粗鲁或难以理解）的</td><td>a laconic reply<br>简短生硬的回答</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 His laconic reply told us nothing about what had actually happened.  他简短生硬的回答没有告诉我们实际发生了什么。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 reserved</td><td>/rɪˈzɜːrvd/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>内向的，缄默的</td><td>a quiet, reserved man<br>安静内向的男子</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Her father was a reserved man who rarely spoke about his feelings.  她的父亲是个内向的人，很少谈论自己的感受。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 restrained</td><td>/rɪˈstreɪnd/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>受约束的，克制的</td><td>restrained by rules<br>受规则约束</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 His restrained reply hid how angry he actually felt inside.  他克制的回答掩盖了他内心真正的愤怒。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 silent</td><td>/ˈsaɪlənt/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>沉默的，无声的</td><td>remain silent<br>保持沉默</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He remained silent throughout the entire family argument.  整场家庭争执中他一直沉默。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 taciturn</td><td>/ˈtæsɪtɜːrn/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>形容词</td><td>沉默寡言的，话少的</td><td>a taciturn farmer<br>沉默寡言的农夫</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The taciturn farmer answered our questions with a nod or a shrug.  沉默寡言的农夫只用点头或耸肩回答我们的问题。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 communicative</td><td>/kəˈmjuːnɪkətɪv/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>健谈的，愿意交流的</td><td>a communicative host<br>健谈的主人</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He grew more communicative after the second cup of strong tea.  喝下第二杯浓茶后他变得更爱说话。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 expansive</td><td>/ɪkˈspænsɪv/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>话多的，健谈的</td><td>in an expansive mood<br>谈兴正浓</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 After dinner he grew expansive and told stories all evening.  晚饭后他变得健谈，整晚讲故事。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 talkative</td><td>/ˈtɔːkətɪv/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>健谈的，爱说话的</td><td>a talkative passenger<br>健谈的乘客</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Our talkative driver described every building along the winding coastal road.  健谈的司机介绍了蜿蜒海滨路上的每座建筑。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>retort</b></td><td>/rɪˈtɔːrt/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>名词</td><td>（尤指机智的）回应，回答</td><td>a sharp retort<br>一句尖锐的回应</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Her sharp retort silenced the heckler and delighted the rest of the audience.  她一句尖锐的回应让捣乱者噎住，其余观众都很开心。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 comeback</td><td>/ˈkʌmˌbæk/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>名词</td><td>机智的回应；复出</td><td>a witty comeback<br>一句机智的回嘴</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She had a quick comeback for every insult he tried.  他每句挖苦，她都有一句机灵的回嘴。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 rejoinder</td><td>/rɪˈdʒɔɪndər/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>名词</td><td>回答，反驳</td><td>a blunt rejoinder<br>一句直率的反驳</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 His rejoinder silenced the entire table at once.  他的一句反驳立刻让满桌人都沉默了。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 repartee</td><td>/ˌrepɑːrˈtiː/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>名词</td><td>机智的应答，妙语</td><td>quick repartee<br>机敏的应答</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Their dinner conversation sparkled with witty repartee that kept every guest laughing.  他们的餐桌交谈妙语连珠，逗得每位客人都在笑。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 reply</td><td>/rɪˈplaɪ/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>不及物动词、名词</td><td>回答，答复</td><td>reply to a letter<br>回一封信</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She replied to my message within a few minutes.  她几分钟内就回复了我的消息。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 response</td><td>/rɪˈspɑːns/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>名词</td><td>回答，回应；反应</td><td>an immediate response<br>立刻的回应</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Her only response was a slight shrug of the shoulders.  她唯一的回应是轻轻耸了一下肩。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 return</td><td>/rɪˈtɜːrn/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>动词、名词</td><td>回报，报答；归还；返回</td><td>return the favor<br>回报这份人情</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She helped me move, so I returned the favor with dinner.  她帮我搬家，所以我请她吃饭作为回报。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 riposte</td><td>/rɪˈpoʊst/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>名词、动词</td><td>巧妙的回应，反驳</td><td>a clever riposte<br>一句巧妙的回击</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The critic's riposte was printed in the following issue.  这位评论家的回击刊在下一期上。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 inquiry</td><td>/ɪnˈkwaɪəri/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>名词</td><td>调查；询问</td><td>a formal inquiry<br>正式的调查</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Parliament ordered an inquiry into the collapse of the bridge.  议会下令调查那座桥的倒塌。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 query</td><td>/ˈkwɪri/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>名词、动词</td><td>疑问，询问；质疑</td><td>answer a query<br>回答一个疑问</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Send any queries about the schedule to my assistant.  关于日程的任何疑问都发给我的助理。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 question</td><td>/ˈkwestʃən/</td><td>★★★★☆</td><td>★★★</td><td>及物动词、名词</td><td>询问，质疑；问题</td><td>question a witness<br>询问证人</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Police questioned three men about the late-night break-in.  警方就深夜入室盗窃询问了三名男子。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>retouch</b></td><td>/ˌriːˈtʌtʃ/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>动词</td><td>润饰，改进</td><td>retouch a photograph<br>修润一张照片</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The editor asked her to retouch the photograph before printing.  编辑要她在付印前润饰这张照片。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 ameliorate</td><td>/əˈmiːliəreɪt/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>改善，改进</td><td>ameliorate the situation<br>改善局面</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The new subsidy did little to ameliorate rural poverty.  新补贴对改善农村贫困几乎没有作用。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 enhance</td><td>/ɪnˈhæns/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>提高，增强，提升</td><td>enhance the flavor<br>提升风味</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 A little salt greatly enhances the sweetness of the fruit.  少许盐能大大提升水果的甜味。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳近 furbish</del></td><td>/ˈfɜːrbɪʃ/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>磨光，擦亮；翻新</td><td>furbish an old house<br>翻新一栋旧房子</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 They furbished the old theater before the festival began.  他们在节庆开始前翻新了那家老剧院。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳近 meliorate</del></td><td>/ˈmiliəˌreɪt/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>改善，使变好</td><td>meliorate living conditions<br>改善生活条件</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Simple drainage work meliorated conditions in the crowded district.  简单的排水工程改善了拥挤街区的状况。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 perfect</td><td>/ˈpɜːrfɪkt/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>形容词、及物动词</td><td>完美的；使完善</td><td>a perfect score<br>满分成绩</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He spent years trying to perfect his technique on the cello.  他花了数年时间钻研以完善自己的大提琴技巧。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 polish</td><td>/ˈpɑːlɪʃ/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>及物动词、名词</td><td>擦亮，磨光；润色</td><td>polish the wooden floor<br>把木地板擦亮</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She polished her essay carefully before sending it in.  她仔细润色文章后才交上去。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 refine</td><td>/rɪˈfaɪn/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>提纯，精炼；改进</td><td>refine crude oil<br>精炼原油</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The plant refines crude oil into gasoline and other useful products.  这家工厂把原油提炼成汽油和其他有用的产品。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 upgrade</td><td>/ˌʌpˈɡreɪd/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>及物动词、名词</td><td>提升，升级</td><td>upgrade the equipment<br>升级设备</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The airline upgraded her to business class without any charge.  航空公司免费把她升级到商务舱。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 deteriorate</td><td>/dɪˈtɪriəreɪt/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>不及物动词</td><td>恶化，变坏</td><td>deteriorate rapidly<br>迅速恶化</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Her health deteriorated sharply during the cold winter.  在寒冷的冬天里她的健康急剧恶化。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 downgrade</td><td>/ˈdaʊnɡreɪd/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>及物动词、名词</td><td>降级，降低等级</td><td>downgrade the credit rating<br>下调信用评级</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Analysts downgraded the company after two weak quarterly reports.  两份疲弱季报后分析师下调了该公司评级。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 worsen</td><td>/ˈwɜːrsən/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>动词</td><td>使变坏，恶化</td><td>worsen over time<br>随时间恶化</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Her cough worsened after a week outdoors in the cold.  在寒冷的户外待了一周后，她的咳嗽加重了。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>retract</b></td><td>/rɪˈtrækt/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>收回，否认</td><td>retract a statement<br>撤回一项声明</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The newspaper agreed to retract the claim and print a formal apology.  报社同意收回那项说法并刊登正式道歉。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 abjure</td><td>/əbˈdʒʊr/</td><td>☆</td><td>★</td><td>动词</td><td>发誓放弃；避免</td><td>abjure violence<br>发誓弃绝暴力</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The group publicly abjured violence as a political tool.  该团体公开宣布放弃以暴力作为政治手段。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳近 abnegate</del></td><td>/ˈæbnɪɡeɪt/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>动词</td><td>放弃，否认</td><td>abnegate one's rights<br>放弃自己的权利</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The monk chose to abnegate all personal comforts.  这位僧人选择放弃一切个人享受。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 disavow</td><td>/ˌdɪsəˈvaʊ/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>拒绝承认，否认</td><td>disavow any knowledge<br>否认知情</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The senator moved quickly to disavow any knowledge of the secret payments.  参议员迅速表态，否认知晓那些秘密付款。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 forswear</td><td>/fɔrˈswer/</td><td>☆</td><td>★</td><td>动词</td><td>发誓放弃，弃绝</td><td>forswear alcohol<br>发誓戒酒</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He forswore gambling after losing all his savings.  输掉全部积蓄后他发誓戒赌。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 recall</td><td>/rɪˈkɔːl/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>动词、名词</td><td>撤销，召回；回忆</td><td>recall the ambassador<br>召回大使</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The maker recalled thousands of cars with faulty brakes.  制造商召回了数千辆刹车有缺陷的汽车。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 recant</td><td>/rɪˈkænt/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>撤回，公开放弃（说法）</td><td>recant a confession<br>撤回供词</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Under pressure he agreed to recant his earlier statement.  在压力下他同意撤回先前的声明。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 renounce</td><td>/rɪˈnaʊns/</td><td>★★</td><td>★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>正式放弃，声明弃绝</td><td>renounce one's claim<br>声明放弃权利主张</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He renounced his claim to the inheritance in writing.  他以书面形式放弃了继承权。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 repeal</td><td>/rɪˈpiːl/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>撤销，废除（法律等）</td><td>repeal the old law<br>废除旧法律</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Lawmakers voted to repeal the outdated statute that had confused courts for decades.  议员们投票废除了这部困扰法院数十年的过时法规。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 repudiate</td><td>/rɪˈpjuːdieɪt/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>否认，拒绝接受</td><td>repudiate a claim<br>否认一项主张</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The author repudiated the views quoted in the article.  作者否认了文章中引用的观点。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 withdraw</td><td>/wɪðˈdrɔː/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>动词</td><td>取出（款项）；撤回，撤退</td><td>withdraw money from the bank<br>从银行取钱</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He stopped at the bank to withdraw money before the long trip.  他在长途旅行前去银行取钱。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 tender</td><td>/ˈtendər/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>形容词、及物动词</td><td>温柔的，心软同情的；正式提出</td><td>a tender heart<br>一颗温柔同情的心</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She has a tender heart and cannot bear to see animals suffer.  她心地温软，见不得动物受苦。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>retrench</b></td><td>/rɪˈtrentʃ/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>不及物动词</td><td>削减开支</td><td>retrench during a recession<br>在衰退期削减开支</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 When orders fell, the firm decided to retrench rather than borrow more money.  订单下降时，公司决定削减开支而不再借钱。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 economize</td><td>/ɪˈkɑːnəˌmaɪz/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>不及物动词</td><td>节约，节省开支</td><td>economize on fuel<br>节省燃料</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The family had to economize sharply after he lost work.  他失业后一家人不得不大幅节省。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 reduce</td><td>/rəˈduːs/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>减少，降低</td><td>reduce the cost sharply<br>大幅降低成本</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 New machines reduced the factory's energy use by half.  新机器把工厂的能耗降低了一半。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 cut down</td><td>/kʌt ˈdaʊn/</td><td>★★★★☆</td><td>★★★★</td><td>动词</td><td>削减，减少；砍倒</td><td>cut down on spending<br>削减开支</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 We had to cut down on staff travel to save money.  为了省钱我们不得不削减员工出差。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 enlarge</td><td>/ɪnˈlɑːrdʒ/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>动词</td><td>扩大，增大</td><td>enlarge the photograph<br>放大照片</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 They enlarged the kitchen by removing one interior wall.  他们拆掉一道内墙把厨房扩大了。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 expand</td><td>/ɪkˈspænd/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★★</td><td>动词</td><td>扩大，扩张；详述</td><td>expand the business<br>扩大业务</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The company plans to expand into three new markets.  公司计划扩张到三个新市场。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>retribution</b></td><td>/ˌretrɪˈbjuːʃn/</td><td>★★</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>报应；惩罚；严厉的报复</td><td>divine retribution<br>天罚，神的报应</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The villagers believed the flood was divine retribution for their greed.  村民认为洪水是对他们贪婪的天罚。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳近 avengement</del></td><td>/əˈvɛndʒmənt/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>报复，复仇</td><td>seek avengement<br>寻求复仇</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The old ballad tells of a bloody avengement across three generations.  这首古老的叙事诗讲述了三代人的血腥复仇。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 payback</td><td>/ˈpeɪˌbæk/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>名词</td><td>报复；回报，回本</td><td>payback for the betrayal<br>对背叛的报复</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He saw the lawsuit as payback for years of humiliation.  他把这场诉讼看作对多年屈辱的报复。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 recompense</td><td>/ˈrekəmˌpens/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>名词、及物动词</td><td>补偿，报酬</td><td>recompense for the loss<br>对损失的补偿</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She was given a small sum as recompense for her trouble.  她得到一小笔钱作为麻烦的补偿。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 reparation</td><td>/ˌrepəˈreɪʃən/</td><td>★☆</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>赔偿，补偿</td><td>pay war reparations<br>支付战争赔款</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The defeated state was forced to pay heavy reparations.  战败国被迫支付巨额赔款。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 repayment</td><td>/riˈpeɪmənt/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>名词</td><td>报答，报复；偿还</td><td>repayment in kind<br>以同样方式回报</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The gift was repayment for kindness shown long ago.  这份礼物是对很久以前的善意的回报。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 requital</td><td>/rɪˈkwaɪtəl/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>报答，补偿；报复</td><td>requital for his kindness<br>对他好意的报答</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She sent a gift in requital for their long hospitality.  她送去礼物以报答他们长久的款待。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 retaliation</td><td>/rɪˌtæliˈeɪʃən/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>报复，反击</td><td>in retaliation for<br>为报复…</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The strike was launched in retaliation for the earlier raid.  这次打击是为报复此前的袭击而发动的。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 revenge</td><td>/rɪˈvendʒ/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>名词</td><td>报复，复仇</td><td>take revenge on<br>向…报复</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He waited years to take revenge on the men who ruined his family.  他等了多年才向毁掉他家庭的那些人报复。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 vengeance</td><td>/ˈvendʒəns/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★</td><td>名词</td><td>复仇，报复</td><td>swear vengeance<br>发誓复仇</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He devoted the rest of his life to vengeance.  他把余生都用在复仇上。</td></tr>
</tbody>
</table>

## Unit 9

<table>
<thead><tr>
<th>单词</th>
<th>音标</th>
<th>常用</th>
<th>口语</th>
<th>词性</th>
<th>中文释义</th>
<th>常用短语</th>
</tr></thead>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>retrograde</b></td><td>/ˈretrəɡreɪd/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>倒退的</td><td>a retrograde step<br>一次倒退之举</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Banning the new textbooks feels like a retrograde step for the whole district.  禁用新教材对整个学区而言像是一次倒退。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 backward</td><td>/ˈbækwərd/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>形容词、副词</td><td>向后的；落后的；羞怯的</td><td>a backward glance<br>回头一望</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He was too backward to introduce himself to the newcomers.  他太腼腆，不敢向新来的人自我介绍。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 receding</td><td>/rɪˈsiːdɪŋ/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>后退的，渐渐远去的</td><td>a receding hairline<br>渐渐后移的发际线</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 We watched the receding shoreline from the ship's rail.  我们从船舷望着渐渐远去的海岸线。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 regressive</td><td>/rəˈɡresɪv/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>退步的，倒退的</td><td>a regressive policy<br>一项倒退的政策</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Critics called the reform regressive and socially unfair.  批评者称这项改革是倒退且不公平的。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 reversed</td><td>/rɪˈvɜːrst/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>颠倒的，反向的</td><td>in reversed order<br>按相反顺序</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The names appeared in reversed order on the second list.  第二份名单上名字的顺序是颠倒的。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 withdrawing</td><td>/wɪθˈdrɔːɪŋ/</td><td>★★</td><td>★</td><td>形容词</td><td>退缩的，撤回的</td><td>a withdrawing army<br>一支正在撤退的军队</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The withdrawing troops burned the bridges behind them.  撤退的部队烧掉了身后的桥梁。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 atrophy</td><td>/ˈætrəfi/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>不及物动词</td><td>萎缩，衰退</td><td>muscles atrophy quickly<br>肌肉迅速萎缩</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Muscles atrophy quickly when a patient stays in bed for weeks.  病人卧床数周后，肌肉会迅速萎缩。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 crumble</td><td>/ˈkrʌmbəl/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>动词</td><td>崩塌，瓦解；碎裂</td><td>crumble into ruin<br>崩塌成废墟</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Their alliance crumbled as soon as the money ran out.  钱一花完，他们的联盟就瓦解了。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 decay</td><td>/dɪˈkeɪ/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>不及物动词、名词</td><td>衰败，腐烂</td><td>fall into slow decay<br>逐渐衰败</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The abandoned mill has been left to decay for decades.  这座废弃磨坊已任其衰败数十年。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 decline</td><td>/dɪˈklaɪn/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>动词、名词</td><td>拒绝；下降，衰退</td><td>decline an invitation<br>谢绝邀请</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She politely declined the invitation to speak at the ceremony.  她礼貌地谢绝了在典礼上发言的邀请。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 degenerate</td><td>/dɪˈdʒenəreɪt/</td><td>★★</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词、不及物动词</td><td>衰退的，堕落的；恶化</td><td>degenerate into chaos<br>恶化为混乱</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The peaceful protest began to degenerate into shouting and shoving.  和平抗议开始恶化为叫骂和推挤。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 descend</td><td>/dɪˈsend/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>动词</td><td>下降；世代相传</td><td>descend from the summit<br>从山顶下来</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The path descends steeply toward the river through thick pine forest.  小径穿过浓密的松林，向河边陡然下降。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 deteriorate</td><td>/dɪˈtɪriəreɪt/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>不及物动词</td><td>恶化，变坏</td><td>deteriorate rapidly<br>迅速恶化</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Her health deteriorated sharply during the cold winter.  在寒冷的冬天里她的健康急剧恶化。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 ebb</td><td>/ˈeb/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>动词、名词</td><td>衰退，减弱；退潮</td><td>his strength ebbed away<br>他的体力渐渐衰退</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Public enthusiasm for the project ebbed over the winter.  公众对这个项目的热情在冬天渐渐消退。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 regress</td><td>/rɪˈɡres/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>不及物动词</td><td>退化，恶化</td><td>regress to childish behavior<br>退回到幼稚行为</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Under stress he tends to regress to childish and sulky behavior.  在压力下他往往退化为幼稚而怨怼的行为。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 rot</td><td>/ˈrɑːt/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>动词、名词</td><td>腐烂，腐坏</td><td>rot in the damp<br>在潮湿中腐烂</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The wooden posts had rotted through at the base.  木桩底部已经烂穿了。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 sink</td><td>/ˈsɪŋk/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>动词、名词</td><td>下沉，下降；水槽</td><td>sink below the horizon<br>沉到地平线下</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The old boat began to sink soon after the storm.  暴风雨过后不久那条旧船开始下沉。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 worsen</td><td>/ˈwɜːrsən/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>动词</td><td>使变坏，恶化</td><td>worsen over time<br>随时间恶化</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Her cough worsened after a week outdoors in the cold.  在寒冷的户外待了一周后，她的咳嗽加重了。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 progressive</td><td>/prəˈɡresɪv/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>进步的，开明的</td><td>progressive social policies<br>进步的社会政策</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The school follows progressive methods that welcome student questions.  这所学校采用鼓励学生提问的进步方法。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>retrospective</b></td><td>/ˌretrəˈspektɪv/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>形容词、名词</td><td>回顾的，追溯的</td><td>a retrospective exhibition<br>一次回顾展</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The museum opened a retrospective of her early photographs.  博物馆办了一场她早期摄影作品的回顾展。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 backward</td><td>/ˈbækwərd/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>形容词、副词</td><td>向后的；落后的；羞怯的</td><td>a backward glance<br>回头一望</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He was too backward to introduce himself to the newcomers.  他太腼腆，不敢向新来的人自我介绍。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 anticipatory</td><td>/ænˈtɪsəpəˌtɔːri/</td><td>★</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>预期的，期待的</td><td>anticipatory excitement<br>期待中的兴奋</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 An anticipatory hush fell over the hall before the curtain rose.  幕启之前，大厅里一片期待的寂静。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 prospective</td><td>/prəˈspektɪv/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>预期的，未来的</td><td>a prospective buyer<br>一位潜在买家</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Several prospective tenants toured the apartment that afternoon.  那天下午有几位潜在租客看了这套公寓。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>revel</b></td><td>/ˈrevl/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>不及物动词、名词</td><td>享乐，陶醉</td><td>revel in praise<br>陶醉于赞美</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Young actors often revel in the applause of a packed opening night.  年轻演员常陶醉于首演满座时的掌声。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 binge</td><td>/ˈbɪndʒ/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>名词、不及物动词</td><td>狂欢；无节制地大吃大喝</td><td>go on a binge<br>大吃大喝一番</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 After the exams the students went on a three-day binge.  考完试后学生们狂欢了三天。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 carouse</td><td>/kəˈraʊz/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>不及物动词、名词</td><td>痛饮，狂欢作乐</td><td>carouse all night<br>整夜狂饮作乐</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The sailors caroused in the port until the sun came up.  水手们在港口狂饮作乐直到日出。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳近 roister</del></td><td>/ˈrɔɪstər/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>不及物动词</td><td>喧闹作乐，狂欢</td><td>roister in the tavern<br>在酒馆里喧闹狂欢</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Sailors roistered in the port until the ship sailed at dawn.  水手们在港口喧闹作乐，直到黎明开船。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>revenge</b></td><td>/rɪˈvendʒ/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>名词</td><td>报复，复仇</td><td>take revenge on<br>向…报复</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He waited years to take revenge on the men who ruined his family.  他等了多年才向毁掉他家庭的那些人报复。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳近 avengement</del></td><td>/əˈvɛndʒmənt/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>报复，复仇</td><td>seek avengement<br>寻求复仇</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The old ballad tells of a bloody avengement across three generations.  这首古老的叙事诗讲述了三代人的血腥复仇。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 payback</td><td>/ˈpeɪˌbæk/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>名词</td><td>报复；回报，回本</td><td>payback for the betrayal<br>对背叛的报复</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He saw the lawsuit as payback for years of humiliation.  他把这场诉讼看作对多年屈辱的报复。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 reprisal</td><td>/riˈpraɪzəl/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>名词</td><td>报复，报复行动</td><td>fear of reprisal<br>害怕遭到报复</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Witnesses stayed silent for fear of reprisal.  证人们因害怕报复而保持沉默。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 requital</td><td>/rɪˈkwaɪtəl/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>报答，补偿；报复</td><td>requital for his kindness<br>对他好意的报答</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She sent a gift in requital for their long hospitality.  她送去礼物以报答他们长久的款待。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 retaliation</td><td>/rɪˌtæliˈeɪʃən/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>报复，反击</td><td>in retaliation for<br>为报复…</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The strike was launched in retaliation for the earlier raid.  这次打击是为报复此前的袭击而发动的。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 retribution</td><td>/ˌretrɪˈbjuːʃn/</td><td>★★</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>报应；惩罚；严厉的报复</td><td>divine retribution<br>天罚，神的报应</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The villagers believed the flood was divine retribution for their greed.  村民认为洪水是对他们贪婪的天罚。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 vengeance</td><td>/ˈvendʒəns/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★</td><td>名词</td><td>复仇，报复</td><td>swear vengeance<br>发誓复仇</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He devoted the rest of his life to vengeance.  他把余生都用在复仇上。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 avenge</td><td>/əˈvendʒ/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>为…报仇，报复</td><td>avenge his father's death<br>为父之死报仇</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He swore to avenge the insult done to his family.  他发誓要为家族所受的侮辱报仇。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 redress</td><td>/rɪˈdres/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>名词、及物动词</td><td>赔偿，纠正</td><td>seek legal redress<br>寻求法律赔偿</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Victims went to court to seek redress for their losses.  受害者诉诸法庭为损失寻求赔偿。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 requite</td><td>/rɪˈkwaɪt/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>酬谢，报答；报仇</td><td>requite his loyalty<br>报答他的忠诚</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He wanted to requite her kindness with a gift she would truly treasure.  他想用一件她会真正珍视的礼物报答她的善意。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 retaliate</td><td>/rɪˈtælieɪt/</td><td>★★</td><td>☆</td><td>动词</td><td>报复，反击</td><td>retaliate against critics<br>报复批评者</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 If we raise tariffs, our trading partners will almost certainly retaliate.  如果我们提高关税，贸易伙伴几乎肯定会报复。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 forgiveness</td><td>/fərˈɡɪvnəs/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>名词</td><td>宽恕，原谅</td><td>ask for forgiveness<br>请求原谅</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He wrote to ask forgiveness for what he had said.  他写信为自己说过的话请求原谅。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 pardon</td><td>/ˈpɑːrdən/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>名词、及物动词</td><td>赦免，宽恕；原谅</td><td>grant him a pardon<br>给予他赦免</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The president pardoned two men convicted decades earlier.  总统赦免了两名几十年前被定罪的男子。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>revere</b></td><td>/rɪˈvɪr/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>（尤指对神的）尊崇，尊敬</td><td>revered as a hero<br>被尊崇为英雄</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Students in the village still revere the teacher who founded their school.  村里的学生仍然尊崇创办他们学校的那位老师。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 adore</td><td>/əˈdɔːr/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>热爱，极喜爱；崇拜</td><td>absolutely adore someone<br>非常喜爱某人</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The children adore their new puppy.  孩子们非常喜爱他们的新小狗。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 deify</td><td>/ˈdeɪɪfaɪ/</td><td>☆</td><td>★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>神化，奉若神明</td><td>deify a leader<br>神化领袖</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The regime tried to deify its founder in every textbook.  该政权试图在每本教科书里神化其创始人。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 glorify</td><td>/ˈɡlɔːrəˌfaɪ/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>赞美，美化</td><td>glorify war and violence<br>美化战争与暴力</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Critics said the film glorified war instead of questioning it.  批评者说这部电影美化战争而非质疑它。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 esteem</td><td>/ɪˈstiːm/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★</td><td>名词、动词</td><td>尊重，敬重</td><td>hold in high esteem<br>十分敬重</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Her colleagues esteem her for her honesty and courage.  同事们因她的诚实与勇气而敬重她。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 regard</td><td>/rɪˈɡɑːrd/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>名词、及物动词</td><td>关注，重视；看待</td><td>have regard for others<br>顾及他人</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He drives fast with little regard for other people.  他开车很快，几乎不顾及他人。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 respect</td><td>/rɪˈspekt/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>名词、及物动词</td><td>尊敬，敬重；方面</td><td>show respect for<br>对……表示尊敬</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Students should learn to respect opinions that differ from their own.  学生应学会尊重与自己不同的意见。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 venerate</td><td>/ˈvenəreɪt/</td><td>★</td><td>☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>尊敬</td><td>venerate their ancestors<br>崇敬他们的祖先</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Villagers still venerate their ancestors with offerings twice each year.  村民每年两次以供品崇敬他们的祖先。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 worship</td><td>/ˈwɜːrʃəp/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>动词、名词</td><td>崇拜，敬奉；礼拜</td><td>worship ancient gods<br>敬奉古代神灵</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 They gathered at dawn to worship in the small chapel.  他们黎明时聚在小教堂做礼拜。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 blaspheme</td><td>/blæsˈfim/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>动词</td><td>亵渎，辱骂（神圣之物）</td><td>blaspheme against God<br>亵渎神灵</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 In that century anyone who blasphemed publicly risked imprisonment.  在那个世纪，公开亵渎神灵者可能被监禁。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 desecrate</td><td>/ˈdesɪkreɪt/</td><td>★</td><td>☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>亵渎，玷污</td><td>desecrate a holy shrine<br>亵渎神圣的殿堂</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Vandals desecrated the old cemetery and overturned a dozen stones.  破坏者亵渎了那座老墓园，推倒了十几块墓碑。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 profane</td><td>/prəˈfeɪn/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★</td><td>及物动词、形容词</td><td>亵渎</td><td>profane a sacred place<br>亵渎圣地</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Vandals profaned the temple by scrawling slogans on its walls.  破坏者在庙墙上乱涂标语，亵渎了圣殿。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 violate</td><td>/ˈvaɪəleɪt/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>违反；侵犯；亵渎</td><td>violate a sacred grave<br>亵渎神圣的坟墓</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Looters violated the tombs that villagers had guarded for centuries.  盗墓者亵渎了村民守护数百年的墓葬。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>resonate</b></td><td>/ˈrezəneɪt/</td><td>★★</td><td>☆</td><td>动词</td><td>引起共鸣，产生反响</td><td>resonate with readers<br>在读者中引起共鸣</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Her speech resonated with everyone who had lost a job.  她的演讲在每个失业过的人心中引起共鸣。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 ring true</td><td>/rɪŋ ˈtru/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>动词</td><td>听起来真实可信</td><td>his story rings true<br>他的说法听着可信</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Nothing in that confession rings true to me.  那份供词在我听来没有一句可信。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>revive</b></td><td>/rɪˈvaɪv/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>不及物动词</td><td>再获新生</td><td>revive after the crisis<br>危机后重获生机</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Local trade began to revive once the new highway opened last spring.  去年春天新高速开通后，当地贸易开始重获生机。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 reanimate</td><td>/riˈænɪˌmeɪt/</td><td>★</td><td>☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>使复活，使重新振作</td><td>reanimate the debate<br>使争论重新活跃</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 A single letter reanimated a friendship dormant for years.  一封信让沉寂多年的友谊重新活了过来。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 recharge</td><td>/riˈtʃɑːrdʒ/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>动词</td><td>充电；恢复精力</td><td>recharge his batteries<br>恢复精力</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 A quiet weekend in the mountains recharged him completely.  在山里过了个安静的周末，他完全恢复了精力。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 refresh</td><td>/rɪˈfreʃ/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>使恢复精力，使清爽</td><td>refresh the weary travelers<br>使疲惫的旅人恢复精神</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 A cold shower refreshed him after the long hike.  长途徒步后，一个冷水澡让他恢复了精神。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 rejuvenate</td><td>/rɪˈdʒuːvəˌneɪt/</td><td>★☆</td><td>☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>使显得年轻，使恢复活力</td><td>rejuvenate the old district<br>使老城区恢复活力</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Two weeks by the sea seemed to rejuvenate my grandmother.  在海边待了两周，我外婆好像年轻了许多。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 rekindle</td><td>/riˈkɪndəl/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>重新点燃，重新激起</td><td>rekindle interest<br>重新激起兴趣</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The documentary rekindled public interest in deep-sea exploration.  这部纪录片重新激起了公众对深海探索的兴趣。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 renew</td><td>/rɪˈnuː/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>补充更新，续期</td><td>renew the water supply<br>补充水源</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Spring rains renewed the reservoir after a long dry summer.  春雨在漫长干旱的夏季后补满了水库。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 resurrect</td><td>/ˌrezəˈrekt/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>使重生，使复苏</td><td>resurrect an old idea<br>重提一个旧想法</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The new director hopes to resurrect a play that failed thirty years ago.  新导演希望让三十年前失败的一部戏重获生机。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 resuscitate</td><td>/rɪˈsʌsɪteɪt/</td><td>★☆</td><td>☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>使复活，使苏醒</td><td>resuscitate a drowning victim<br>抢救一名溺水者</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Paramedics worked for twenty minutes to resuscitate the man pulled from the lake.  急救人员花了二十分钟抢救从湖里救上来的男子。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 revitalize</td><td>/riˈvaɪtəˌlaɪz/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>使恢复生机，使复兴</td><td>revitalize the local economy<br>振兴当地经济</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The new library helped revitalize a neighborhood everyone had abandoned.  新图书馆帮助振兴了一个人人都已放弃的街区。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳近 revivify</del></td><td>/riˈvɪvɪˌfaɪ/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>使复活，使重新振作</td><td>revivify a dying tradition<br>使一项衰落的传统复兴</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Fresh funding revivified a research program left dormant for years.  新的资金使一个沉寂多年的研究项目重获生机。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 degenerate</td><td>/dɪˈdʒenəreɪt/</td><td>★★</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词、不及物动词</td><td>衰退的，堕落的；恶化</td><td>degenerate into chaos<br>恶化为混乱</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The peaceful protest began to degenerate into shouting and shoving.  和平抗议开始恶化为叫骂和推挤。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 deteriorate</td><td>/dɪˈtɪriəreɪt/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>不及物动词</td><td>恶化，变坏</td><td>deteriorate rapidly<br>迅速恶化</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Her health deteriorated sharply during the cold winter.  在寒冷的冬天里她的健康急剧恶化。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 worsen</td><td>/ˈwɜːrsən/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>动词</td><td>使变坏，恶化</td><td>worsen over time<br>随时间恶化</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Her cough worsened after a week outdoors in the cold.  在寒冷的户外待了一周后，她的咳嗽加重了。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>revoke</b></td><td>/rɪˈvoʊk/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>撤销，废除；吊销</td><td>revoke a license<br>吊销执照</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The court revoked his driving license after the second offense.  法院在他第二次违法后吊销了他的驾照。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 abolish</td><td>/əˈbɑːlɪʃ/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>废除，取消</td><td>abolish the death penalty<br>废除死刑</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The government promised to abolish the unpopular entrance fee.  政府承诺废除那项不受欢迎的入场费。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 abrogate</td><td>/ˈæbrəɡeɪt/</td><td>★</td><td>☆</td><td>动词</td><td>正式废除（法律、条约）</td><td>abrogate a treaty<br>废除条约</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Parliament voted to abrogate the outdated law.  议会投票废除这项过时的法律。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 annul</td><td>/əˈnʌl/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>宣告无效，取消</td><td>annul the marriage<br>宣告婚姻无效</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The court agreed to annul the marriage after only three weeks.  法院同意在仅仅三周之后宣告这段婚姻无效。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 cancel</td><td>/ˈkænsəl/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>取消，撤销；抵消</td><td>cancel the meeting<br>取消会议</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 They canceled the outdoor concert because of the heavy rain.  因为大雨，他们取消了露天音乐会。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 invalidate</td><td>/ɪnˈvælɪdeɪt/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>使无效，使作废</td><td>invalidate a contract<br>使合同无效</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 A missing signature will invalidate the whole form.  缺少签名会使整张表格失效。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 negate</td><td>/nɪˈɡeɪt/</td><td>★★</td><td>☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>否认；否定，取消</td><td>negate the effect<br>抵消效果</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 One careless error can negate weeks of careful work.  一个粗心的错误能抹掉数周的细致工作。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 null</td><td>/ˈnʌl/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>无效的；等于零的</td><td>null and void<br>无效的，作废的</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The court declared the agreement null and void.  法院宣布该协议无效。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 nullify</td><td>/ˈnʌləfaɪ/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>使无效，废除</td><td>nullify an agreement<br>使协议失效</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The judge nullified the election result.  法官宣布选举结果无效。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 recall</td><td>/rɪˈkɔːl/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>动词、名词</td><td>撤销，召回；回忆</td><td>recall the ambassador<br>召回大使</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The maker recalled thousands of cars with faulty brakes.  制造商召回了数千辆刹车有缺陷的汽车。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 repeal</td><td>/rɪˈpiːl/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>撤销，废除（法律等）</td><td>repeal the old law<br>废除旧法律</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Lawmakers voted to repeal the outdated statute that had confused courts for decades.  议员们投票废除了这部困扰法院数十年的过时法规。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 void</td><td>/ˈvɔɪd/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>及物动词、形容词、名词</td><td>使无效；排空；空虚</td><td>void the contract<br>使合同无效</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Missing a single payment can void the entire warranty agreement.  漏付一期就可能使整个保修协议失效。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 establish</td><td>/ɪˈstæblɪʃ/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>确证，证实；建立</td><td>establish the facts beyond doubt<br>确证事实无疑</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The investigation established the facts beyond any reasonable doubt.  调查毫无疑问地确证了事实。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 enact</td><td>/ɪˈnækt/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>制定或颁布（法律）</td><td>enact a new tax law<br>制定新税法</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Parliament moved quickly to enact a new tax law before the recess.  议会在休会前迅速制定了一部新税法。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>revolt</b></td><td>/rɪˈvoʊlt/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>使厌恶，使反感</td><td>revolted by the smell<br>被气味恶心到</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The smell of the spoiled milk revolted everyone in the small kitchen.  变质牛奶的气味让狭小厨房里的每个人都反胃。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 disgust</td><td>/dɪsˈɡʌst/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>及物动词、名词</td><td>使厌恶；厌恶</td><td>disgust even his allies<br>连他的盟友也感到厌恶</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The state of the kitchen disgusted the health inspector.  厨房的状况让卫生检查员十分厌恶。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳近 nauseate</del></td><td>/ˈnɔːzieɪt/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>动词</td><td>使恶心；使厌恶</td><td>nauseate the audience<br>使观众反感</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The graphic descriptions in the film nauseated much of the audience.  影片中露骨的描写让大部分观众感到恶心。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 repel</td><td>/rɪˈpel/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>击退，驱退；使厌恶</td><td>repel the attackers<br>击退进攻者</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The greasy smell of the kitchen seemed to repel every customer who entered.  厨房油腻的气味似乎让每个进来的顾客都感到厌恶。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 repulse</td><td>/rɪˈpʌls/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>使厌恶，排斥</td><td>repulsed by the smell<br>被那气味恶心到</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The sight of the rotting fruit repulsed everyone standing near the crate.  腐烂水果的样子让站在箱子旁的每个人都感到恶心。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 sicken</td><td>/ˈsɪkən/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>动词</td><td>生病，患病；使厌恶</td><td>sicken and die<br>患病死去</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Many of the sheep sickened after drinking from the polluted stream.  许多羊喝了污染的溪水后患病。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 allure</td><td>/əˈlʊr/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>及物动词、名词</td><td>吸引，诱惑</td><td>the allure of fame<br>名声的诱惑</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Cheap rents allure young artists to this run-down district.  低廉的租金吸引年轻艺术家来到这个破旧街区。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 attract</td><td>/əˈtrækt/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>吸引，引起</td><td>attract attention<br>吸引注意</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The exhibition attracted thousands of visitors in its first week.  展览首周吸引了数千名参观者。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 bewitch</td><td>/bɪˈwɪtʃ/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>使着迷；用魔法迷住</td><td>bewitch the audience<br>让观众为之着迷</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Her singing bewitched everyone in the crowded little hall.  她的歌声让拥挤小厅里的每个人都着了迷。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 captivate</td><td>/ˈkæptɪveɪt/</td><td>★</td><td>★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>使着迷，深深吸引</td><td>captivate the audience<br>深深吸引观众</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Her first novel captivated readers around the world.  她的第一部小说令世界各地的读者着迷。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 charm</td><td>/ˈtʃɑːrm/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>及物动词、名词</td><td>迷住，吸引；魅力</td><td>charm the audience<br>迷住观众</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The young pianist charmed the audience with her first piece.  这位年轻钢琴家用第一首曲子迷住了观众。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 enchant</td><td>/ɪnˈtʃænt/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>使入迷，使陶醉</td><td>be enchanted by the scenery<br>被风景迷住</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The old fishing village enchanted every visitor who stayed there.  那个古老渔村令每位住过的游客陶醉。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 entice</td><td>/ɪnˈtaɪs/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>诱使，引诱</td><td>entice customers with discounts<br>用折扣吸引顾客</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Free samples were used to entice shoppers into the new store.  免费样品被用来引诱购物者进入新店。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 fascinate</td><td>/ˈfæsɪneɪt/</td><td>★</td><td>★★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>强烈吸引，使入迷</td><td>fascinate young readers<br>使小读者着迷</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Stories about strange deep-sea creatures fascinate young readers all over the world.  关于奇异深海生物的故事让全世界的小读者着迷。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 lure</td><td>/ˈlʊr/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>动词、名词</td><td>吸引，诱惑；诱饵</td><td>lure customers in<br>把顾客吸引进来</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Low prices lured shoppers away from the older stores.  低价把购物者从老店吸引走了。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 seduce</td><td>/sɪˈduːs/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>诱使（背离正道），引诱</td><td>seduce someone into crime<br>引诱某人犯罪</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 High salaries seduced many teachers away from the classroom.  高薪诱使许多教师离开了课堂。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 tempt</td><td>/ˈtempt/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>诱惑，引诱</td><td>tempt fate<br>冒险行事，玩火</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The smell of fresh bread tempted us into the little bakery.  新鲜面包的香味把我们诱进了那家小面包店。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>ribald</b></td><td>/ˈrɪbəld/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>形容词</td><td>（举止、言语）下流粗俗的</td><td>ribald jokes<br>粗俗下流的笑话</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The old sailor entertained the crew with ribald songs late into the night.  老水手用粗俗下流的歌曲让船员们乐到深夜。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 bawdy</td><td>/ˈbɔːdi/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>淫猥的，下流的</td><td>a bawdy song<br>一首下流的歌</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The sailors sang bawdy songs late into the night.  水手们唱着下流的歌唱到深夜。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 blue</td><td>/ˈbluː/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>下流的，猥亵的；蓝色的</td><td>blue humor<br>低俗的黄色幽默</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 His routine was too blue for a family audience.  他的表演对家庭观众来说太下流了。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 coarse</td><td>/kɔːrs/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>粗糙的，表面不平整的；粗俗的</td><td>coarse language<br>粗俗的言语</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The workers used coarse language that shocked the visiting students.  工人们说的粗话让来访的学生们大吃一惊。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 crude</td><td>/ˈkruːd/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>粗糙的，未加工的；粗俗的</td><td>crude oil<br>原油</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The furniture was crude but surprisingly sturdy and very cheap.  这家具做得粗糙，却意外结实又便宜。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 dirty</td><td>/ˈdɜːrti/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>形容词、及物动词</td><td>肮脏的；卑鄙的</td><td>dirty windows<br>脏污的窗子</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The children came home with dirty hands and happy faces.  孩子们回家时手很脏，脸上却很开心。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 filthy</td><td>/ˈfɪlθi/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>污秽的，极脏的</td><td>filthy old clothes<br>污秽的旧衣服</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The kitchen floor was filthy after the long noisy party.  漫长喧闹的聚会后厨房地板脏得一塌糊涂。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 gross</td><td>/ˈɡroʊs/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>过于肥胖的；粗俗的</td><td>a gross, bloated figure<br>臃肿肥胖的身形</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The caricature gave the mayor a gross, swollen belly.  那幅漫画给市长画了个臃肿肥大的肚子。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 indecent</td><td>/ɪnˈdiːsnt/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>不得体的，下流的</td><td>indecent language in public<br>公开场合的下流言语</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The comedian was fined for using indecent language on live television.  这位喜剧演员因在直播电视上使用下流言语被罚款。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 nasty</td><td>/ˈnæsti/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>令人厌恶的，肮脏的；恶劣的</td><td>a nasty smell<br>难闻的气味</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 A nasty stain spread slowly across the pale carpet.  一块难看的污渍在浅色地毯上慢慢蔓延。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 obscene</td><td>/ɑbˈsiːn/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>淫秽的，猥亵的</td><td>obscene language<br>淫秽的言语</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The magazine was banned for printing obscene photographs.  这本杂志因刊登淫秽照片而被查禁。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 pornographic</td><td>/ˌpɔːrnəˈɡræfɪk/</td><td>★★</td><td>★</td><td>形容词</td><td>色情的，淫秽的</td><td>pornographic material<br>色情材料</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Customs officers seized several crates of pornographic prints.  海关人员查获了几箱色情印刷品。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 profane</td><td>/prəˈfeɪn/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★</td><td>及物动词、形容词</td><td>亵渎</td><td>profane a sacred place<br>亵渎圣地</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Vandals profaned the temple by scrawling slogans on its walls.  破坏者在庙墙上乱涂标语，亵渎了圣殿。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 vulgar</td><td>/ˈvʌlɡər/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>粗俗的，无教养的</td><td>vulgar display of wealth<br>粗俗的炫富</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Their vulgar display of wealth embarrassed even their closest friends.  他们粗俗的炫富让连最亲近的朋友都感到难堪。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 decent</td><td>/ˈdiːsənt/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>得体的，正派的</td><td>decent and respectful behavior<br>得体而恭敬的举止</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He is a decent man who always keeps his promises.  他是个正派的人，总是守承诺。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 proper</td><td>/ˈprɑːpər/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>适当的，正确的</td><td>the proper procedure<br>正确的程序</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Please store the samples in the proper containers.  请把样本存放在合适的容器里。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 seemly</td><td>/ˈsiːmli/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>得体的，合宜的</td><td>seemly conduct in public<br>公共场合得体的举止</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The teacher expects seemly conduct from her students in public places.  老师期望学生在公共场所举止得体。</td></tr>
</tbody>
</table>

## Unit 10

<table>
<thead><tr>
<th>单词</th>
<th>音标</th>
<th>常用</th>
<th>口语</th>
<th>词性</th>
<th>中文释义</th>
<th>常用短语</th>
</tr></thead>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>rickety</b></td><td>/ˈrɪkəti/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>不稳的</td><td>a rickety wooden chair<br>摇摇晃晃的木椅</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 We climbed a rickety wooden staircase to reach the attic bedroom.  我们爬上摇摇晃晃的木楼梯，来到阁楼卧室。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 insecure</td><td>/ˌɪnsɪˈkjʊr/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>不牢固的，不稳固的；缺乏自信的</td><td>an insecure ladder<br>一把不牢固的梯子</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The shelf was so insecure that it collapsed under the books.  那个架子太不牢固，被书压塌了。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 precarious</td><td>/prɪˈkeriəs/</td><td>★★</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>危险的，不稳定的；可疑的，不明确的</td><td>a precarious position<br>不稳定的处境</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The climbers found themselves in a precarious position on the icy ledge.  攀登者发现自己处在冰封岩架上的危险境地。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 shaky</td><td>/ˈʃeɪki/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>不可靠的，不稳的</td><td>shaky reasoning<br>站不住脚的推理</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The theory rests on shaky historical evidence.  该理论建立在不可靠的历史证据上。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 unsound</td><td>/ənˈsaʊnd/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>不可靠的，有缺陷的</td><td>unsound reasoning<br>有缺陷的推理</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The engineer judged the old bridge structurally unsound.  工程师判定这座旧桥结构不牢。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 unsteady</td><td>/ʌnˈstedi/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>不稳的，摇晃的</td><td>unsteady hands<br>不稳的手</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Her voice was unsteady as she read the letter to us.  她给我们读那封信时声音颤抖不稳。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 weak</td><td>/ˈwiːk/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>脆弱的，虚弱的</td><td>a weak structure<br>脆弱的结构</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The bridge has a weak structure and cannot bear trucks.  这座桥结构脆弱，无法承载卡车。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 firm</td><td>/ˈfɜːrm/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>坚定的，坚固的</td><td>a firm decision<br>坚定的决定</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She remained firm despite enormous pressure from the board.  尽管董事会施加巨大压力，她依然坚定。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 sound</td><td>/saʊnd/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>牢固的；健康强壮的；（逻辑）严谨的</td><td>sound financial advice<br>稳妥的理财建议</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Her lawyer gave sound financial advice that saved the family thousands.  她的律师给出稳妥的理财建议，为家里省下数千元。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 stable</td><td>/ˈsteɪbəl/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>形容词、名词</td><td>稳定的；马厩</td><td>a stable income<br>稳定的收入</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 His condition has been stable since the operation last Tuesday.  自上周二手术以来他的病情一直稳定。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 sturdy</td><td>/ˈstɜːrdi/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>强健的，结实的</td><td>a sturdy wooden table<br>结实的木桌</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 A sturdy wooden table stood in the middle of the farmhouse kitchen.  农舍厨房中间摆着一张结实的木桌。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>restorative</b></td><td>/rɪˈstɔːrətɪv/</td><td>★☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>有益健康的</td><td>a restorative sleep<br>恢复元气的睡眠</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 A long walk by the sea proved wonderfully restorative after weeks of stress.  数周压力之后，在海边长时间散步格外有益身心。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 tonic</td><td>/ˈtɑːnɪk/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词、名词</td><td>滋补的，有益健康的</td><td>a tonic effect<br>滋补的功效</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 A week in the mountains had a tonic effect on her.  在山里待一周对她起了滋补强身的作用。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 curing</td><td>/ˈkjʊrɪŋ/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>有疗效的，治愈的</td><td>curing properties<br>治愈的功效</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Locals attribute curing powers to this bitter mountain herb.  当地人认为这种苦味山草有治病的功效。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 healing</td><td>/ˈhiːlɪŋ/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>形容词、名词</td><td>治愈的，有疗效的</td><td>healing power of time<br>时间的治愈力</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The doctor praised the healing effect of rest and fresh air.  医生称赞休息和新鲜空气的治愈效果。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 remedial</td><td>/rɪˈmiːdiəl/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>补救的，治疗的</td><td>take remedial action<br>采取补救措施</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The school offers remedial classes for students who fell behind.  学校为落后的学生开设补习班。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳反 sanative</del></td><td>/ˈsænətɪv/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>有疗效的，治疗的</td><td>sanative herbs and springs<br>有疗效的草药与泉水</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Villagers long believed the spring had sanative properties.  村民长期相信这泉水有疗效。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 wholesome</td><td>/ˈhoʊlsəm/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>有益身心健康的</td><td>wholesome home-cooked food<br>有益健康的家常饭菜</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The children grew strong on wholesome food and plenty of fresh air.  孩子们靠有益健康的食物和充足的新鲜空气长得很结实。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>ridicule</b></td><td>/ˈrɪdɪkjuːl/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>及物动词、名词</td><td>嘲笑；讥讽；愚弄</td><td>an object of ridicule<br>被嘲笑的对象</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Classmates began to ridicule him for his old shoes and worn coat.  同学们开始因他的旧鞋和破外套而嘲笑他。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 deride</td><td>/dɪˈraɪd/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>嘲弄，嘲笑</td><td>deride the proposal<br>嘲笑该提议</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Critics derided the proposal as naive and completely out of touch.  批评者嘲笑该提案幼稚且完全脱离现实。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 gibe</td><td>/dʒaɪb/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>动词</td><td>嘲弄</td><td>gibe at his accent<br>嘲弄他的口音</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The other students gibed at his accent until he stopped speaking.  其他学生嘲弄他的口音，直到他不再开口。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 jape</td><td>/dʒeɪp/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>名词、动词</td><td>嘲弄</td><td>a harmless jape<br>无恶意的玩笑</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He japed about his own baldness to put the guests at ease.  他拿自己的秃头开玩笑，让客人们放松下来。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 jeer</td><td>/ˈdʒɪr/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>动词、名词</td><td>嘲笑，讥讽</td><td>jeer at the loser<br>嘲笑失败者</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The crowd jeered at the player who missed the penalty.  人群嘲笑那名罚失点球的球员。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 lout</td><td>/ˈlaʊt/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>名词</td><td>粗鲁的蠢人</td><td>an ill-mannered lout<br>举止粗鲁的蠢人</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The crowd mocked the lout who shouted through the whole concert.  人群嘲笑那个整场演唱会大喊大叫的粗人。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 mock</td><td>/ˈmɑːk/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>形容词、动词</td><td>假的，模拟的；嘲笑</td><td>a mock trial<br>模拟审判</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Students held a mock election to learn how voting works.  学生举行模拟选举以了解投票如何运作。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 taunt</td><td>/tɔːnt/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>嘲弄，讥讽；挑衅</td><td>taunt the loser<br>嘲弄失败者</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The winning boys taunted the loser until a teacher intervened.  获胜的男孩们不停嘲弄输者，直到老师出面干预。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 commend</td><td>/kəˈmend/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>赞扬，表扬</td><td>commend her courage<br>赞扬她的勇气</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The principal commended her courage in front of the whole school.  校长在全校面前赞扬了她的勇气。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 praise</td><td>/ˈpreɪz/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>名词、动词</td><td>赞扬，称赞</td><td>win high praise<br>获得高度赞扬</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Her first novel won high praise from serious critics.  她的第一部小说赢得了严肃评论家的高度赞扬。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>rife</b></td><td>/raɪf/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>普遍的；充斥的；流行的</td><td>rife with rumors<br>充斥着传言</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The small town was rife with rumors about the mayor's resignation.  小镇上充斥着关于市长辞职的谣言。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 abounding</td><td>/əˈbaʊndɪŋ/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>形容词</td><td>充满的，大量的</td><td>abounding in fish<br>盛产鱼类</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 They found a river abounding in trout.  他们发现一条鳟鱼很多的河。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 abundant</td><td>/əˈbʌndənt/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>大量的，丰富的</td><td>abundant evidence<br>大量的证据</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Water was abundant after the spring rains.  春雨过后水源充足。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 awash</td><td>/əˈwɔːʃ/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>（如洪水般）泛滥的</td><td>awash with cash<br>现金泛滥</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The small town was awash with cash during the oil boom.  石油繁荣时期，这个小镇资金充裕。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 flush</td><td>/flʌʃ/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>形容词、不及物动词</td><td>充裕的，有钱的；冲洗</td><td>flush with money<br>钱很充裕</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 After the bonus he was flush with money for months.  拿到奖金后他有好几个月钱都很充裕。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 fraught</td><td>/frɔːt/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>充满（危险）的；忧虑的</td><td>fraught with danger<br>充满危险</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The mountain route is fraught with danger in early spring.  早春时节这条山路危机四伏。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 lousy</td><td>/ˈlaʊzi/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>多得很的，充满的；糟糕的</td><td>lousy with tourists<br>游客多得很</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 In August the town is lousy with tourists from every corner of Europe.  八月这座小镇挤满了来自欧洲各地的游客。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 replete</td><td>/rɪˈpliːt/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★</td><td>形容词</td><td>充满…的，富于…的</td><td>replete with errors<br>错误连篇</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 His essay was replete with spelling errors.  他的文章满是拼写错误。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 swarming</td><td>/ˈswɔːrmɪŋ/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>拥挤的，密集涌动的</td><td>swarming with tourists<br>挤满游客</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The market was swarming with shoppers on Saturday morning.  周六早上市场里挤满了购物者。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 teeming</td><td>/ˈtiːmɪŋ/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>大量的，充满的</td><td>teeming with life<br>充满生机</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The pond was teeming with tadpoles in May.  五月池塘里满是蝌蚪。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 thick</td><td>/ˈθɪk/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>充满的，密布的；厚的</td><td>thick with smoke<br>烟雾弥漫</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The air was thick with dust from the passing trucks.  空气里满是过路卡车扬起的尘土。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 thronging</td><td>/ˈθrɑːŋɪŋ/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>蜂拥而至的，拥挤的</td><td>thronging crowds<br>蜂拥的人群</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Thronging fans filled the stadium hours before the concert began.  蜂拥的粉丝在演唱会开始前几小时就挤满了体育场。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 deficient</td><td>/dɪˈfɪʃənt/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>不足的，缺乏的</td><td>deficient in vitamins<br>缺乏维生素</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The diet was deficient in iron and essential vitamins.  这种饮食缺乏铁和必需的维生素。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 incomplete</td><td>/ˌɪnkəmˈpliːt/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>不完全的，未完成的</td><td>an incomplete list<br>不完整的清单</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The survey remains incomplete because two districts never returned their forms.  调查仍不完整，因为两个区从未交回表格。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 insufficient</td><td>/ˌɪnsəˈfɪʃənt/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>不足的，不够的</td><td>insufficient evidence<br>证据不足</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The evidence was insufficient to convict him of the crime.  证据不足，无法判定他有罪。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 short</td><td>/ˈʃɔːrt/</td><td>★★★★☆</td><td>★★★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>短缺的，不足的</td><td>short of money<br>缺钱</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 We are short of money until the end of the month.  到月底之前我们都缺钱。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>rift</b></td><td>/rɪft/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>名词</td><td>分裂，不和</td><td>heal the rift<br>弥合裂痕</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 A deep rift opened between the two brothers after their father died.  父亲去世后，两兄弟之间出现了深深的裂痕。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 crack</td><td>/ˈkræk/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>动词、名词</td><td>破解，破译</td><td>crack the enemy's code<br>破解敌方密码</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Mathematicians finally cracked the enemy's code after months of work.  数学家们经过数月努力终于破解了敌方密码。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 fissure</td><td>/ˈfɪʃər/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>名词、动词</td><td>裂缝；分歧</td><td>a deep fissure in rock<br>岩石上的深裂缝</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 A narrow fissure ran along the face of the cliff.  一道狭窄的裂缝沿着崖壁延伸。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 fracture</td><td>/ˈfræktʃər/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>名词</td><td>破裂</td><td>a hairline fracture<br>一道细微裂缝</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The scan revealed a hairline fracture in his left wrist.  扫描显示他左手腕有一道细微裂缝。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 schism</td><td>/ˈskɪzəm/</td><td>★★</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>分裂，派系对立</td><td>a bitter schism<br>严重的分裂</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 A bitter schism split the party into two hostile factions.  一场激烈的分裂把该党撕成两个敌对派系。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 cleave</td><td>/kliːv/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>劈开，割裂；紧贴，坚持</td><td>cleave the log in two<br>把木头劈成两半</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 One blow of the axe cleaved the log in two.  斧头一击就把木头劈成了两半。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
