<tr><td>↳近 divide</td><td>/dɪˈvaɪd/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>动词、名词</td><td>分开，划分；分歧</td><td>divide into groups<br>分成小组</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The teacher divided the class into four groups of five.  老师把班级分成四个五人小组。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 rip</td><td>/ˈrɪp/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>动词、名词</td><td>撕开，猛地扯开；裂口</td><td>rip open the envelope<br>撕开信封</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He ripped the letter open before he even sat down.  他还没坐下就把信撕开了。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 split</td><td>/ˈsplɪt/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>动词、名词</td><td>分裂，分开</td><td>split into groups<br>分成小组</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The committee split into two camps over the proposal.  委员会因该提案分裂成两派。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 sunder</td><td>/ˈsʌndər/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>分开，割断</td><td>sunder the alliance<br>使联盟破裂</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The bitter dispute sundered an alliance that had lasted decades.  这场激烈的争端割断了维持数十年的联盟。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 tear</td><td>/ˈter/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>动词、名词</td><td>撕开，扯破；眼泪</td><td>tear the paper in half<br>把纸撕成两半</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She tore the photograph in half and threw it away.  她把照片撕成两半扔掉了。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 reconciliation</td><td>/ˌrekənsɪliˈeɪʃən/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>和解，调和</td><td>seek reconciliation with rivals<br>寻求与对手和解</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Years of quiet talks finally brought reconciliation between the two families.  多年的私下商谈最终让两家和解。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 rapprochement</td><td>/ˌræproʊʃˈmɑːn/</td><td>★☆</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>和解，恢复友好</td><td>a slow rapprochement<br>缓慢的和解</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Trade talks began a cautious rapprochement between the two capitals.  贸易谈判开启了两国首都间谨慎的和解。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>rile</b></td><td>/raɪl/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>刺激，惹怒</td><td>rile up the crowd<br>激怒人群</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Nothing riles him more than being interrupted in the middle of a sentence.  没有什么比话说到一半被打断更让他恼火。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 aggravate</td><td>/ˈæɡrəveɪt/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>加重，恶化；激怒</td><td>aggravate the situation<br>使局势恶化</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Running on the injury will only aggravate it.  带着伤跑步只会让伤势加重。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 annoy</td><td>/əˈnɔɪ/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>使恼怒，不断烦扰</td><td>annoy the neighbors<br>不断打扰邻居</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The barking dog began to annoy every neighbor on the quiet street.  那只不停犬吠的狗开始烦扰这条安静街上的每位邻居。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 enrage</td><td>/enˈreɪdʒ/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>使愤怒，激怒</td><td>enrage the audience<br>激怒观众</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 His flippant apology only enraged the families further.  他轻率的道歉只让那些家庭更加愤怒。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 exasperate</td><td>/ɪɡˈzæspəreɪt/</td><td>☆</td><td>★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>使恼怒，激怒</td><td>exasperate his parents<br>使他的父母恼火</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 His constant lateness exasperated even the most patient teachers.  他总是迟到，连最有耐心的老师都被激怒了。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 grate</td><td>/ɡreɪt/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>动词</td><td>刮擦（以发出刺耳的声音）；骚扰，惹恼</td><td>grate on my nerves<br>让我心烦</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Her constant humming began to grate on everyone in the small office.  她不停的哼唱开始让小办公室里的每个人心烦。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 inflame</td><td>/ɪnˈfleɪm/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>使加剧</td><td>inflame public anger<br>使公众愤怒加剧</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The mayor's careless comments only inflamed public anger over the housing shortage.  市长轻率的言论只是加剧了公众对住房短缺的愤怒。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 infuriate</td><td>/ɪnˈfjʊrieɪt/</td><td>★</td><td>★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>激怒</td><td>infuriate the critics<br>激怒批评者</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The decision to cut pensions infuriated thousands of workers across the country.  削减养老金的决定激怒了全国成千上万的工人。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 irritate</td><td>/ˈɪrɪteɪt/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>使恼怒；刺激</td><td>irritate the customers<br>使顾客恼火</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Loud advertisements before every video irritate most of our customers.  每个视频前的大声广告让我们大多数客户感到恼火。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 nettle</td><td>/ˈnetl/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>惹怒</td><td>nettled by criticism<br>被批评惹怒</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She was clearly nettled by the reporter's rude question.  记者无礼的提问显然惹恼了她。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 peeve</td><td>/piːv/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>打扰，惹恼</td><td>peeve the audience<br>惹恼观众</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Loud phone conversations on the train really peeve the other passengers.  火车上大声打电话确实惹恼其他乘客。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 pique</td><td>/piːk/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>激起（兴趣）；使生气</td><td>pique her curiosity<br>激起她的好奇心</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The strange title piqued her curiosity, so she borrowed the book.  奇怪的书名激起了她的好奇心，于是她借走了那本书。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 provoke</td><td>/prəˈvoʊk/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>激怒；驱使，激起</td><td>provoke an angry response<br>引发愤怒的回应</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 His careless remark provoked an angry response from the whole audience.  他不慎的言论激起了全体观众的愤怒回应。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 roil</td><td>/rɔɪl/</td><td>☆</td><td>★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>激怒</td><td>roil the crowd<br>激怒人群</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The careless remarks of the mayor roiled the crowd outside the courthouse.  市长轻率的言论激怒了法院外的人群。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 allay</td><td>/əˈleɪ/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>减轻，平息</td><td>allay public fears<br>平息公众恐慌</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The statement was meant to allay concerns about safety.  这份声明意在减轻人们对安全的担忧。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 alleviate</td><td>/əˈliːvieɪt/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★</td><td>动词</td><td>缓和，减轻</td><td>alleviate the pain<br>减轻疼痛</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 A warm bath helped alleviate the soreness in his back.  热水澡帮他缓解了背部的酸痛。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 assuage</td><td>/əˈsweɪdʒ/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>缓和，减轻；平息</td><td>assuage his fears<br>减轻他的恐惧</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The mayor's speech did little to assuage public anger.  市长的演讲几乎没能平息公众的怒火。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 ease</td><td>/ˈiːz/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★★</td><td>动词、名词</td><td>减轻，缓和；容易</td><td>ease the pain<br>减轻疼痛</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The medicine eased her headache within minutes of the first dose.  这药在服下第一剂几分钟内就缓解了她的头痛。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 mitigate</td><td>/ˈmɪtɪɡeɪt/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>减轻，使缓和</td><td>mitigate the damage<br>减轻损害</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Planting trees can mitigate the effects of summer heat.  种树可以减轻夏季高温的影响。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 mollify</td><td>/ˈmɑːlɪfaɪ/</td><td>☆</td><td>★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>平息，抚慰，缓和</td><td>mollify an angry customer<br>安抚愤怒的顾客</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 A sincere apology from the manager mollified the furious client.  经理诚挚的道歉安抚了这位愤怒的客户。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳反 palliate</del></td><td>/ˈpælieɪt/</td><td>☆</td><td>★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>减轻，缓和；掩饰</td><td>palliate the symptoms<br>缓解症状</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The new drug can only palliate the pain, not cure the disease.  这种新药只能减轻疼痛，无法治愈疾病。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 relieve</td><td>/rɪˈliːv/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>减轻，缓解；换班替换</td><td>relieve the pressure<br>缓解压力</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 A short walk relieved the tension in her shoulders.  短暂散步缓解了她肩膀的紧张。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 soothe</td><td>/suːð/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>安慰，抚慰；减轻</td><td>soothe a crying baby<br>安抚哭闹的婴儿</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Soft music helped soothe the restless patient through the long night.  轻柔的音乐帮助安抚了焦躁的病人度过漫长的夜晚。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>ripen</b></td><td>/ˈraɪpən/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>动词</td><td>使成熟，变成熟</td><td>ripen in the sun<br>在阳光下成熟</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The tomatoes will ripen quickly if you leave them on the sunny windowsill.  把西红柿放在向阳的窗台上会很快成熟。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 age</td><td>/ˈeɪdʒ/</td><td>★★★★☆</td><td>★★★★☆</td><td>动词、名词</td><td>变陈；使变老；年龄</td><td>age the wine in oak<br>在橡木桶中陈酿</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The cheese must age for two years before it is sold.  这种奶酪必须陈化两年才能出售。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 develop</td><td>/dɪˈveləp/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>动词</td><td>发展，培养</td><td>develop a skill<br>培养一项技能</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She developed a habit of reading for an hour each night.  她养成了每晚阅读一小时的习惯。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 grow</td><td>/ˈɡroʊ/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>动词</td><td>种植，生长；成长</td><td>grow vegetables<br>种蔬菜</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 They grow tomatoes and beans on a small rooftop garden.  他们在小小的屋顶花园里种番茄和豆子。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 mature</td><td>/məˈtʃʊr/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>形容词、动词</td><td>成熟的；（使）成熟</td><td>a mature judgment<br>成熟的判断</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He seems remarkably mature for a boy of fifteen.  对一个十五岁男孩来说他显得格外成熟。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 fade</td><td>/ˈfeɪd/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>不及物动词</td><td>衰退，消退；褪色</td><td>hopes began to fade<br>希望开始消退</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Their hopes of rescue began to fade after three days.  三天后他们获救的希望开始消退。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 flag</td><td>/flæɡ/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>不及物动词</td><td>变得衰弱</td><td>his energy began to flag<br>他的精力开始衰减</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 His energy began to flag after the twentieth mile.  跑到第二十英里后他的精力开始衰减。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 shrivel</td><td>/ˈʃrɪvəl/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>动词</td><td>皱缩，枯萎，干瘪</td><td>shrivel in the sun<br>在阳光下皱缩</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The unwatered seedlings shriveled within two days of intense heat.  没浇水的幼苗在两天酷热中就干瘪了。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 wane</td><td>/weɪn/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★★</td><td>不及物动词、名词</td><td>减少，衰退</td><td>wax and wane<br>盈亏消长，兴衰</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Public interest in the scandal waned within a month.  公众对这桩丑闻的兴趣一个月内就减退了。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 wilt</td><td>/wɪlt/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>不及物动词</td><td>精神萎靡，憔悴</td><td>wilt in the heat<br>在酷热中萎靡</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The runners began to wilt in the heat of the final mile.  跑到最后一英里的酷热中，选手们开始萎靡。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 wither</td><td>/ˈwɪðər/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>不及物动词</td><td>枯萎，凋谢</td><td>wither in the heat<br>在高温中枯萎</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The tomato plants withered after a week without rain or shade.  一周无雨又没有遮荫，番茄苗都枯萎了。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>riot</b></td><td>/ˈraɪət/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>名词</td><td>喧闹，暴乱</td><td>a riot broke out<br>爆发了骚乱</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 A riot broke out downtown soon after the verdict was announced.  判决宣布后不久，市中心爆发了骚乱。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 clamor</td><td>/ˈklæmər/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>名词</td><td>喧闹，喧哗，噪声</td><td>a clamor of voices<br>一片人声喧哗</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 A clamor of voices rose from the crowded courtyard.  拥挤的院子里传出一片人声喧哗。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 commotion</td><td>/kəˈmoʊʃn/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>名词</td><td>骚乱</td><td>cause a commotion<br>引起一阵骚动</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The escaped goat caused quite a commotion in the market.  逃跑的山羊在市场上引起一阵不小的骚动。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 disorder</td><td>/dɪˈsɔːrdər/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>名词、及物动词</td><td>混乱，无序；失调，疾病</td><td>public disorder<br>公共秩序混乱</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The room was left in complete disorder after the party.  聚会之后房间被弄得一片混乱。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 ferment</td><td>/ˈfɜːrment/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>名词、动词</td><td>动乱，骚动；发酵</td><td>in a state of ferment<br>处于动荡之中</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The whole country was in a state of ferment before the election.  大选前全国处于动荡之中。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 tumult</td><td>/ˈtuːmʌlt/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>名词</td><td>骚动，暴动</td><td>the tumult of the crowd<br>人群的骚动</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The tumult in the square grew louder as more protesters arrived.  随着更多抗议者到来，广场上的骚动越来越大。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 turmoil</td><td>/ˈtɜːrmɔɪl/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★★</td><td>名词</td><td>混乱，动荡，骚动</td><td>in political turmoil<br>处于政治动荡中</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The country remained in political turmoil for nearly a decade.  这个国家在近十年里一直处于政治动荡之中。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 upheaval</td><td>/əpˈhiːvəl/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>名词</td><td>剧变，动乱</td><td>social upheaval<br>社会剧变</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The war brought decades of social upheaval to the region.  战争给这一地区带来数十年的社会剧变。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 uproar</td><td>/ˈʌˌprɔːr/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>名词</td><td>喧嚣，骚动</td><td>cause an uproar<br>引起轩然大波</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The new tax plan caused an uproar among small business owners.  新税收方案在小企业主中引起轩然大波。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 serenity</td><td>/səˈrenəti/</td><td>★★</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>宁静，安详</td><td>a sense of serenity<br>一种宁静感</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The garden offers a rare serenity in the middle of the city.  这座花园在城市中心提供了难得的宁静。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 tranquility</td><td>/træŋˈkwɪləti/</td><td>★★</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>宁静，淡定</td><td>an air of tranquility<br>宁静的气息</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He enjoyed the tranquility of the snowy fields at dusk.  他享受黄昏时白雪覆盖的田野的静谧。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>rite</b></td><td>/raɪt/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>名词</td><td>惯例，仪式</td><td>a sacred burial rite<br>神圣的葬礼仪式</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The village still performs an ancient rite to mark the end of winter.  村里仍会举行一种古老的仪式来标志冬天结束。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 ceremony</td><td>/ˈserəˌmoʊni/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>名词</td><td>典礼，仪式</td><td>hold an opening ceremony<br>举行开幕典礼</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The graduation ceremony lasted nearly three hours in the hot sun.  毕业典礼在烈日下持续了近三小时。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 form</td><td>/ˈfɔːrm/</td><td>★★★★☆</td><td>★★★★☆</td><td>动词、名词</td><td>构成，形成；形式</td><td>form a committee<br>组成一个委员会</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Six neighbors formed a committee to repair the shared path.  六位邻居组成委员会修理共用的小路。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 observance</td><td>/əbˈzɜːrvəns/</td><td>★★</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>遵守；（仪式的）举行</td><td>strict observance of tradition<br>严格遵守传统</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The village is known for its faithful observance of ancient customs.  这个村庄以忠实遵守古老习俗著称。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 protocol</td><td>/ˈproʊtəkɔːl/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>名词</td><td>正确的礼仪规范</td><td>diplomatic protocol<br>外交礼仪规范</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Diplomatic protocol requires that the older ambassador be greeted first.  外交礼仪规范要求先向资历较长的大使致意。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 ritual</td><td>/ˈrɪtʃuəl/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>名词、形容词</td><td>仪式，例行程序</td><td>a morning ritual<br>晨间例行仪式</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Brewing coffee slowly is his cherished morning ritual.  慢慢煮咖啡是他珍视的晨间仪式。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 solemnity</td><td>/səˈlemnəti/</td><td>★☆</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>庄严，严肃</td><td>with due solemnity<br>以应有的庄严</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The coronation was conducted with all the solemnity the occasion demanded.  加冕礼以这一场合所要求的全部庄严举行。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>rive</b></td><td>/raɪv/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>动词</td><td>撕开</td><td>rive the log apart<br>把木头劈开</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Lightning can rive a tall oak straight down the middle of its trunk.  闪电能把高大的橡树从树干正中劈开。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 cleave</td><td>/kliːv/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>劈开，割裂；紧贴，坚持</td><td>cleave the log in two<br>把木头劈成两半</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 One blow of the axe cleaved the log in two.  斧头一击就把木头劈成了两半。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 lacerate</td><td>/ˈlæsəreɪt/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>使非常痛苦</td><td>lacerate someone's feelings<br>深深伤害某人感情</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 His careless remarks lacerated her feelings more deeply than he realized.  他随口的话比他意识到的更深地伤害了她的感情。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 rend</td><td>/rend/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>撕裂，猛拉</td><td>rend the cloth in two<br>把布撕成两半</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The angry protester tried to rend the banner into two ragged halves.  那名愤怒的抗议者试图把横幅撕成破烂的两半。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 ribbon</td><td>/ˈrɪbən/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>名词、动词</td><td>撕成条；带子，丝带</td><td>torn to ribbons<br>被撕成碎条</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The flag hung in ribbons after weeks of storms.  经过数周风暴，旗子已被撕成条条挂着。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 rip</td><td>/ˈrɪp/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>动词、名词</td><td>撕开，猛地扯开；裂口</td><td>rip open the envelope<br>撕开信封</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He ripped the letter open before he even sat down.  他还没坐下就把信撕开了。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 rupture</td><td>/ˈrʌptʃər/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>动词</td><td>打破；打碎，破裂</td><td>rupture a blood vessel<br>使血管破裂</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The old pipe could rupture at any moment under such high pressure.  这根旧管道随时可能在如此高的压力下破裂。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 shred</td><td>/ʃred/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>名词</td><td>碎片，细条；少量</td><td>not a shred of evidence<br>没有一丝证据</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 There is not a shred of evidence linking him to the theft.  没有一丝证据能把他和这起盗窃联系起来。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 split</td><td>/ˈsplɪt/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>动词、名词</td><td>分裂，分开</td><td>split into groups<br>分成小组</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The committee split into two camps over the proposal.  委员会因该提案分裂成两派。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳近 tatter</del></td><td>/ˈtætər/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>名词、动词</td><td>破布条，碎片；撕碎</td><td>in tatters<br>破烂不堪；彻底破灭</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 His reputation lay in tatters after the investigation.  调查之后他的名誉已经一败涂地。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 tear</td><td>/ˈter/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>动词、名词</td><td>撕开，扯破；眼泪</td><td>tear the paper in half<br>把纸撕成两半</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She tore the photograph in half and threw it away.  她把照片撕成两半扔掉了。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 associate</td><td>/əˈsoʊʃieɪt/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★</td><td>动词</td><td>联合，结交；联想</td><td>associate with the group<br>与该团体结交</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He refused to associate himself with such a risky plan.  他拒绝与这样冒险的计划有牵连。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 coalesce</td><td>/ˌkoʊəˈles/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★</td><td>动词</td><td>合并，融合</td><td>coalesce into a group<br>合并成一个团体</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The scattered protests coalesced into a national movement.  分散的抗议汇合成一场全国性运动。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 combine</td><td>/kəmˈbaɪn/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>动词</td><td>结合，联合</td><td>combine two elements<br>结合两种元素</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The recipe combines sweet and sour flavors very successfully.  这道菜谱把甜味和酸味结合得很成功。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 conjoin</td><td>/kənˈdʒɔɪn/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>动词</td><td>结合，连接</td><td>conjoin the two parts<br>把两部分接合</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The bridge conjoins the island and the mainland neatly.  这座桥把小岛与大陆巧妙相连。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 conjugate</td><td>/ˈkɑːndʒəɡeɪt/</td><td>★☆</td><td>☆</td><td>动词、形容词</td><td>结合，配对；成对的</td><td>conjugate two molecules<br>使两个分子结合</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Researchers conjugate the drug to an antibody for precise delivery.  研究者把药物与抗体结合以实现精准投递。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 connect</td><td>/kəˈnekt/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>动词</td><td>连接，联结</td><td>connect the two wires<br>连接两根导线</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 A narrow tunnel connects the two halves of the building.  一条窄隧道把大楼的两半连起来。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 fuse</td><td>/ˈfjuːz/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★★</td><td>动词</td><td>融合，熔合</td><td>fuse two traditions<br>融合两种传统</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Her music fuses jazz rhythms with traditional folk melodies.  她的音乐把爵士节奏与传统民谣旋律融为一体。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳反 interfuse</del></td><td>/ˌɪntərˈfjuz/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>动词</td><td>使混合，交融</td><td>interfuse the two styles<br>交融两种风格</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The composer interfuses folk melodies with modern harmony.  作曲家把民间旋律与现代和声交融在一起。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 join</td><td>/ˈdʒɔɪn/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>动词</td><td>连接，接合；加入</td><td>join the two pieces<br>把两块接起来</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Join the ends of the rope with a firm knot.  用牢固的结把绳子两端接起来。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 link</td><td>/ˈlɪŋk/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>名词、动词</td><td>联系，纽带；连接</td><td>a close link<br>紧密的联系</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Researchers found a close link between sleep and memory.  研究者发现睡眠与记忆之间存在紧密联系。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 unify</td><td>/ˈjuːnəˌfaɪ/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>统一，使成一体</td><td>unify the country<br>统一国家</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 A common language helped unify the scattered tribes into one nation.  共同的语言帮助把分散的部落统一为一个民族。</td></tr>
</tbody>
</table>
